# 第 3 章　明霄竞武试锋芒•上

*2.4 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Finest Duel Under the Pristine Blue (I)　·　明霄竞武试锋芒•上

## 013　Homecoming Wardance　故客重游，演武天舟

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色：Trailblazer; Pom-Pom; Himeko; Welt; Dan Heng; March 7th; Jing Yuan; Yanqing; Yunli; Mok Tok　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Obsequies Performed, A Long Road Ahead'' completed

### Receive comms from the Xianzhou

> *Before embarking on the next stop of their journey, the Express received a comms from the Xianzhou Luofu — Jing Yuan extended an invite to all wanderers to join the Wardance.*

*(Upon teleporting to the Parlor Car)*

**System:** You have an interstellar message. Please check it promptly.

*(Investigate the screen)*

**System:** You have an interstellar message. Please check it promptly.

**Pom-Pom:** Hmm? Whew...

> **帕姆：**喔？唔……

**Himeko:** What's up, Pom-Pom?

> **姬子：**怎么了，帕姆？

**Pom-Pom:** Just got a message from the Xianzhou Luofu. Looks like it'll conflict with our original schedule.

> **帕姆：**列车刚刚收到了一条从罗浮仙舟发来的消息，从摘要来看，似乎会和接下来的行程计划有冲突。

**Jing Yuan:** It's been a while, my friends on the Astral Express. How's your trailblazing expedition going?

> **景元：**许久不见了，星穹列车的朋友们，不知各位的开拓之旅是否顺利？

**Jing Yuan:** Soon, the Xianzhou Luofu will be holding the Luminary Wardance. Those who have aided the Luofu in overcoming the crisis are cherished allies of the Xianzhou, thus, on behalf of the Seat of Divine Foresight, I'm extending an invitation to attend the ceremony. Your presence would be greatly appreciated.

> **景元：**近日罗浮仙舟即将举行庆礼「星天演武」仪典。诸位曾帮助罗浮弭平灾厄，是仙舟联盟的好朋友。在此景元代表神策府诚邀诸位莅临观礼，请诸位务必拨冗赏光。

**Himeko:** Well, things are getting lively. We've barely recovered from The Family's Charmony Festival, and we're already being invited to another special event.

> **姬子：**还真是热闹啊，才从家族的「谐乐大典」中抽身，这么快就有了新的邀请。

- ▸ Trailblazing is all about having fun.

> ▸ 开拓，就是快乐。

**March 7th:** Why not think about it this way. Our trailblazing expeditions are turning into "blast expeditions," where we eat, drink, and play wherever we go.

> **三月七：**这么一想，咱们「开拓之旅」就要变成了到处吃吃喝喝玩玩的「快落之旅」。

**Pom-Pom:** Blast... expedition?

> **帕姆：**快…落？

**March 7th:** Yeah, wherever we go, we eat, play, and have a blast!

> **三月七：**是啊，吃吃喝喝玩玩，一路快速堕落的「快落」。

- ▸ I'm still recovering from the trauma caused by the last "festivity."

> ▸ 对「盛会」都有心理创伤了。

**March 7th:** Yeah, me too!

> **三月七：**咱也一样！

**March 7th:** But wait, won't it look unprofessional if we attend two ceremonies in a row? Aren't we supposed to be focused on restoring astral charts and doing some serious scientific exploration?

> **三月七：**还有还有，连赶两场「盛会」活动，是不是显得有些不务正业了？列车的正事该是恢复星图和航路数据，顺带做些严肃的科考工作吧？

**Dan Heng:** So, we should leave March behind to take care of the Express?

> **丹恒：**那就让三月留下维护列车？

**March 7th:** H—Hey! That's not what I meant! I'm all for some fun... I just hope there won't be any surprise party crashers like Friday or Saturday.

> **三月七：**我、我可没说不想去。我是很喜欢凑热闹的啦…只是希望下一个凑热闹的地方不要再冒出个什么星期五、星期六来跟我们作对。

**Welt:** The Xianzhou Luofu has recently overcome a crisis. By holding the Wardance, they're demonstrating to everyone that they've returned to a state of peace and safety.

> **瓦尔特：**罗浮仙舟才度过了危机，举办演武仪典也是在对外彰显自己和平安全的状态。

**March 7th:** But that's what everyone said before we went to Penacony! "You'll be totally safe under The Family's protection!"

> **三月七：**去匹诺康尼之前大家也是这么说的！「在『家族』的保护下不会有任何危险～」

**Dan Heng:** No need to worry. The Wardance is not like the Charmony Festival with all its hidden secrets. It's just a festival to honor the Reignbow Arbiter and the Cloud Knights who fought against the Abominations of Abundance and protected the Xianzhou ships.

> **丹恒：**倒也不必杯弓蛇影。演武仪典不似「谐乐大典」那样隐藏了诸多秘密。它只是为纪念帝弓与云骑对抗丰饶孽物，拯救仙舟的壮举而设的节庆罢了。

**Dan Heng:** Aside from starskiff performances, it's mostly martial contests, nothing too different from the Taikiyan Roboball contest we've seen before.

> **丹恒：**典礼前后除去星槎巡航的演出外，不过是些比武斗剑的竞赛。和我们去看过的泰科铵机动球没什么区别。

**Welt:** What do you think, Himeko? Since we've accepted Miss Black Swan's proposal, we should probably head to Amphoreus for refueling.

> **瓦尔特：**姬子，你觉得如何？按照列车目前的状况，我们接受了黑天鹅女士的提议，应该安排一次去翁法罗斯的「开拓之旅」，补充燃料。

**Himeko:** Mm, there's certainly no rush. This trailblazing expedition is quite unique, and the Express needs to be fully stocked and prepared before moving on to the next stop.

> **姬子：**时间倒也不算紧迫。这次「开拓之旅」情况特殊，在去往下一站前，列车也应做好补给、支援方面的准备。

**Himeko:** With Madam Herta's help, I was planning to deliver some Leviathan fossils from Calenga Abyss to Ruan Mei, member #81 of the Genius Society. It could earn us some favors before we set off. However, it may take a few weeks.

> **姬子：**正巧有黑塔女士牵线，我本打算在启程前为天才俱乐部#81阮•梅女士从「金伦加深域」带回一具古兽遗骸，做些人情交换。不过这么一算，至少需要几周时间才能返航。

**March 7th:** Ah, so that means... we're not going to the Luofu.

> **三月七：**喔，那就是…去不成罗浮啦。

**Himeko:** Being an adult means maintaining relationships, whether we like it or not, March. Since we've been invited, it's only right for the Astral Express to attend the ceremony.

> **姬子：**小三月，维系关系，这就是成年人生活所必须支付的小小代价。既然罗浮仙舟发出了邀请，那么作为朋友，星穹列车也理当赴约。

**Himeko:** So, here's the plan: Pom-Pom will take everyone to the Xianzhou Luofu. Mr. Yang and I will meet up with Ruan Mei and fulfill our promise. Meanwhile, (Trailblazer), March, and Dan Heng will represent the Express and attend the Wardance.

> **姬子：**我打算这么安排：帕姆会将所有人先送往罗浮。之后，我和瓦尔特先生则继续前往完成与阮•梅的约定。开拓者、三月和丹恒三人则可以作为列车观礼的代表出席仪典。

**Welt:** What do you two think, (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng?

> **瓦尔特：**开拓者，丹恒，没问题吧？

- ▸ Sounds great. Let's split into groups!

> ▸ 那分组就这么定了！

**Welt:** I don't hear any objections.

> **瓦尔特：**看起来大家都没什么异议。

- ▸ I want to play with Leviathan fossils, too!

> ▸ 我要玩古兽化石！

**March 7th:** Himeko and Mr. Yang will be busy with serious research. Besides, fossils can't compete with martial contests when it comes to fun, right?

> **三月七：**姬子姐和杨叔可是去正经搞科研呢！古兽化石哪有看演武斗剑好玩？

**March 7th:** Plus, you're the only one with so many friends on the Luofu. I'll be lost if you don't come along! You've got to lead the way.

> **三月七：**再说了，就属你在罗浮朋友多，你不去我都不知道该去哪玩！你得带路啊。

**Pom-Pom:** Now that everyone's on board with the plan, it's time to warp to the Xianzhou Luofu.

> **帕姆：**既然各位乘客已经达成一致了，那我们就准备跃迁前往仙舟「罗浮」了帕。

### Discuss travel plans with March 7th and Dan Heng

### (Optional) Discuss the travel plan with the other members of the Express Crew

*(Talk to Welt Yang, optional)*

**Welt:** Calenga Abyss... what does she expect to find there? Current research on Leviathan merely proves how little we know about such life forms...

**Himeko:** That's why geniuses are interested in that field. Science is all about uncovering the unknown...

The conversation between the two adults brims with complex jargon, from "Assumptive Astrobiological Chemistry" to "Trans-dimensional Evolutionary Theory," filling the carriage with an air of dense perplexity.

- ▸ Have a safe trip, you two.
- ▸ Come back soon.

**Himeko:** Don't miss us too much, (Trailblazer). If I stumble upon some cool Leviathan fossils, I'll bring a few back as souvenirs for you.

*(Talk to March 7th and Dan Heng)*

**March 7th:** Himeko really knows how to convince people. Hehe, between Leviathan fossils and the Wardance, the latter definitely sounds more fun.

> **三月七：**姬子姐真是懂人心啊。嘻嘻，在古兽化石和演武仪典之间，还是后者听起来比较有意思。

**March 7th:** By the way, Dan Heng... this time, you'll be taking a stroll with us on the Luofu, right?

> **三月七：**对了…丹恒，这次去罗浮，你会跟我们一块儿下车走走的吧？

**Dan Heng:** Just let you and (Trailblazer) wander around aimlessly on the Luofu by yourselves? I don't think so.

> **丹恒：**放你和开拓者两个在罗浮上乱逛？我可不放心。

**Dan Heng:** Plus, Mr. Yang is right. The Ambrosial Arbor Crisis just ended, and both the long-life and short-life species are still feeling uneasy. And that's why Jing Yuan wants to organize the Wardance, to show that the Xianzhou Luofu is stable and safe.

> **丹恒：**而且瓦尔特先生说的没错，建木灾异刚刚平息，无论是长生种还是短生种都对灾难心有余悸。景元选在此时举办演武仪典，显然是为了对外彰显罗浮的安定。

**Dan Heng:** Since he has extended an invitation, it's only right that I visit my old friend.

> **丹恒：**既然他发出邀请，于情于理，我都应该去看看老朋友。

**March 7th:** Coming back to this place brings back so many memories, you know...

> **三月七：**故地重游，本姑娘内心真是颇有一番感慨……

- ▸ So, here's something for all of you, a poem by March 7th...

**March 7th:** Hey, I'm not actually gonna recite a poem! I was just thinking about all the twists and turns we went through when we first arrived on the Xianzhou...

> **三月七：**也没有到要为此情此景吟诗的地步啦！只是想到上一回抵达仙舟时的波折和惊悚……

- ▸ What memories? Care to spill the details?

**March 7th:** Well, it's nothing too poetic. I was just thinking about all the twists and turns we went through when we first arrived at the Xianzhou ship...

> **三月七：**也不是什么了不得的诗情画意啦，只是想到上一回抵达仙舟时的波折和惊悚……

**March 7th:** This time, we're not being forced or enticed, or chasing after wanted criminals. And we didn't have to sneak in through the cargo dock. This trip has been incredibly smooth...

> **三月七：**这一回咱们既没有被人半胁迫半诱骗，也不是为了追捕什么通缉犯，更不是从卸货码头登陆，如此一帆风顺……

**March 7th:** Quite unusual, I must say!

> **三月七：**真是难得啊！

- ▸ Definitely unusual!

**Dan Heng:** I agree.

> **丹恒：**难得啊。

- ▸ We're so easily pleased. I actually feel sorry for us...

**March 7th:** Such is the fate of us Nameless, I suppose.

> **三月七：**没办法，这就是无名客的宿命吧。

- ▸ Oh, come on, don't jinx us...

**March 7th:** Haha, I'm just hoping for the best! You're the one jinxing us!

> **三月七：**我这是美好的祝愿，你这才叫乌鸦嘴吧！

【游戏内短信 · (Trailblazer)! I saw the...】

**Yanqing:** (Trailblazer)! I saw the Sky-Faring Commission's notice that the Express has docked on the Xianzhou

> **彦卿：**开拓者，我看到天舶司发来了通知，列车已经完成了接驳

**Yanqing:** Are you guys on the Luofu already?

> **彦卿：**所以，你们抵达罗浮了吗？

- ▸ Just got to Starskiff Haven!

**(Trailblazer):** Yup, fresh off the train. We're now at Starskiff Haven

- ▸ Quick with the news, eh?

**(Trailblazer):** I see you're quick with the news!

**Yanqing:** Heh, the general sent me to welcome you all at Starskiff Haven. See you in a bit!

> **彦卿：**将军安排我在星槎海迎接各位，一会儿见面聊

### Look for Yanqing in the crowd

> *Arriving at the Luofu, you are greeted by an overwhelming sight of starskiffs.*

**March 7th:** Is the Starskiff Haven always this crowded? There are so many people here! I can barely hear anything!

> **三月七：**星槎海有这么热闹吗？今天人真是格外多啊！都快听不清彼此说话了！

**(Trailblazer):** Yanqing said General Jing Yuan sent him to welcome us, but where is he?

> **开拓者：**彦卿说，将军派他来接咱们，可他人在哪儿呢？

**Dan Heng:** Let's wait for him in front of the loom. It's the most prominent landmark on the dock.

> **丹恒：**我们去织机前面等他吧。那是码头上最显眼的地标。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Pepeshi Tourist:** Hey, you guys! Hold on a moment!

> **皮皮西游客：**三位，三位，请留步！

**March 7th:** Did they just call us? Look at their outfits... they're from Penacony, right?

> **三月七：**那两个人是不是在叫我们啊？看打扮…像是匹诺康尼人？

**Pepeshi Tourist:** Are you familiar with the Xianzhou Luofu?

> **皮皮西游客：**不知你们几位是不是熟悉罗浮仙舟的情况？

**Dan Heng:** We know a little bit about it. What do you need?

> **丹恒：**略知一二。请问有什么事吗？

**Intellitron Tourist:** We're from Penacony, maybe you've heard of it? We came to this ship to gather interesting materials for making dream bubbles.

> **智械游客：**我们俩来自匹诺康尼，也许你听说过这个地方？这次来到仙舟，是为了采集一些有趣的梦境素材。

**March 7th:** We just left there. Talk about coincidence.

> **三月七：**巧了，我们刚从那儿离开。

**Intellitron Tourist:** That's great! Do you know any must-see attractions on the Luofu?

> **智械游客：**那就太好了，你们知道在这「罗浮」之上，有什么值得一去的景点吗？

- ▸ You've come to the right people!

> ▸ 那你可问对人了！

**March 7th:** Exactly! We're Xianzhou experts!

> **三月七：**那是！我们仨可是仙舟通！

- ▸ Why don't you ask the locals?

> ▸ 这应该问本地人吧？

**Pepeshi Tourist:** We did! But the locals were all like, "Come on, there's nothing worth seeing on the Luofu! It's so boring here!"

> **皮皮西游客：**我们问了啊！但本地人都回答说：「罗浮的景点有啥好去的，都没意思！」

**Intellitron Tourist:** Can't blame them. If someone asked me, "What's there to see on Penacony?" I'd probably struggle to answer too.

> **智械游客：**也可以理解。如果有人问我：「匹诺康尼都有什么值得去的景点呢？」我恐怕也会一时语塞。

**Pepeshi Tourist:** That's why we're asking fellow tourists!

> **皮皮西游客：**所以这种事还是要问其他游客啦！

**March 7th:** Most of the tourists around at the moment are here to attend the Wardance, and that's why we're here too.

> **三月七：**这段时间来的游客，多半会去参观演武仪典。我们几个就是为此而来的啊。

**Pepeshi Tourist:** Yeah, we know about that ceremony, but isn't the fighting ring still closed?

> **皮皮西游客：**我当然知道演武仪典的。但这仪典的擂台不是还没开放吗？

**Intellitron Tourist:** I've heard the ring was actually converted from a huge decommissioned Luofu fighter jet.

> **智械游客：**我听说这一次的演武擂台，是拿罗浮退役的巨型斗舰改装而成的。

- ▸ So, it's even larger than a regular starskiff?

> ▸ 比一般的星槎还大？

**Intellitron Tourist:** No doubt about it! It's a massive fighter jet! It's got to be larger than a civilian starskiff!

> **智械游客：**那还用说！这可是巨型斗舰，肯定比民用的星槎大多了！

- ▸ So, it's even bigger than the Radiant Feldspar?

> ▸ 比「晖长石号」还大？

**Intellitron Tourist:** The Radiant Feldspar is bigger, I guess!

> **智械游客：**那多半是「晖长石号」更大吧！

**Pepeshi Tourist:** But for now, all we can do is wait until the Wardance starts in a few weeks before we can board it. We've still got work to do, so we can't just sit around waiting for it to start, that's why we're asking you about some must-see attractions.

> **皮皮西游客：**不过咱现在只能等到几周后演武仪典开幕，才有机会登舰了。在此之前，我们俩也不能耽误工作——所以才问你们，有什么比较值得一去的景点？

**Intellitron Tourist:** We're looking for unique experiences that you won't find on Penacony. Our clients love these kinds of dream bubbles the most.

> **智械游客：**我们希望能找到那种，在匹诺康尼的日常梦境中难得一见的特殊体验。我们的客户最喜欢这种梦泡了。

**March 7th:** You're the expert here, (Trailblazer). Give them some suggestions!

> **三月七：**开拓者，这里你最熟，快给他们出出主意吧～

- ▸ Why not visit Aurum Alley?

> ▸ 去金人巷吧。

**Dan Heng:** Hmm, good idea. The everyday vibe of the Aurum Alley is a rare find on the Planet of Festivities.

> **丹恒：**这是个不错的主意。金人巷的人间烟火气，在「盛会之星」上难得一见。

**Pepeshi Tourist:** Everyday vibe... Yeah, our clients have had their fair share of adrenaline-pumping Dreamscapes. Maybe something down-to-earth would touch their hearts.

> **皮皮西游客：**人间烟火气啊…确实，现在我们这些客户啊，尝遍了惊险刺激的梦境。也许反而是这类接地气的内容能让他们找回感动。

**Intellitron Tourist:** Thanks for the suggestion. We'll head to the Aurum Alley to gather materials. Maybe we'll run into each other there in a couple of days.

> **智械游客：**感谢三位的指点，我们这就去金人巷采风。也许过两天，我们会在那里再度相遇。

- ▸ Why not visit Fyxestroll Garden?

> ▸ 去绥园吧。

**March 7th:** Oh, yeah! Fyxestroll Garden! It's famous for supernatural stuff, with those mischievous heliobi floating around! It's pretty fascinating!

> **三月七：**对对对，去绥园！那里可是罗浮著名的灵异圣地，到处都飘着些调皮的小鬼火，可有意思了！

**Intellitron Tourist:** Supernatural? I don't really like scary things... but our clients will love it.

> **智械游客：**灵异？我不太喜欢恐怖题材…不过，我们的客户一定会喜欢。

**Pepeshi Tourist:** It does sound interesting. Let's go check out Fyxestroll Garden! Maybe we'll bump into each other there!

> **皮皮西游客：**听起来确实有点意思。那我们就去你们说的「绥园」啦～之后咱也许能在绥园再见！

- ▸ Why not visit Scalegorge Waterscape?

> ▸ 去鳞渊境吧。

**Dan Heng:** That's indeed a good idea. While the Dreamscape on Penacony is all artificial, Scalegorge Waterscape is a celebration of nature's resurgence. It has some remarkable scenery.

> **丹恒：**这倒是个好主意。梦境中尽是些恒常不变的人造景观，但这鳞渊境却是「自然」侵蚀「人造」而缔造的独特风景。

**Intellitron Tourist:** Awesome. I love being out there in nature. Let's go to Scalegorge Waterscape first.

> **智械游客：**这个好，我最喜欢自然风光了。那我们就先去鳞渊境吧。

**Pepeshi Tourist:** I'm a bit worried that nature-themed dream bubbles might be outdated... but hey, let's go check it out anyway.

> **皮皮西游客：**我有点担心，自然风光相关的梦泡会不会有些过时…不过无妨，我们去看看吧。

**Intellitron Tourist:** See you later. Maybe we'll run into each other there in a few days.

> **智械游客：**再见，也许这几天我们会在鳞渊境再相遇。

- ▸ Why not climb to the top of the Ambrosial Arbor?

> ▸ 去爬上建木之巅吧！

**March 7th:** Hey! That's a terrible idea! You'll get them arrested!

> **三月七：**喂！不要乱给别人出这种会被抓起来的馊主意啦！

**Intellitron Tourist:** Haha... you're just joking, right? How about giving us some more practical suggestions?

> **智械游客：**哈哈…你真有意思。还是给我们提供些更靠谱的建议吧。

**Pepeshi Tourist:** ...Wait a sec. Maybe it's not such a terrible idea after all? No risk, no reward! Come on, let's go climb that tree!

> **皮皮西游客：**…等一下。这个主意未必是个馊主意。富贵险中求啊！走吧，我们去爬树！

**Intellitron Tourist:** Slow down a bit. Wait for me.

> **智械游客：**你跑慢点，等我一下。

**March 7th:** Hey, wait! Just think about what you're doing! And... don't mention our names if something goes wrong! Ugh, I hope they don't get into trouble... This is nerve-wracking.

### Accompany Yanqing and ask about the situation from the Cloud Knights present

*(Approach Cloud Knight Soldier and IPC Worker)*

**IPC Worker:** While we appreciate your rescue, my Xianzhou friends, don't you think it's a bit too much to detain us and our cargo?

> **星际和平公司员工：**仙舟的朋友，你们虽然救了我们，我们是很感激。但连我们带货物也一块扣押，这是不是就有些不像话了？

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** Sorry, but we've been ordered to detain you and your cargo for inspection until we figure out the source of the attack.

> **云骑士卒：**我们收到命令，在查清袭击事件前因后果之前，必须暂时扣押贵方的货物进行检查。

> **星际和平公司员工：**这批货物压根就不是运往罗浮的，还是公司的专利科技，你们凭什么「检查」啊？

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** According to the protocol, all cargo arriving on the Luofu must go through inspection.

> **云骑士卒：**按照流程，任何抵达罗浮的货物都必须经过检查。

> **星际和平公司员工：**我们也不算入港啊！我们是遭遇了步离人的袭击，才不得不来罗浮的港口避难啊！

**Yanqing:** Looks like this argument could go on forever. Let's not get involved in a heated dispute that won't lead to a resolution.

> **彦卿：**看起来，他们会争论很久。像这样吵不出个结果的纠纷，咱们还是不要卷入为妙。

> **云骑队长：**是我…抱歉，我们一时看守失察，都是我的责任。

**Yanqing:** With the Wardance approaching, safety should be a top priority. Now, tell me, how did borisin prisoners end up in Starskiff Haven?

> **彦卿：**来，说说吧，演武仪典召开在即，罗浮的一切以安全稳定为上。为什么在星槎海会出现步离囚犯？

**Yanqing:** According to the protocol, borisin prisoners should be held on a starskiff and taken directly to The Shackling Prison under strict supervision without ever touching the ground. Who allowed a prisoner transport ship to dock at the passenger terminal?

> **彦卿：**按照流程，危险重犯应当收押在星槎中，全程不落地送往幽囚狱。是谁允许押解犯人的船停靠在客运码头的！

**???:** Please don't blame this captain. This incident involves the Zhuming's diplomatic vessel.

> **？？？：**怪不得这位队长，是朱明使节舰太过热心了。

**Yanqing:** Who are you?

> **彦卿：**你又是谁？

**???:** I'm Lujun, an officer of the patrol defense squad. Thank you for your help, Lieutenant Yanqing. The situation unfolded rapidly, and it shouldn't be held against the captain.

> **？？？：**在下路君，巡防卫队的值守武官。多谢彦卿骁卫出手相助。事起仓促，绝非这位队长的过错。

**Lujun:** Here's what happened. An IPC transport ship was attacked by the borisin just before arriving, and the Zhuming's diplomatic vessel came to the rescue. They fought off the borisin pirates and imprisoned them on their ship.

> **路君：**公司途径罗浮中转的运输船在即将抵达之际遭到了步离人的袭击。朱明的使节舰出手相助，击溃了这股步离劫匪并收押在船舱里。

**Yanqing:** So, an IPC ship was attacked by the borisin near the Luofu, and the Zhuming envoys saved them? This sounds complicated...

> **彦卿：**在罗浮航行的宙域，公司的舰船，被步离人打劫，而朱明的使节救了他们？这案情听起来真复杂……

**Lujun:** Honestly, it gives me a headache too. The Zhuming diplomatic ship, adhering to standard procedure, docked at the passenger terminal to hand these criminals over to the Luofu.

> **路君：**属下也是百思不得其解。朱明的使节舰按规矩，停靠在客船码头，将这群犯人移交罗浮这边。

**Lujun:** You know, with all the outsiders flooding onto the Luofu, the starskiff lanes are under immense pressure. The borisin desperadoes decided to put up a fight before the prisoner transport starskiff could get there, and that's what you just witnessed.

> **路君：**您也知道，这些日子来，数不清的外客涌入罗浮，星槎航道压力陡增。还没等来押解的星槎与航道管制，步离人狂徒便狗急跳墙了。于是有了方才您看到的一幕。

**Cloud Knight Captain:** We'll make sure these prisoners are sent to The Shackling Prison as soon as possible.

> **云骑队长：**我们会尽快将这些人犯送往幽囚狱的。

**Yanqing:** I see, it's an unusual situation indeed. I'll report it to the Security Department of the Realm-Keeping Commission and ask for their cooperation in handling the aftermath.

> **彦卿：**如此看来，确实有事急从权之处。我会将此事呈报给地衡司的治安厅，让他们配合善后。

The Cloud Knights calm the crowd, then dispersed, and the street was restored to its peaceful state.

**March 7th:** You look much maturer now, Yanqing.

**Lujun:** We'll see to it that these criminals are sent to The Shackling Prison as soon as possible. The cleanup operation is already underway, Lieutenant Yanqing, so there's no need to worry about anything.

> **路君：**我和弟兄们会尽快将这群劫匪送进幽囚狱，善后工作也在进行，彦卿骁卫请勿担心。

**Yanqing:** Thanks. By the way, you're... an officer of the patrol defense squad, right? I don't remember meeting you before...

> **彦卿：**有劳了。你是…巡防卫队的武官？看起来似乎面生的很……

**Lujun:** Well, I'm just a junior officer. I recently returned to the Luofu from the front lines and was temporarily assigned here for security duty before the ceremony.

> **路君：**哪儿的话，将军侍卫又怎么会记得我这样的小小角色。在下刚刚随出征部队返抵罗浮，因为仪典召开前的安全工作，被临时调派至此。

## 014　Emissary Mystique　衔令来使，真意难识

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Palace of Astrum　|　角色：Trailblazer; March 7th; Dan Heng; Jing Yuan; Yanqing; Huaiyan; Yunli; Jiaoqiu; Moze　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Homecoming Wardance'' completed

### Head to the Palace of Astrum to see the General

> *With Yanqing welcoming you, you returned to the Xianzhou Luofu, where you have been away for a — um, not a particularly long time — to participate in the upcoming Wardance. The general awaits your arrival in the Palace of Astrum. What kind of task will he use to "welcome" the guests from the Express? Just thinking about it is making you feel the tiniest bit of flutter.*

*(Enter the Palace of Astrum)*

**Jing Yuan:** It's been a long journey, Elder Huaiyan. Thank you for your presence.

> **景元：**炎老，舟车劳顿，真是辛苦您了。

**Huaiyan:** Heh, don't mention it. Thank you for taking the time to welcome me.

> **怀炎：**哎，不辛苦，倒是劳你拨冗迎接了。

*(Approach Jing Yuan and Huaiyan)*

**Yanqing:** General, I've brought our guests from the Express. I'm sorry for my bad timing. I didn't know you were meeting a guest, General.

> **彦卿：**将军，我将列车的客人接来了。不知道将军见客，彦卿来的不是时候。

**Jing Yuan:** Don't worry. You're just in time. It's been a while, my friends from the Astral Express!

> **景元：**无妨，你来得正是时候。星穹列车的诸位，好久不见！

- ▸ How's everything going, General?

**Jing Yuan:** All is well when you have friends coming from afar.

> **景元：**有朋远来，自然一切都好。

- ▸ I dreamed about you on Penacony.

**Jing Yuan:** Oh? Well It's an honor to be in your dreams, my friend.

> **景元：**喔？我入故人梦，明我长相忆。能在诸位的美梦之中有一席之地，荣幸之至。

- ▸ Did you miss us?

**Jing Yuan:** Sure, how couldn't I have? It's not often we find such moments to reunite and enjoy one another's presence.

> **景元：**想，怎么能不想呢？数人世相逢，百年欢笑，能得几回又？

**March 7th:** Since when did (Trailblazer) and General Jing Yuan become such good buddies, Dan Heng? They seem to be having a great time.

> **三月七：**丹恒，开拓者到底什么时候和将军混得这么熟了？两人说起话来谈笑风生的。

**Dan Heng:** A lot must have happened without our knowledge. At least I hope that's the case...

> **丹恒：**在我们不知道的时候，一定发生了不少事。最好是这样……

**Jing Yuan:** Allow me to introduce you to General Huaiyan. He's the Arbiter-General of the Xianzhou Zhuming, known as the Flaming Heart.

> **景元：**容我向诸位引介，这位是仙舟「朱明」的天将，烛渊将军，怀炎。

**Huaiyan:** Hehe, no need to be so formal. I'm just a tourist here, no different from other tourists who've come to attend the ceremony.

> **怀炎：**哈哈。不必如此正式，老朽此行便衣简从，与来观礼的游客并无区别。

**Jing Yuan:** Elder Huaiyan is not only one of the Arbiter-Generals but also the Furnace Master of the Artisanship Commission. Besides his martial skills, he excels in forging various weapons. Such talents are unique, even among the Arbiter-Generals.

> **景元：**炎老不仅是帝弓的将军，亦是工造司的百冶；戎马倥偬之外，更擅长百般工巧。如此人瑞，在天将中亦是独一无二。

**Huaiyan:** Be it Arbiter-General or Furnace Master, these are merely titles given to me long ago. I've retired several times already, but with the current change in circumstances, the Marshal has called me back to duty, and I had no choice but to answer the call.

> **怀炎：**将军也好、百冶也罢，都不过是应时加身的名头。老朽早已卸任数次，只是如今局势变化，元帅再度征召，我不得不走马上任。

**Huaiyan:** Well, in the end, I'm to blame. Living such a long life naturally brings its share of disapproval.

> **怀炎：**说到底也怪我，活得实在是太久了些，难免遭人非议。

**Dan Heng & Yanqing:** It's an honor to meet you, General Huaiyan.

> **丹恒与彦卿：**拜见怀炎将军。

**March 7th:** It's my honor to meet you, General Huaiyan...

> **三月七：**拜见怀炎将军……

- ▸ Glad to meet you, General.

**Huaiyan:** No need to be all formal. Today I'm just a guest on the Luofu, the same as all of you.

> **怀炎：**诸位不必客气。今日我与你们一样，是罗浮的客人。

**Huaiyan:** So, these three are the ones you mentioned, Jing Yuan? The heroes who helped you with the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis?

> **怀炎：**景元，这三位就是你之前提到的，助你弭平建木灾异的救星咯？

**Jing Yuan:** Indeed. There's Dan Heng, March 7th, and (Trailblazer)... Without their help, I'm afraid the Luofu might not have easily overcome this crisis.

> **景元：**正是。丹恒，三月七，还有开拓者…若无这几位朋友力挽狂澜，罗浮怕不能轻松渡过此劫。

**Huaiyan:** So, the Imbibitor Lunae's reincarnation has returned to the Luofu and will attend the Wardance. I'd love to have a drink with you should the chance present itself.

> **怀炎：**饮月君的后世重回罗浮观礼演武仪典，若有机会，老朽想同你喝上一杯。

**Dan Heng:** You're more than welcome, General Huaiyan.

> **丹恒：**丹恒随时奉陪。

**Huaiyan:** And this young friend is...?

> **怀炎：**那旁边这位小朋友是……？

**Jing Yuan:** Yanqing, my apprentice. He remains by my side as my retainer due to his youth, which I hope will season him with experience. He will stand for the Luofu's Cloud Knights in the upcoming Wardance, ready to take on all kinds of challenges.

> **景元：**我的弟子，彦卿。只因年纪尚浅，晚辈让他待在身边充任侍卫，希望能让他多受历练。这次演武仪典，他会代表罗浮云骑守擂竞锋，接受四方挑战。

**Huaiyan:** Great, great! It's a real treat to see so many talented young people around here today.

> **怀炎：**好、好、好！老朽今日能一次得见这么多青年俊彦，真是不枉此行。

**Huaiyan:** Oh, I almost forgot... This is my apprentice, Yunli...

> **怀炎：**哦对了，上了年纪便容易忘事…旁边这位是我的徒孙，云璃……

**Yanqing:** It's you!

> **彦卿：**是你！

**Yunli:** Oh, it's you. Hello.

> **云璃：**喔，是你啊。你好。

**Huaiyan:** Oh, you two already know each other? Guess we don't need any introductions then!

> **怀炎：**嚯嚯，你们认得啊，看来不用介绍了！

**Yanqing:** What a coincidence! I was afraid I'd have trouble finding this girl.

> **彦卿：**真是踏破铁鞋无觅处，我本以为要费上不少工夫才能找到这位姑娘。

**Huaiyan:** Oh? Now you've piqued my curiosity... Tell me, how did you two become acquainted?

> **怀炎：**喔？老朽的好奇心都被吊起来了…快说说，你们俩怎么就互相认得了？

**Yanqing:** She helped me capture the escaped borisin prisoners at the Starskiff Haven. Allow me to express my gratitude for you, but when you left... you took my Flying Sword with you.

> **彦卿：**这位姑娘在星槎海出手帮助镇伏逃跑的步离人囚犯，彦卿先在此谢过。但你离开时…也顺手带走了我的飞剑。

**Yunli:** Your Flying Sword?... Oh, so that's why I found a dagger in my bag. Turns out it's yours.

> **云璃：**飞剑？…喔，我还在想怎么行囊里凭空多出一把短剑，原来是你的。

**Yanqing:** Yes, it is. Now that we've met again, I hope...

> **彦卿：**正是。既然有缘再会，希望你能……

**Yunli:** Nope, that won't do.

> **云璃：**这恐怕不好。

**Yanqing:** Won't do?

> **彦卿：**不好？

**Yunli:** You want your sword back, right? Well, you can't just take it back. On the Zhuming, when you lose your sword on the battlefield, you have to reclaim it on the battlefield.

> **云璃：**你是想要回这把剑吧？可以，但不能是以这种方式。我们朱明的规矩，在战场上失去的剑，要在战场上拿回来。

**Yunli:** As for this little sword, it was supposed to strike that escaped borisin prisoner, but unfortunately its owner's agitated state caused it fly off like a kite with a broken string, and it missed its target.

> **云璃：**这把小剑便是如此。它飞在空中，本该射向那名逃窜的步离人。可惜剑主心性纷扰，反倒让它像只折翼的飞鸢，失了准头。

**Yunli:** By the way, if I hadn't caught it and helped it hit its mark, that borisin prisoner would have gotten away.

> **云璃：**况且，若不是我伸手接过这剑，助它命中目标，那步离人早已逃之夭夭了。

**Yanqing:** Hold on a sec, Yunli. You took my sword without even asking, and now you're refusing to give it back?

> **彦卿：**云璃姑娘，你二话不说带走了我的剑，怎么还能若无其事地拒绝物归原主？

**Yunli:** ...Heh, so much for Luofu swordmasters.

> **云璃：**…罗浮剑士，不过如此。

**Yanqing:** What did you just say?

> **彦卿：**你说什么？

**Yunli:** If you just stepped up and took your sword back from me fair and square, I would have totally respected you. But nope, you tried to play it down, expecting me to just hand it back to you like it's nothing, in front of everyone...

> **云璃：**你若能大大方方、堂堂正正地从我手上夺回自己的剑，云璃心服口服。可你没有，你竟想着大事化小，当着大庭广众随随便便让我还剑……

**Yunli:** With all due respect, you don't honor your sword, so you don't deserve it.

> **云璃：**恕我直言，你对助我等杀敌护身的剑器毫无尊重，配不上这柄剑。

**Yanqing:** Hasn't anyone told you that taking without asking is stealing? If you want to settle this with swords... Fine, let's have a one-on-one duel right now...

> **彦卿：**姑娘，难道没人教过你「不告而取是为贼」吗？如果非要用剑说话…可以，就咱们俩，现在一对一……

**Jing Yuan:** Yanqing.

> **景元：**彦卿。

**Yunli:** Well, that's more like it. Just be careful, because I'm not as easy to handle as the borisin.

> **云璃：**嗯，这才对。你可得小心了，我不像步离人那般好对付。

**Huaiyan:** You too. Be quiet and apologize to Yanqing!

> **怀炎：**你也住嘴吧，赶快向彦卿弟弟赔罪！

**Yunli:** Hey, whose side are you on, Grandpa?

> **云璃：**爷爷，你到底站哪头的？

**Huaiyan:** I... um... I don't take sides!

> **怀炎：**爷爷…呃，爷爷哪头都不站！

**Jing Yuan:** It's a small misunderstanding, and an apology would be too much. I've heard about the Zhuming's incredible swordplay and craftsmanship, most notably the legendary Flamewheel Octet. Seeing Miss Yunli, who is among those ranks today... Well I must say, she definitely has that fiery edge.

> **景元：**小小误会，「赔罪」言重了。耳闻朱明仙舟剑术与匠艺位于巅峰者有「焰轮八叶」的美名传世，今日见到名列其间的云璃小姐…确有火一般的风姿心性。

**Huaiyan:** ...Such grandiose names. Some folks love to spin these fancy titles, trying to set the Cloud Knights apart. Yunli is still just a young girl, a bit awkward and hot-tempered. So please forgive her if she's being rude.

> **怀炎：**…什么美名。总有好事之人热爱编撰虚名，为云骑乱立山头。云璃只是个孩子，面薄怕生，行事无礼之处还望各位见谅。

**Jing Yuan:** Well, everyone, Elder Huaiyan and I have some business to discuss. For now, Yanqing, why don't you entertain our guests and take Miss Yunli to the inn?

> **景元：**诸位，我和炎老还有些事要商量。眼下让彦卿招待几位客人与云璃小姐先去客栈入住。

**Jing Yuan:** I'll find another chance to talk with you all. I'd like to express my gratitude to the Astral Express for helping the Luofu during the crisis.

> **景元：**之后我会另选时机与各位畅谈。好好答谢星穹列车当初救助罗浮于水火之中的恩情。

**March 7th:** That's so kind of you. I mean you've already thanked us so many times.

> **三月七：**真是客气，您都谢了好多回了。

**Huaiyan:** Please forgive me for coming at an inconvenient time.

> **怀炎：**怪老朽来得不是时候，三位客人见谅。

**Dan Heng:** You needn't apologize, General Huaiyan.

> **丹恒：**怀炎将军言重了。

**Huaiyan:** Alright, Yunli. Take this opportunity to clear things up with Yanqing.

> **怀炎：**丫头，去吧，借这个机会好好和彦卿解开误会。

**Yunli:** Yeah, yeah, it's better to make friends than enemies, but I won't be heading to the inn just yet... I want to visit Lingsha. She just arrived on the Luofu and could use some help settling in.

> **云璃：**是是是，冤家宜解不宜结。不过，我打算先不去客栈…我要见见灵砂姐姐。她刚到罗浮，正需要人帮忙安顿呢。

**Jing Yuan:** Yanqing, once you've helped our guests get settled, go to the Artisanship Commission for me. I've heard about the attack and the detainment of the IPC ship. Qingzu sent word that the IPC members are protesting and wish to have their cargo back.

> **景元：**彦卿，安顿了客人后，替我再跑一趟工造司。我听闻公司舰船遇袭被扣押一事。青镞送来了消息，说公司的人正在抗议，想取回船上的货物。

**Jing Yuan:** See if you can calm them down. Don't get aggressive. Just make it clear that the Xianzhou Luofu has no intention of violating their rights.

> **景元：**你代表我去安抚他们一下吧，不可太强硬，只要表达罗浮仙舟无意侵犯他们的权利就是。

**Yanqing:** I'm on it.

> **彦卿：**彦卿领命。

### Leave the Palace of Astrum with Yanqing

> *Accompanied by Yanqing, you all finally gained a meeting with Jing Yuan, learning the purpose of the Wardance's revival after the Ambrosial Arbor crisis. You also learned of the generals of the Zhuming and Yaoqing coming forth to observe the ceremony. Their motives were anything but earnest.*

*(Talk to Jing Yuan and Huaiyan, optional)*

**Jing Yuan:** I'm sorry for Yanqing's rudeness.

**Huaiyan:** Not at all. Taunted by Yunli like that, even a training dummy would be angry. You've taught your apprentice well. If it were me in my younger days, I'm afraid I'd have drawn my sword and fought.

**Huaiyan:** Yanqing can understand your concerns. On the other hand, Yunli is still a naive girl who's only interested in swordplay. Honestly, I brought her here to participate in the Wardance because I want to broaden her horizons.

*(Talk to Yunli, optional)*

**Yunli:** This trip is totally worth it. Compared to the Zhuming, the Luofu is so much livelier.

**Yunli:** But it's a shame the Luofu swordmasters don't seem that great...

- ▸ Glad to meet you, Miss Yunli.

**Yunli:** Glad to meet you, too. Are you here to participate in the Wardance?

- ▸ I'm just a tourist attending the ceremony.

**Yunli:** So, you're not a participant? You look like you know your way around a fight.

**Yunli:** Well, you can still sign up. The Cloud Knights' ring is always open to outsiders.

- ▸ Can outsiders participate in the Wardance as well?

**Yunli:** Of course. To honor martial arts, the Cloud Knights' ring is always open to outsiders.

**Yunli:** If you can defeat the ringmaster selected by the Xianzhou, you'll not only win rewards and prizes but also be making a name for yourself.

**Yunli:** Sounds tempting, doesn't it?

- ▸ What's the "Flamewheel Octet" the general spoke of?

**Yunli:** That's how outsiders refer to my peers in the Zhuming Cloud Knights and the Artisanship Commission.

**Yunli:** We all train under Grandpa's guidance, learning the art of craftsmanship and swordplay. That's how we got that title, I guess.

**Yunli:** Just don't mention it in front of Grandpa. He always says that empty titles bring pointless challenges and conflicts. We Zhuming swordmasters pride ourselves on the success of the group over the individual.

- ▸ Aren't you coming with us?

**Yunli:** I have some things to do. Besides, little Yanqing doesn't seem too happy about me tagging along.

- ▸ He shouldn't have said you "stole" his sword.
- ▸ But you did take his sword...

**Yunli:** You're right. I "took" it with my skills, so I guess you can say I'm a robber or something. How dare he imply I'm a thief. Totally different!

**Yunli:** Besides, the sword seems scared of its own master. I'd like to ask him – you claim to cherish your sword as your life, yet you don't even recognize the state it's in right now?

**Yunli:** I didn't intend to keep his sword. I was planning to take this chance to return it to him, but now I've changed my mind.

**Yunli:** I'll give that poor flying sword some proper maintenance. I won't consider returning the sword unless he learns how to say "please," "hello," "thank you," and "sorry."

**Yunli:** So he can forget about it for now!

You scratch your head, feeling a tingling sensation on your scalp at the thought of the impending troubles Yanqing will face. While you're distracted, the young girl has already wandered off on her own, her whereabouts unknown.

*(Talk to March 7th and Dan Heng, optional)*

**March 7th:** Hey, Dan Heng, I thought all the Xianzhou generals were like Jing Yuan...

**March 7th:** But that General Huaiyan, he looks... so thin and scraggy, as if even a light breeze would knock him over. Is that old man really an Arbiter-General?

**Dan Heng:** Each Xianzhou ship has its own division and specialty within the Alliance, not all of the Arbiter-Generals are good at leading the charge. You heard what Jing Yuan said, right? General Huaiyan was once the head of all the skilled craftsmen on the Zhuming.

- ▸ There appears to be more than meets the eye.

**March 7th:** Don't you find it strange, (Trailblazer)? Do Xianzhou people age? I rarely see any old people on the Xianzhou ship, and when I do, they're usually travelers.

**March 7th:** But... General Huaiyan looks like an ordinary old grandpa. Is he really a Xianzhou general?

- ▸ Why does he look so old?

**March 7th:** Yeah, you've noticed that too. It's really weird... Do the Xianzhou people age? I rarely see any old people on the Xianzhou ship, and when I do, they're usually travelers.

**March 7th:** But... General Huaiyan looks like an ordinary old grandpa. Is he really a Xianzhou general?

- ▸ Perhaps he's a robot.

**March 7th:** Like Madam Herta? Hmmm, that would make sense. Anyway, it's really weird for a Xianzhou native to look so old.

**Dan Heng:** Good question. He's right there, so why don't you ask him yourself?

**March 7th:** Nah, I'd like to live a little longer.

**Dan Heng:** We should leave. General Jing Yuan asked Yanqing to entertain us, so obviously, he has important matters to discuss. Let's not waste any time here.

*(Talk to Yanqing)*

**Yanqing:** This is the report Qingzu just sent me. Let me take a look...

> **彦卿：**这是青镞姐刚才发过来的案呈，让我看看……

**Yanqing:** ...

> **彦卿：**……

**Yanqing:** ...

> **彦卿：**……

**Yanqing:** Looks like the general has given me a tough challenge. He wants me to try and help put the IPC's mind at ease.

> **彦卿：**将军还真是给了我一个大难题，要说服公司。

- ▸ Is this some kind of test from the general?

**Yanqing:** Well, it's not exactly a "test." As Cloud Knight officers, we not only learn the art of war and martial arts but also occasionally have to handle diplomatic disputes.

> **彦卿：**也谈不上「考验」吧。除了战术与武艺之外，云骑士官偶尔也得解决一些外交纠纷。

**Yanqing:** It's just, you know... Talking things out isn't as straightforward as duking it out with weapons on the battlefield. This is especially true when you're up against the IPC, with their non-stop corporate babble.

> **彦卿：**只是…用语言交流，终归不如战场上用武器沟通那么简单。尤其是要面对公司的人，他们说起话来总是一套一套的，最让人头疼了。

**Yanqing:** Well, let's not worry about that for now. Shall I take you to the inn?

> **彦卿：**不操心这些了，我带各位去客栈吧？

- ▸ Let's go.
- ▸ Not yet.

After watching Yanqing and the others leave...

**Jing Yuan:** It is the Luofu's honor to have you in attendance at the Wardance.

> **景元：**罗浮的演武仪典能得到您大驾光临，自然是罗浮的荣幸。

**Jing Yuan:** Yet, the fact that a simple martial arts ceremony has attracted esteemed generals from the Zhuming and the Yaoqing implies intentions beyond mere spectating. Might there be any specific instructions from the Marshal?

> **景元：**只是，区区一个演武仪典，却能劳动「朱明」和「曜青」的两位天将同时到来，恐怕也不仅仅是为了观礼吧？炎老这次前来，元帅可有什么吩咐？

**Huaiyan:** You're overthinking it, Jing Yuan. As I said, I'm here to broaden my granddaughter's horizons. I have no ulterior motives.

> **怀炎：**景元，你多心啦。我说过，这次前来除了让孩子见见世面，老朽对罗浮的状况并无说三道四的想法。

**Huaiyan:** However, I have no clue what the Yaoqing Arbiter-General has in mind.

> **怀炎：**但那位曜青将军有什么打算，同样也不是老夫能说三道四的。

**Huaiyan:** Do you remember when you accepted this position? I told you that an Arbiter-General's battlefield goes beyond the physical one. You'll need to lead and manage everything on the Xianzhou... The title of Arbiter-General holds a weight far greater than its literal meaning.

> **怀炎：**还记得吗？在你履任之初，我曾告诉过你，「帝弓天将的战场不仅在仙舟之外」。上阵折冲，对内斡旋…将军这个名头所承载的重量要远胜于它的字面意思。

**Huaiyan:** So many years have passed, and you've done well. However, longevity for the Xianzhou people can be a curse. Living too long means that every mistake you made will lurk in the shadows, and one day, they'll eventually catch up to you.

> **怀炎：**那么多年过去了，你始终做得很好。但对仙舟人来说，活得太久是一种诅咒。这意味着生命中犯下的每一次错误都在暗处虎视眈眈，终有一日追上你，吞没你。

**Huaiyan:** The Marshal knows everything that has happened on the Luofu. As for the Merlin's Claw of the Yaoqing...

> **怀炎：**罗浮上发生的一切，元帅皆已知悉。而曜青的天击将军……

**Huaiyan:** ...She has come specifically for you.

> **怀炎：**…她正是为你而来。

**Huaiyan:** Speaking of which, why hasn't she arrived yet? They say the Merlin's Claw strikes like lightning. Being late isn't her style.

> **怀炎：**不过怪了，她怎么还没来？都说曜青的天击将军一贯动如雷霆，先声夺人，今天这么迟到可不是她的作风啊。

**???:** That's not true, General Huaiyan. She's been here a while, but I'm sure you've heard of her unbridled nature.

> **？？？：**怀炎将军此言差矣，敝上一早就到了。只是两位想必也有所耳闻，她的性情向来不受拘束。

**???:** As soon as she disembarked from the starskiff, she mentioned having something to attend to and simply disappeared.

> **？？？：**她一下星槎就跑了个没影，说有事要办，拦都拦不住。

**Jing Yuan:** You must be the messengers from the Xianzhou Yaoqing, I assume.

> **景元：**想必两位就是仙舟「曜青」的使者了。

**???:** We are Jiaoqiu and Moze, retainers of the Merlin's Claw. It's an honor to meet you in person, Arbiter-Generals.

> **？？？：**天击将军帐下幕僚，椒丘、貊泽，拜见两位天将。

**Huaiyan:** Now, this is interesting. A guest who doesn't come to visit but sends a message instead. What does she mean? Tell me, what could be more important to her than coming here?

> **怀炎：**有意思，客人到访不径直来见主人，反倒是派人传信。这又是唱的哪一出？你说说，她有什么比来这儿更重要的事情啊？

**Jiaoqiu:** Master heard about a spectacular view in Scalegorge Waterscape. I believe she went there to enjoy it.

> **椒丘：**敝上听说，罗浮之上鳞渊境中最近多了一处奇景，十分壮观，想来是赏景去了。

**Huaiyan:** A "spectacular" view? Hah! Did you hear that, Jing Yuan? This person is being sarcastic.

> **怀炎：**好个「奇景」。我来给你翻译翻译，景元，这小子是在阴阳怪气你呢。

**Jiaoqiu:** Please do not misunderstand me, General Huaiyan. I was simply stating the truth. Master thought it would be inappropriate to keep you waiting, so she sent us here...

> **椒丘：**怀炎将军言重了，在下不过是据实传达罢了。我家将军考虑到让两位久等不妥，先遣我们二人前来……

**Jiaoqiu:** Once she's finished with admiring the scenery, she'll personally come and apologize to the both of you.

> **椒丘：**待她赏景结束，便会亲自到访，向两位致歉。

In the meantime, outside the Palace of Astrum...

**Yanqing:** Drat, I forgot to ask Yunli to return my sword...

> **彦卿：**糟糕，忘了让云璃把剑还我了……

- ▸ I'll go get it back for you.

**Yanqing:** No need to bother. The Zhuming envoys won't be leaving the Luofu anytime soon. I'll ask for it later when we meet again.

> **彦卿：**不必麻烦了。朱明的人一时半刻也不会离开罗浮，之后见面再讨要也不迟。

- ▸ I don't think you'll ever get your sword back!

**Yanqing:** Unacceptable! Every sword is precious to me!

> **彦卿：**那可不行！每把剑都是彦卿的宝贝！

**Yanqing:** If it weren't for the general's interference, I would have taught that shameless girl a lesson in swordplay...

> **彦卿：**若不是将军阻拦就凭那女孩大言不惭的口气，我一定要让她好好领教一番我的剑法…

**Yanqing:** By the way, I don't know if it's just me, but the general seemed a little... hmmm, reserved? Could it be because of Elder Huaiyan's visit?

> **彦卿：**说起来，不知是不是错觉，我总觉得今天的将军有些…唔，有些拘谨？是因为那位怀炎老先生的到访吗？

**March 7th:** Reserved? Really?

> **三月七：**拘谨？有吗？

**Yanqing:** Maybe I'm just overthinking things.

> **彦卿：**也许是彦卿多心了。

**Dan Heng:** No, you're not. When I entered the Palace of Astrum, I realized that the messenger from the Xianzhou Zhuming was actually the Arbiter-General himself. So, the messenger from the Xianzhou Yaoqing must be the Merlin's Claw herself, I presume?

> **丹恒：**你并没有多心。进司辰宫开始，我才意识到你所说的朱明仙舟的使者竟是朱明的将军本人。那么，曜青仙舟的使者，想必也就是那位天击将军了？

**Yanqing:** That's right.

> **彦卿：**没错。

**Dan Heng:** That's what makes this entire thing so unusual.

> **丹恒：**这就是整件事情不寻常的地方了。

**March 7th:** What's so unusual about it? They simply received an invitation from Jing Yuan, just like the Crew, right?

> **三月七：**这到底有什么不寻常的地方嘛？他们不就是像列车组那样收到了来自景元的邀请？

**Dan Heng:** The Wardance is just a small festival, and now we have two Arbiter-Generals from other Xianzhou ships here. I'm afraid they're here for something more.

> **丹恒：**演武仪典不过是一场小小的节庆，能让其他仙舟的两位天将同时来到这儿，只怕另有原因。

**Dan Heng:** Maybe they've come to hold Jing Yuan accountable for the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis?

> **丹恒：**他们是为了罗浮建木灾异一事前来问责的吧。

**March 7th:** Hold him accountable? Come on, didn't the Luofu fall victim to the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus and the Antimatter Legion? Why would they blame the victim?

> **三月七：**问责？不至于吧。药王秘传的坏家伙和反物质军团入侵罗浮大搞破坏，罗浮不是受害者吗？哪有受害者被问责的道理啊？

**Dan Heng:** Dan Shu's rebellion and Phantylia's scheming are merely one side of the story to the other Arbiter-Generals of the Alliance. Only a single piece of incontrovertible evidence remains, creating an endless source of potential complications.

> **丹恒：**丹枢的叛乱、幻胧的计划…在联盟其他天将眼中只是一面之词。只有一样铁证被留了下来，遗患无穷。

**Yanqing:** The Ambrosial Arbor.

> **彦卿：**建木。

**Dan Heng:** Yes, it's undeniable that the plaguemark, which was subdued by the Xianzhou Luofu, has resurfaced. But was it really a conspiracy instigated by the Antimatter Legion, or does it indicate a traitorous intent from within the Luofu, implicating Jing Yuan himself?

> **丹恒：**罗浮仙舟压制许久的寿瘟祸迹再度重生，是不争的事实。但究竟是反物质军团的阴谋策动，还是罗浮内部起了叛心，连景元本人也涉入其中？

**Dan Heng:** Once the spark of suspicion is kindled, it proves hard to extinguish.

> **丹恒：**猜疑的火一旦点燃，就很难被熄灭了。

- ▸ We can vouch for Jing Yuan, though.

**Dan Heng:** The general must have had that in mind when he invited us to attend the Wardance

> **丹恒：**将军邀请我们前来演武仪典观礼，想必也有这一层考虑。

- ▸ No wonder we were called back for the ceremony.

**March 7th:** What was I thinking, seriously. Here I was looking forward to a carefree and enjoyable trip, but it seems wherever we go, drama is just around the corner.

> **三月七：**我真傻，真的。原以为能有一场说走就走，快快乐乐的旅行。现在看来，到哪儿星穹列车都脱不开是非啊。

**Yanqing:** Aww... I was so excited. I thought those Arbiter-Generals were just here to see the ceremony...

> **彦卿：**可恶，我一开始竟还真以为天将们只是前来观礼，还满心欢喜呢……

**Yanqing:** By the way, I heard that an alchemist from the Zhuming diplomatic ship has arrived, and rumors say that she's to be the new Cauldron Master of the Alchemy Commission on the Luofu.

> **彦卿：**如此想来，我听说这一次随朱明使节舰而来的还有一位丹士，据说会出任罗浮丹鼎司的新任司鼎。

**Dan Heng:** An alchemist from the Zhuming serving as the Cauldron Master on the Luofu? While it's not unheard of, the timing itself...

> **丹恒：**朱明派来的人，担任罗浮的司鼎？倒是有先例，只是这时机……

**Yanqing:** Thanks to your words, Mr. Dan Heng, now I finally see the underlying tensions.

> **彦卿：**听丹恒先生一说，彦卿才察觉到这背后涌动的暗流。

**Yanqing:** The general is under tremendous pressure right now, but I was completely oblivious to his troubles... How naive of me!

> **彦卿：**将军此时正承担着莫大的压力。可我竟全然不察将军的苦恼…真是幼稚！

**March 7th:** Come on! Don't think like that! Leave the adult matters to the adults. Even if you wanted to do something for the general, it's not like you can do anything.

> **三月七：**你也别这么想嘛！大人们的事本来就该大人们来操心。何况眼下你能为你家将军做些什么呢？

**Yanqing:** ...

> **彦卿：**……

**March 7th:** Uh, did I say something wrong again?

> **三月七：**呃，我是不是又说错话了。

- ▸ You're a real master of conversation!
- ▸ Please, March, just keep quiet.

**Yanqing:** Miss March is right. I don't have the skills to share the general's burdens at the moment.

> **彦卿：**三月小姐说的没错，彦卿本事低微，眼下还不能为将军分忧。

**Yanqing:** ...Still, I'll do my best to follow his instructions. Let's go. Once I've taken you to your accommodations, I need to go to the Artisanship Commission to handle the IPC's protest.

> **彦卿：**…但我可以尽力做好将军交代的每一件事。我们走吧，在将三位的住宿安排妥当后，我还要去工造司处理公司的抗议。

**March 7th:** He looks like he has a lot on his mind. We can't just let him go alone... Why don't we accompany you to the Artisanship Commission?

> **三月七：**看他这心事重重的样子，我还真有点放心不下…我们陪你一起去工造司吧！

**Yanqing:** This... is too much trouble. While I appreciate your kindness, dealing with the IPC workers could be tricky... I'm afraid this will cause trouble for the Express...

> **彦卿：**这…不妥吧。三位愿意帮忙，我很感激，但公司职员格外难缠…我担心反倒让列车惹上麻烦……

**March 7th:** No worries! We're pretty experienced in dealing with the IPC! You've heard of the Ten Stonehearts, right? We've dealt with quite a few of them, right, (Trailblazer)?

> **三月七：**你放心好了！对付公司的人，我们列车组可相当有经验！「石心十人」你听过吧？我们和其中好几个都打过交道！对吧，开拓者？

- ▸ Brag all you want, but don't drag me into this.

**March 7th:** Hey, who's bragging? Anyway, this time it's just a few ordinary IPC workers. Surely you don't think they'll be even trickier than Aventurine, do you?

> **三月七：**谁乱吹牛了！几个公司职员，难道比砂金还厉害？不能够吧～

- ▸ You're a real master of conversation!

**March 7th:** Yeah, exactly... Uhm, are you being sarcastic?

> **三月七：**就是就是…呃，你这话听着不像好话。

- ▸ Stop, stop! I don't want to end up in an IPC lawsuit.

**March 7th:** What's with you turning into a scaredy-cat? I didn't see you worrying about lawsuits when you wrecked that giant mech.

> **三月七：**你怎么越来越胆小了？你砸坏人家大铁人的时候也没见你怕吃官司。

**Yanqing:** Well, since you're willing to help, I won't decline your kindness. Let's head to the Artisanship Commission and meet them.

> **彦卿：**好吧，既然老师们有意帮忙，那我也不再推辞。去工造司会会他们吧。

## 015　From Growls to Grace　狴犴吠狂，妙语生香

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Artisanship Commission (Location)　|　角色：Trailblazer; Skott; Kongqing; Xikui; Yanqing; Dan Heng; March 7th; Lingsha　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Emissary Mystique'' completed

### Accompany Yanqing to the Artisanship Commission

> *At the advent of the Wardance ceremony, the Xianzhou Luofu became a crossroads of conflict among many — not just among guests of ulterior motives, but also IPC ships that were forced to dock after escaping from borisin attacks. To resolve everyone's disputes, you accompanied Yanqing to the Artisanship Commission.*

*(Upon teleporting to the Artisanship Commission)*

**???:** Can I really reason with you Xianzhou people?

> **？？？：**你们这些仙舟人到底讲不讲道理啊？

**???:** Heh, I think I get it now. In your words, this is called "looting a burning house," right?

> **？？？：**呵呵，我算是明白了～用你们的话说，这叫「趁火打劫」，对吧？

**Xikui:** But I AM trying to reason with you here!

> **夕葵：**我现在就是在和你讲道理啊！

*(Approach the marked location)*

**(Trailblazer):** (That toxic voice sounds familiar... Haven't I heard it before in Aurum Alley?)

> **开拓者：**（这阴阳怪气的声音实在耳熟…似乎在金人巷里听到过？）

**Skott:** You know what? This isn't my first time dealing with the Sky-Faring Commission, I can handle your unreasonable ways.

> **斯科特：**我也不是没和你们天舶司打过交道，你们那刁难人的行事作风我早就习惯了。

**Skott:** But straight-up snatching IPC cargo, isn't that going a bit too far?

> **斯科特：**但现在你们直接明抢公司的货物，这是不是有点过分了？

**Kongqing:** Just as I've said it many times already. Once we've inspected the cargo and completed the security check, you can be on your way. Is there something wrong with your ears, or is it just your brain?

> **空青：**都说了很多次了，只要开箱完成安全检查，我们自然会放行。你是耳朵出了问题，还是脑袋不利索。

**Skott:** I'm hearing you loud and clear! I'm thinking clear! And my answer is crystal clear: Not a chance!

> **斯科特：**我听得很清楚，也想得很明白！我的话说得更直白：没戏！

**Skott:** Keep detaining my cargo, and I'll file a complaint directly with your general!

> **斯科特：**再扣着我的货物不放，我一纸诉状直接告到你们将军那里去！

- ▸ What's all this arguing about?

**Skott:** Don't think you can possibly convince me! Uh huh?! Why are you here?!

> **斯科特：**你们叫多少人来都不可能说服我！嗯？！怎么是你啊？！

- ▸ Skott, what are you doing here!?

**Skott:** I was gonna ask you the same thing! Why do I keep running into you?!

> **斯科特：**我还想问你呢！怎么又遇见你了！

- ▸ Did I just hear a dog barking?

**Skott:** You jerk! Who are you calling a "dog?" Wait... w-why are you here?!

> **斯科特：**混蛋！你说谁是狗？怎么…又是你啊？！

**Skott:** You're staying on the Xianzhou, are you? What terrible luck. Wherever you go, disasters aren't far behind...

> **斯科特：**你这家伙是赖在仙舟不走了是吧？真是祸不单行啊，遇上你准没好事……

**Xikui:** Aren't you the guests from the Astral Express? What brings you and Yanqing to the Artisanship Commission?

> **夕葵：**这不是星穹列车的客人吗？怎么和彦卿一起来工造司了？

- ▸ I heard you're in trouble.

**Skott:** They're in trouble? No, it's me! I'm the one in trouble!

> **斯科特：**他们遇到了点麻烦？哼，是我！我遇到了麻烦！

- ▸ I'm here to help Yanqing sort out the trouble caused by the IPC.

**Skott:** Trouble caused by the IPC? I'd say it's caused by the Sky-Faring Commission!

> **斯科特：**公司的麻烦？该说是天舶司给我们造成的麻烦才对！

**Yanqing:** Looks like you've met this IPC worker before, (Trailblazer)... I'm not sure if that's a good or bad thing.

> **彦卿：**看来这位公司员工是开拓者老师的旧识…也不知这算是好事还是坏事。

**Yanqing:** I was sent here to deal with the IPC protest, Miss Xikui. What's going on here?

> **彦卿：**夕葵姐姐，我被派来解决公司的抗议，眼下到底是什么情况？

**Xikui:** As you know, this IPC transport ship was attacked by the borisin and rescued by the Zhuming's diplomatic ship. Then the Cloud Knights were instructed to bring it back to the dock for repairs and inspections.

> **夕葵：**如你所知，朱明使节舰救下了这艘被步离人袭击的公司运输船，然后知会云骑军辇道卫，将它带回港口完成修复与检查。

**Xikui:** And this is Mr. Skott, the person in charge of this transport ship.

> **夕葵：**这位，就是运输船的负责人，斯科特先生。

**March 7th:** So, you're Skott! I've heard (Trailblazer) mention you! Weren't you kicked off the Luofu before? Why did you come back?

> **三月七：**原来你就是斯科特啊！我听开拓者提起过你诶～你不是被赶出罗浮了吗？怎么又回来了？

**Skott:** Like I wanted to come back! I thought I'd just dock at the harbor for repairs and leave this forsaken place for good. Little did I know, as soon as the ship entered the harbor, a bunch of Cloud Knights showed up and snatched all our cargo from the hold!

> **斯科特：**你以为我想回来吗！我原本以为只是进港维修一番，就能离开这晦气的地方。谁知道一进港就冲上来几个云骑，把货舱里的货物都抢走了！

**Xikui:** What do you mean by "snatched?" I've told you a million times. It's a security check.

> **夕葵：**什么叫「抢走」？说了多少遍，这是安全检查。

**Skott:** Then why did you bring the cargo to the Artisanship Commission? You even brought in some shady craftsman. It's obvious that you're trying to steal the IPC's patented technology!

> **斯科特：**那怎么又拉到工造司来了？还找来个獐头鼠目的匠人，这不明摆着要偷学公司的专利技术吗？

**Kongqing:** Listen here... Firstly, the Sky-Faring Commission detected dangerous items that could possibly be weapons in your cargo hold, that's why they called me here to double-check. Secondly, where the heck did you get the idea that I'm a "shady craftsman!?"

> **空青：**我说这位…远道而来的客人，第一，天舶司检查到你们的货物中存在疑似危险武器的物品，所以才要求我来进行确认检查。第二，我到底哪里獐头鼠目了？！

**Skott:** Even if there are dangerous items, what do they have to do with you? It's not even being shipped to the Luofu. We'll just fix the ship and be on our way, we won't unload our cargo here.

> **斯科特：**我就纳闷了，就算是危险武器，关你们屁事啊，又不是运往罗浮？我们修好船就走，不会在这儿卸货的。

**Xikui:** But you'll have to stay in the port for several days before your ship is repaired and you can take off again. How can we just leave unchecked items sitting here?

> **夕葵：**但你们要在港口停留数日，才能修复引擎再次启航。我们怎么可能让未经检查的危险品在罗浮港口停留数日呢？

**Yanqing:** I understand... but we don't need to disassemble the cargo if it's just a security check, right?

> **彦卿：**我明白了…安全检查应该不需要拆解货物吧？

**Kongqing:** In most cases, we don't. However, our scans discovered that the cargo doesn't only contain machinery but also some... substance that resembles biological tissue.

> **空青：**本来不需要。我们原本只做了些进一步的扫描检测。但，扫描结果却发现货箱中存放的不只有机械结构，还有一些…近似生物组织的特征。

**March 7th:** Biological tissue? Does this crate contain living things?

> **三月七：**生物组织？这箱子里装着活物？

**Kongqing:** I'm afraid we'll need to wait for the Alchemy Commission for further confirmation. In any case, according to our regulations, we need to unseal one of the crates for further examination, but this IPC specialist has been hindering us on the grounds of patent secrecy.

> **空青：**具体的结论恐怕要等丹鼎司的人来确认。不管怎样，按照条例，我们都要启封其中一个货品进一步检查。但这位公司专员却用专利保密的理由一再阻挠。

**Xikui:** The Alliance's regulations on biological products are very strict. Without further inspection, there is no way for the Sky-Faring Commission to release the cargo.

> **夕葵：**联盟对生物制品的管理条例非常严格。如果不进一步检查，天舶司是无论如何都不可能放行的。

**Skott:** Oh, really? Fine! If anyone lays a finger on that shipment, they'll have me to deal with! It doesn't matter if it's mechanical or biological, it's none of your business! I'm filing a complaint against the Sky-Faring Commission's ridiculous regulations!

> **斯科特：**好啊，谁想动这批货物，我斯科特就和谁拼命！不管我们运的东西是生物还是机械，都和你们没有关系！我要投诉天舶司的处理程序！

**Yanqing:** This Mr. Skott seems stubborn and difficult to persuade. Honestly... I really don't want to have a vicious confrontation with the IPC...

> **彦卿：**看来这位斯科特先生非常顽固，不是很容易说服。唉…彦卿实在不希望和公司闹到撕破脸的地步……

- ▸ Well, I know a thing or two about convincing Skott...
- ▸ Leave it to me! I'm an expert negotiator!

**Yanqing:** I heard how you helped Aurum Alley, (Trailblazer). The IPC representative back then was Mr. Skott, right? Since you've dealt with him before... it looks like I'll have to rely on you again, (Trailblazer).

> **彦卿：**我听说过开拓者之前帮助金人巷的事情。当时公司方面的代表，就是这位斯科特先生吧？既然你有对付他的经验…看来这次也得麻烦开拓者老师了。

**Skott:** What are you guys whispering about over there? Just hurry up and give us back our cargo!

> **斯科特：**你们在那里嘀嘀咕咕什么呢？快点把货物还给我们！

### Help Yanqing debate with the IPC staff

*(Talk to Skott)*

*(Open Negotiation Tutorial)*

*(Negotiate with Skott)*

**(Trailblazer):** (As I recall, this guy won't listen to reason and can only be persuaded with intimidation. But he does seem to follow rules to some extent. Let's use that against him.)

*Opponent information:*

**Skott:**

One of the representatives stationed on Xianzhou by the IPC. He picks on the weak but fears the powerful. His experience in the IPC has taught him that he has to abide by the rules, at least on the surface.

*Negotiation strategies:*

*(Emotional Trigger)*

**(Trailblazer):** Your logic is too confusing for anyone to follow. Maybe you should learn the art of communication from a monkey before you continue spouting nonsense.

*(Smoke and Mirrors)*

**(Trailblazer):** Alright, we understand your position, but rules are rules, and we can't bend them now... However, we'll note down your concerns and pass them on to management. We'll keep you updated on any developments.

*(Feigned Retreat)*

**(Trailblazer):** Huh...? Could you repeat that? I didn't quite hear you. Could you say it again?

> **开拓者：**（我记得这家伙的性格属于典型的吃硬不吃软，但姑且还算是遵守规则。利用他的性格特点来对付他吧。）

*(Round 1)*

**Skott:** Speaking of regulations, we have our own laws and regulations too. According to Article 4 of the Xianzhou Alliance-IPC Trade Consensus, the Alliance and IPC shall never infringe on each other's intellectual property rights.

> **斯科特：**既然你们搬出安全规章，那咱们就来谈谈法律。按照《仙舟联盟-星际和平公司贸易共识宣言》第四款之规定，联盟和公司绝不能侵犯对方的知识产权。

- ▸ Make proposals from the legal perspective.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** The Alliance can sign a non-disclosure agreement with you. That way, you won't have to worry about any infringements, right?

> **开拓者：**联盟可以和你签署保密协议，这样就不用担心他们窥探了吧？

**Xikui:** We can sign a mutually acceptable non-disclosure agreement in accordance with the IPC's rules.

> **夕葵：**我们可以按照公司的规矩，签署一份双方都能接受的保密协议。

**Skott:** Well, t-that makes sense, but... how can we trust you to honor the terms?

> **斯科特：**话…话是这么说，可谁知道你们会不会遵守！

- ▸ Start by making him feel uncomfortable.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** I didn't expect to see you again, Mr. Skott! We had such fun in Aurum Alley last time. It seems our paths are destined to cross once more.

> **开拓者：**没想到还能和你再相见，斯科特先生～我还挺怀念咱们在金人巷的对手戏～也许这就是我们之间的缘分吧。

**Skott:** ...Stop pretending we're friends! If I could actually beat you, I'd punch you in the face! So, are you guys going to resolve this problem? Or are you just going to let this clown put on a show here?

> **斯科特：**…少套近乎！要不是我打不过你，非给你两拳不可！你们到底解决不解决问题？还是想让这小丑在这儿继续丢人现眼？

**March 7th:** Let's just pretend we don't know , Dan Heng.

> **三月七：**丹恒，我都想假装不认识他/她了。

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Back in Aurum Alley, you were dealing with civilian merchants, where you could use the IPC's name to scare them.

> **开拓者：**之前在金人巷你面对的是民间商会，你可以抬出公司来吓唬他们。

**(Trailblazer):** But this time, the Cloud Knight lieutenant standing before you represents the will of Luofu's general and won't be intimidated by your words.

**Yanqing:** Of course, I'm sure we both want a peaceful resolution.

> **彦卿：**当然，我相信我们双方都希望事情能和平解决。

**Skott:** Yeah, that's true. Of course, I want things to be resolved peacefully, but aren't we getting stuck in rigid formalities here?

> **斯科特：**说得不错，我当然希望事情能顺利解决，但眼下这不是被僵化的手续卡住了脖子吗？

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Hey, everyone! Meet my old friend, Skott. He's very talented, especially at imitating animals.

> **开拓者：**给诸位介绍一下，这位是我的老朋友斯科特。他多才多艺，尤其擅长模仿动物朋友。

**Skott:** S-stop it! Let's focus on the business at hand. What's the point in dragging up the past?

> **斯科特：**住…住口！谈事就谈事，你翻那些旧账有意思吗？

**March 7th:** Whoa, you're amazing! Can you teach me? Which animals can you imitate?

> **三月七：**哇！好厉害～能教教我吗？你都擅长模仿什么动物呀？

**Skott:** J-just forget it...

*(Round 2)*

**Skott:** Even if we set aside the secrecy of intellectual property, these prototypes built by the Intelligentsia Guild are incredibly valuable, beyond your wildest imagination! If anything goes wrong, you won't be able to pay for it even with your lives!

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** I've got a brilliant idea! I'll just smash everything with my bat, and then you can hire someone to assess the damage. That way, your cargo won't need any inspection, right?

> **开拓者：**对了，我要是抡起球棒一砸，你就不得不请人来核计损失索赔吧？这样一来，岂不就省去了安全检查？

**Yanqing:** Calm down, (Trailblazer). The general wants us to handle this peacefully.

**Skott:** So, this is the great idea you came up with? Fine! Why don't you just step over my lifeless body?

> **斯科特：**这就是你想出来的解决方案？行啊，你干脆从我尸体上踏过去算了。

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Don't underestimate Xianzhou technology. Even if they need to dismantle your cargo, the Artisanship Commission can easily put it back together.

> **开拓者：**你可别小瞧仙舟的技术。检查过程就算是把货物拆成零件，工造司的人也能给你拼回去。

**Skott:** Yeah, go ahead and brag about their skills. After all, you're just a negotiator they hired.

> **斯科特：**但我不一样，作为公司的成员，我的船队接受了这次运输订单，我就必须对这里的每一件货物的完好负责到底。

- ▸ Pay you compensation? You wish!

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Hah! I've taken Herta's curios, and even destroyed Ruan Mei's experimental subjects, but have I ever paid a penny for them? And now you think you can just come and ask me for compensation?

**Skott:** Listen up, brat. Don't think that your fancy connection to the Genius Society scares me. The IPC and those geniuses are working together. We're not taking orders from them.

> **斯科特：**听好了小子/小姑娘，少拿天才俱乐部的关系来压我。公司和那群高高在上的天才们是各取所需的关系，不是上下级。

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Can you give us an exact amount, Mr. Skott? If there's any damage after the inspection, the Express... um, I mean, the Sky-Faring Commission will compensate you.

> **开拓者：**斯科特先生能否核算出一个具体金额呢？在检修过后要是有所损坏，列车…呃，不对，是天舶司方面是愿意照价赔偿的。

**Xikui:** The Sky-Faring Commission... Yeah, they will compensate you... provided a detailed report of the damage is submitted.

> **夕葵：**天舶司…当然…可以赔偿。前提是要能明确出具相关的损坏报告。

**Skott:** I don't doubt the financial strength of the Sky-Faring Commission... However! This is not just about money!

> **斯科特：**我不怀疑天舶司的财力…但是！这也不是钱的事儿！

*(Round 3)*

**Skott:** Besides, the cargo on this transport vessel belongs to the Intelligentsia Guild. If you want to inspect the cargo, shouldn't you at least call in a member of the Intelligentsia Guild to be present?

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** I'm pretty tight with Dr. Ratio from the Intelligentsia Guild, almost like family. Doesn't that make me practically a member of the Intelligentsia Guild too?

**Skott:** What's all this nonsense? Even if you were married to him, you still couldn't represent the Intelligentsia Guild!

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** You're pulling out all the stops, and you've even dragged members of the Intelligentsia Guild into this. Seems like you're not interested in resolving this at all.

**(Trailblazer):** Since we've met each other before, Mr. Skott, let me share some information. Your transport ship was attacked by the borisin, and the Cloud Knights might see it as more than just a random attack.

> **开拓者：**斯科特先生，看在咱们打过交道的份上，我不妨给你透个底：你们的运输船被步离人袭击，这件事可大可小。

**Skott:** Hehe, you think you can scare me with that? What exactly do you mean?

> **斯科特：**呵呵呵，你当我是吓大的吗？可大怎么说，可小又怎么说？

**Yanqing:** Sure, you might purely be innocent victims of a borisin attack, but think about it another way. Why would the borisin launch such an attack near the Luofu? Perhaps some of your crew members colluded with them?

> **彦卿：**往小了说，列位是受步离人劫掠的被害者。但往大了说，为什么步离人要在通往罗浮的航线上袭击你们？莫非船员中有人勾结了步离人？

**Yanqing:** We may need to interrogate every one of your crew to eliminate this potential risk?

> **彦卿：**也许需要详加审问全体船员，才能排除这个隐患？

**Skott:** ...You know what? I don't think we really need a member from the Intelligentsia Guild present. I'm just hoping... that all the procedures will be thoroughly followed, you know?

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** According to the principle of territoriality, since your vessel is stranded on the Xianzhou, the Sky-Faring Commission has the right to inspect it.

> **开拓者：**按照属地原则，既然这批货物滞留在仙舟的港口，天舶司当然有权检查。

**Yanqing:** Indeed. According to Article 27 of the Xianzhou Legal Code: "All official and private goods, upon entering the port, shall be subject to inspection. Failure to submit to such inspection shall be deemed equivalent to the possession of unlawful items and shall be subject to legal ramifications."

> **彦卿：**的确如此。据《仙舟律》港阜卷廿七条所载：「诸官、私物，一入港阜，当受搜检。拒之者以赃论。」

**Skott:** Huh? My Synesthesia Beacon must not be working... because I have no idea what you just said.

> **斯科特：**啊？什么意思？…我这联觉信标怎么都翻译不出来？

**Yanqing:** Simply put, if you insist on hindering the inspection, we'll have to treat the cargo as unlawful items and confiscate it.

> **彦卿：**简单来说，就是如果你执意要拒绝我们搜查货物，我们就不得不将它当做赃物收缴了。

**Skott:** Confiscate it?! How... how can you Xianzhou people be so unreasonable!

> **斯科特：**收缴？！你们…你们仙舟人怎么能这么不讲道理！

**Yanqing:** If this were Pier Point, and incoming cargo posed a safety risk, the IPC would take it in for containment and disposal, correct?

> **彦卿：**斯科特先生，要是在庇尔波因特，入港的货物有安全隐患时，公司也会将它收容处置，没错吧？

**Skott:** Umm... t-that's true, but the IPC s-sometimes makes exceptions. For example, they've always given special terms to Xianzhou vessels.

> **斯科特：**呃…说的倒是没错，但、但公司偶尔也会允许一些例外。比如仙舟的舰船，公司向来是乐意网开一面的。

- ▸ Try to resolve this according to IPC regulations.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Since the Intelligentsia Guild entrusted the cargo to the IPC for transportation, it's the IPC's responsibility to ensure its safety, right? Why would you need a member of the Intelligentsia Guild to be present?

> **开拓者：**既然博识学会将货物交付给公司运输，公司就理应对货物的安全负责？为什么要联系博识学会？

**Skott:** Hehe, let me tell you something! As stated in Article 17 of the IPC's Building Material Logistics Department Transport Agreement Law: "Subsequent to the dispatch of goods and during their transit, all risks, save for those pertaining to the transportation safety, shall be the sole liability of the consignor."

> **斯科特：**嘿嘿，这就是你不懂了～按照公司的《筑材物流部运输合同法》第17条之规定：「当货物已寄出且仍在途中时，除运输事务安全外一切风险责任由寄件方承担。」

**Skott:** Well! We only protect the integrity of the goods and prevent anyone with ill intentions from getting their hands on them, for example...

> **斯科特：**哎！我们只负责保护货物的完整性，防止有心觊觎的人染指。比如…咳咳。

**Skott:** As for whether the cargo itself poses a security threat to the destination, that's not our responsibility. It appears we have reached an area beyond your expertise, doesn't it? The complexities of legal studies are indeed challenging!

> **斯科特：**至于货物本身是否会对目的地产生安全威胁，这个责我们可担不起。嘿嘿，触及到你们知识盲区了吧？法学难着呢！

*(Round 4)*

**Skott:** We both need to follow the regulations, because that's how the IPC and the Xianzhou operate, right? As an IPC worker, I have to abide by its regulations. If I make an exception and allow you to inspect the cargo, it'll spell disaster for me.

> **斯科特：**公司和仙舟都是法治社会，得遵守办事流程啊！我身为公司雇员，也必须遵守公司的条令律法。要是破例让你们检查，那真是把天给捅塌了啊。

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Everyone has their own set of rules. Now that you're on their turf, shouldn't you follow their rules, too?

**Skott:** Yeah, I get it. I know I'm on the Xianzhou, but if I violate the IPC regulations, I'll be in deep trouble when I get back!

> **斯科特：**我当然知道自己站在仙舟的疆域上，但是违反了流程，我回去也是要受处分的啊！

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** If this conflict with the Sky-Faring Commission escalates, it won't just make things difficult for your business, but it'll also have an impact on your career down the line.

**Skott:** Of course, I... I never wanted conflict with the Xianzhou. Like I said, all I wanted was to retrieve the cargo and be done with it, but they just refuse to let us go.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Please, help me! I mean it! I bragged to my friends that I could convince you... If you don't do me this favor, I'll be so embarrassed! Come on, don't be so cold-hearted!

**Yanqing:** (Trailblazer)... do we really have to stoop this low?

*(If negotiation fails)*

**Skott:** Alright, that's enough. I'm not going to repeat myself a hundred times.

> **斯科特：**够了，我讨厌把同样的理由重复说上一百遍。

**Skott:** I thought you were reasonable and civilized, but now it seems you Xianzhou folks just don't understand the basics of business.

> **斯科特：**原本以为各位是可以讲理的文明人，但现在看来，仙舟人还是不能理解何谓商业精神。

**Skott:** We've signed a confidentiality and security agreement with the Intelligentsia Guild, and if we breach it, we'll have to pay a hefty sum! Even if we sold the souls of every employee in the Marketing Development Department on this planet, it still wouldn't be enough to cover the costs.

> **斯科特：**我们与博识学会签署过保密安全协议，一旦违约就要支付天价赔偿！就算把这个星区市场开拓部的员工的灵魂全数典当了也填不上这个窟窿。

**Skott:** Listen up, kid. I don't mind delaying this transport for a few more days. Once the engines are repaired, our ship will leave immediately, and you won't have any reason to inspect the cargo.

> **斯科特：**听好了，小子。我不介意把时间再拖上几日，反正引擎一旦修好，运输船就会离港，你们也就没有理由再进行检查了。

**Yanqing:** If that's the case, I can assure you the Cloud Knights won't let your ship leave.

> **彦卿：**我向诸位保证，云骑不会放任这艘舰船离开。

**Skott:** Oh, really? Then you'll receive a ton of complaints from the Intelligentsia Guild and the IPC's Marketing Development Department. You're too young, I don't think you understand the chaos your rash decision will cause.

> **斯科特：**那么，到时候会有无数博识学会、公司「市场开拓部」的抗诉接踵而来。你还年轻，你根本不明白自己一个草率的决定会引起如何狼狈的轩然大波。

**Yanqing:** Since you put it that way, Mr. Skott, I'll take it as your agreement to the inspection procedure. Now...

> **彦卿：**斯科特先生把话说到了这份上，我就当你认同了检查手续。来人啊……

**Skott:** Not so fast, you little brat. According to the IPC regulations, I have the right to arm myself and protect the property on my transport ship.

> **斯科特：**小子，你最好别太冲动。公司的规章赋予了我武装捍卫运输财产的权力。

**Yanqing:** I thought you were reasonable and civilized, but now it seems the IPC just wants to play by its own rules. My apologies, Mr. Skott.

> **彦卿：**我原本以为各位是可以讲理的文明人，但看起来公司只想遵守自己的游戏规则。抱歉了，斯科特先生。

**Skott:** Stop him, guys! For the Amber Lord!

> **灵砂：**工造司发来文书，说有含有不明生物样本的货物滞留此间，需要丹鼎司派人勘验。左右我闲着也是闲着，就亲自来咯～

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Alchemy Commission (Location)　|　角色：Trailblazer; Sushang; Lingsha; Yanqing; Dan Heng; March 7th; Yunli; Feixiao; Jiaoqiu; Moze; Shehong; Yuluo　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''From Growls to Grace'' completed

### Follow Lingsha to the Alchemy Commission meeting

> *Under the mediation of the newly appointed Alchemy Commission's Cauldron Master, you all successfully fended off the accusatory blame from the IPC Specialist, after which you had a chat with her at the Alchemy Commission. But more speed bumps threatened this doomsday talk, for the swordswoman had been waiting long for a showdown with Yanqing.*

*(Talk to Yuluo, optional)*

**Shehong:** I'm Shehong, a messenger sent by Preceptor Taoran. He wanted to come and congratulate his kindred on becoming Cauldron Master personally, but health limitations hindered his visit.

**Shehong:** So, I'm here to pay my respects to the Cauldron Master on his behalf. Could you let her know about my arrival, dear chief alchemist?

> **玉络：**龙师大人的「薄礼」想来相当贵重吧。我一个小小的丹士长，可不敢代为转交，若是中间出了纰漏，我可担待不起啊。

> **无奈的医助：**但您放心，只要她看完诊，立刻就到您了。而且那位贵客说了，您此次诊费有她全部承担。

> **生气的患者：**那…那确实可以接受。你们这位「贵客」倒是个讲道理的好人，我也就不计较了。

**Lingsha:** All these years, and the view at the Alchemy Commission hasn't changed a bit.

> **灵砂：**这么多年过去，罗浮丹鼎司的景色依旧未曾变改。

**Lingsha:** The tides come and go, but the ancient sea remains the same. For us Vidyadhara, there's nothing more nostalgic than our homeland.

> **灵砂：**古海恒常，潮来潮去，对于我们持明而言，没有比这更值得怀念的故土了。

**Dan Heng:** ...You're a Luofu native, Miss Lingsha?

> **丹恒：**…灵砂小姐是罗浮本土人士？

**Lingsha:** Yes, I grew up here, listening to the sound of waves while researching prescriptions with my mentors and peers at the Alchemy Commission.

**Lingsha:** Well, once a seed is planted, no matter how long it takes, it'll eventually sprout and bear fruit.
