> **飞霄：**「大捷将军」？这个称呼未免也太自恋了吧。不成不成！

**Feixiao:** I heard there's a Dozing General on the Luofu, so I came up with a humble nickname for myself: The "lacking general" — lacking in worries, regrets, and rivals.

> **飞霄：**我听说罗浮有个「闭目将军」，所以我也给自己起了个同样谦逊的外号：「三无将军」——无虑，无悔，无敌。

**Feixiao:** Sounds much better, right?

> **飞霄：**这么称呼，是不是听起来好多了？

**Lingsha:** Yeah, that's a befitting title that sounds both humble and impressive.

> **灵砂：**确实是个谦逊低调且不失威风的好名头。

**Lingsha:** Now that the sparring session is over, Yanqing and Yunli, shouldn't you politely thank General Feixiao for her guidance and bury the hatchet?

> **灵砂：**彦卿、云璃，既然切磋结束，是不是该有礼貌地感谢飞霄将军的指点，然后安静地握手言和了？

**Yunli:** ...Here's your sword. Keep it safe, or it might get taken away again. By the way, we haven't settled the score yet. I'll defeat you fair and square next time we fight.

> **云璃：**…剑，还你，你可得把你的剑看好了，免得下次又被人夺去。还有，这次没分出胜负，下次我会让你输得心服口服的。

**Yanqing:** This is how she apologizes? Now that I finally got my sword back, I should report to the Seat of Divine Foresight. I'll take my leave, General Feixiao.

> **彦卿：**她这算是道歉的态度吗？拿回了剑，彦卿也该回神策府复命了。飞霄将军，彦卿先行告退。

**Yanqing:** Oh, by the way, Miss Lingsha, if you've got some free time, I'd like to invite you to the Seat of Divine Foresight for a chat with General Jing Yuan. I think there's more to those "personal grudges" you mentioned earlier...

> **彦卿：**对了，灵砂小姐。若有闲暇，彦卿想请您来神策府一叙，与我家将军开诚布公聊一聊。相信今日你所说的「恩怨」，其中一定另有隐情……

**Lingsha:** Thanks for stepping in, General Feixiao. Otherwise, I'd have had to knock them out with my incense.

> **灵砂：**多谢飞霄大人出手了。若是您再晚来些，我已准备点上迷香，麻翻他们两个了。

**Feixiao:** Not at all, just doing what you asked. How about we call it even as payment for the Healer Lady's consultation?

> **飞霄：**不必客气，你请托的事，我办到啦。就当是抵了衔药龙女的诊金如何？

**Lingsha:** Sorry, but even a general needs to pay their bills. We don't do credit here. And let's not forget, you'd have been waiting decades for a chance to see the Dragon Lady if it weren't for me.

> **灵砂：**抱歉，就算贵为将军，也要埋单付钱吧？何况，丹鼎司的医馆从来明码标价，概不赊欠。更何况问诊龙女大人的队伍早已排到了几十年后啦。

**Feixiao:** Well, you can always send the bill to the Seat of Divine Foresight and say it's for mentoring those kids. After all, it was quite the effort splitting them up, I nearly had to get tough. Now, if you'll excuse me, I need to find a spot and get some fresh air...

> **飞霄：**那你把账单寄到神策府上好了，就说是我指点那两个小家伙的教学费用。毕竟方才为了分开他们，我差点动了真格。眼下让我找个地方透透气……

A moment later, Feixiao leisurely walks to the ancient seaside shore to wait...

**Feixiao:** Back already? You've met with Jing Yuan and wandered around for a few hours. So, what do you think?

> **飞霄：**回来了？你们见过了景元，也到处逛了几个时辰，有何感想？

**Jiaoqiu:** It appears that the Divine Foresight is using this Wardance as a show of strength to convince everyone that the Luofu is prospering after the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis...

> **椒丘：**在我看来神策将军是想借演武仪典「示众以强」，证明罗浮在建木灾异之后的局势太平无恙，欣欣向荣……

**Feixiao:** But... I know you're going to say "But," right?

> **飞霄：**「不过」…接下来你要说「不过」了对吧？

**Jiaoqiu:** But, the influx of people attending the Wardance is like a breeding ground for disorder and rumors. One wrong move, and the Luofu could be in a world of chaos.

> **椒丘：**不过嘛，随演武仪典所涌入的人群可是不安和流言最大的传声筒。一着棋错，乱象迭起。

**Moze:** The Cloud Knights on the streets remain vigilant, so at the very least General Jing Yuan is aware of this. As for other matters, I'm unable to say.

> **貊泽：**街上的云骑颇为警醒，可见那位将军还是明白这层道理的。至于别的，我瞧不出来。

**Jiaoqiu:** I'd prefer to be excused from future meetings with generals. I'm just a military healer, and now all of a sudden I'm thrust onto the center stage having cordial chats with two generals?

> **椒丘：**以后有这等面见将军的好差事，你还是饶了我吧。我一个随军医士怎么就要被推到台前，和两位将军谈笑风生了呢？

**Moze:** My work doesn't lend itself to being in the limelight, either.

> **貊泽：**我的工作性质也不适合人前露面。

**Feixiao:** Just stop whining. At least you're in one piece, right?

> **飞霄：**别抱怨了，我看你们两个身上也没多几个窟窿眼嘛。

**Feixiao:** Before getting in touch with General Jing Yuan, I want to put aside my assumptions and see his "momentum." That includes the overall bearing of the Cloud Knights on the street, what people are saying, and how those close to him behave.

> **飞霄：**在接触之前，我想先摒弃成见，观对方「所成之势」再下判断。这所成之势嘛，自然就是街上云骑的风貌，人们的风评，还有与他亲近之人的行止。

**Jiaoqiu:** "The might of an army dwells not within its pawns, but within the force of its collective momentum. Recognizing this fact reveals the true measure of power." Thanks for enlightening me, General.

> **椒丘：**「军之强弱，非在其卒，实在其势。审势之成，乃明强弱。」将军所言，属下受益匪浅。

**Feixiao:** A perfectly clear statement turned confusing thanks to your translation — you've made me lose where I was now. Anyway, this is how I operate in battle, so you might as well get used to it.

> **飞霄：**好好一句大白话，让你一翻译，我自己都听不懂了。总之，这是我打仗时的习惯，你们也给我习惯习惯吧。

**Jiaoqiu:** Are you treating General Jing Yuan as your enemy?

> **椒丘：**您这是把景元将军当作敌人审视了吗？

**Feixiao:** The longest-serving general of the Xianzhou Luofu. Do you think he'd have only a few enemies?

> **飞霄：**罗浮仙舟治军最久的将军，他的敌人，还会少吗？

**Jiaoqiu:** By the way, General, you met the Healer Lady, yes? Could you show me the medicine she prescribed you?

> **椒丘：**对了，将军，你已经见过衔药龙女了吧？能否让我瞧瞧她开出的诊断处方？

**Feixiao:** Well, the Healer Lady couldn't do anything about my condition. She just told me to "enjoy some tasty food."

> **飞霄：**对于我的情况，龙女也无法可施，只是让我「吃点好的」。

**Jiaoqiu:** ...So, not even the famed Healer Lady could help?

> **椒丘：**…就算是名动一方的衔药龙女，也没法子么？

**Jiaoqiu:** Don't worry. I'll fulfill my promise and find a way to cure you. Actually, I've found some leads.

> **椒丘：**不必担心，我会完成当年的承诺，找到医好你的办法。眼下我已有了眉目。

**Feixiao:** Well, life and death, Jiaoqiu, it's all predetermined.

> **飞霄：**椒丘，生死之事，自有定数。

**Feixiao:** Upon starting my military career, I made a pledge that the rest of my life would be dedicated to being the Xianzhou's spearhead, hunting down the Abominations of Abundance till the end of my days.

> **飞霄：**自从军之日开始，我就立下誓愿，余生要成为仙舟的锋镝，射向丰饶孽物。

**Feixiao:** As long as I can fulfill that deep-seated desire... I don't care how long I live.

> **飞霄：**只要能完成这一夙愿，往后究竟能活多久…我都不在乎。

**Feixiao:** You asked if I view General Jing Yuan as my enemy... No, my real enemy has always been myself.

> **飞霄：**你刚刚说我将景元视作敌人…不，我的敌人从来只有自己。

**Moze:** "Enjoy some tasty food" So... what's for dinner tonight?

> **貊泽：**「吃点好的」，所以…晚上我们吃什么？

**Jiaoqiu:** Geez... you really know how to read the room, don't you?

> **椒丘：**你小子…到底会不会看气氛？

**Feixiao:** You guys figure it out for yourselves. I'm due to catch up with an old war friend I've not seen for quite a long time.

> **飞霄：**你们俩自个儿安排吧，我和一位多年没见的老战友有约了。

Meanwhile, Yanqing and (Trailblazer) return to the Exalting Sanctum.

**March 7th:** Hey, was that the Yaoqing General who just dropped in out of nowhere? She's so awesome!

> **三月七：**刚才那个从天而降的就是曜青的云骑将军吗？她真的好帅！

**March 7th:** I mean, when Yunli swung that massive sword, she just casually blocked her attack with ease...

> **三月七：**云璃挥舞这么大一把剑，她这么轻轻一击，就化解了云璃的攻势——

**Yanqing:** ...And mine, too.

> **彦卿：**…还有我的。

**March 7th:** Ahem, yeah... But to go up against Yunli is quite impressive, you know? That aura of heroism and grace. It almost makes me want to learn Xianzhou swordplay!

> **三月七：**咳咳…当然了！作为云璃的对手，那位舞剑的少侠也是英气逼人，身法灵动！看得我都想拜他为师，学习仙舟剑术了！

**March 7th:** You think so too, right, (Trailblazer)?

> **三月七：**你说是不是啊，开拓者？

- ▸ Yep, those moves were fabulous!

**Dan Heng:** I agree. General Feixiao is indeed impressive.

> **丹恒：**是啊，的确是位极有压迫感的将军。

**(Trailblazer):** Uh... I was talking about Yanqing, actually.

> **开拓者：**呃，我是说彦卿。

- ▸ Hmmm, that general is totally something else...

**Dan Heng:** I agree. General Feixiao is indeed impressive.

> **丹恒：**是啊，的确是位极有压迫感的将军。

**March 7th:** I was actually hoping you'd give Yanqing some praise!

> **三月七：**我其实希望你夸夸彦卿啊。

- ▸ Oh, yeah, Xianzhou swordplay is really good! Like really, really good!

**March 7th:** Can't you put a little more enthusiasm into it?

> **三月七：**给我更有感情一点！

**Yanqing:** Thanks for the kind words, Miss March. The Wardance is coming up, and I've been chosen to represent the Cloud Knights in the ceremony.

> **彦卿：**谢谢三月小姐的安慰。演武仪典召开在即，彦卿侥幸有资格能代表云骑军出战。

**Yanqing:** I've had my fair share of defeats lately, and even though I know there are always more skilled swordmasters out there, seeing General Feixiao's skills today has made me feel a bit uneasy again.

> **彦卿：**这些日子以来，我不知吃了多少次败仗，早就明白了「天外有天，人外有人」的道理。但今日见识了飞霄将军的身手，心中又开始惴惴不安了。

**Dan Heng:** Don't underestimate yourself. After all, generals won't fight in the ring during the Wardance. Just remember the state of mind you had when you single-handedly took on me and Blade, putting life and death aside. With that mindset, you can prevail against most challengers.

> **丹恒：**倒也不必妄自菲薄，毕竟演武仪典将军不会下场。只要牢记你当日以一人之力挑战我和刃时将生死置之度外的心境，便足以战胜大部分挑战者。

**Yanqing:** I see. Thanks for the advice, Master Dan Heng! By the way, now that today's events are over, General Jing Yuan wants to invite all of you to the Seat of Divine Foresight. He has something important to discuss.

> **彦卿：**我明白了，感谢丹恒老师的指点！今日之事算是告一段落，我家将军想请各位来神策府中小坐，说是有要事相商。

- ▸ Something important?

**March 7th:** I bet it's about how to deal with the generals from the Yaoqing and the Zhuming.

> **三月七：**想必是为了商讨如何应付曜青和朱明将军的到来。

**Yanqing:** *sigh* I really don't want to get caught up in grown-up games so soon... I just hope Generals Feixiao and Huaiyan can see the truth. We don't need any more chaos on the Luofu before the Wardance.

> **彦卿：**唉，彦卿不想这么早领略成年人勾心斗角的事情啊…只希望飞霄和怀炎二位将军能明察事实。演武仪典到来前，罗浮之上可不要再生波折了。

*(Mission completed)*

*(Unlock Achievement A Crossing of Blades and Hearts)*

## 017　By Words of Many　神锋有归，众议难违

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Exalting Sanctum　|　角色：Trailblazer; Huaiyan; Jing Yuan; Yanqing; March 7th; Dan Heng; Yunli; Yukong; Feixiao　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''The Quieting of Quillons'' completed

### Head to the Seat of Divine Foresight to see the general

> *In the Seat of Divine Foresight, Jing Yuan invited all of you to commune with General Huaiyan and scrutinize the reason of the Yaoqing and Zhuming generals' presences. At this moment, you realized that, in the midst of quelling the Ambrosial Arbor crisis, the Astral Express had already caught the attention of the Xianzhou Alliance. What surprised you even more was General Huaiyan's attitude in pressing the issue subtly.*

*(Idle quotes)*

**Huaiyan:** It's getting late...

**Jing Yuan:** Please, stay a little longer, Elder Huaiyan.

**Jing Yuan:** I've invited some guests from the Express.

*(Approach Jing Yuan and Huaiyan)*

**Jing Yuan:** Earlier at the Palace of Astrum, I introduced these guests from the Astral Express to you, Elder Huaiyan, but with all the people around, we only exchanged pleasantries. Now, I'd like to officially introduce them to you.

> **景元：**早些时候在司辰宫，晚辈已向炎老介绍过星穹列车的客人了。不过那时人多耳杂，只能寒暄些琐事。现在，我将三位正式引荐给炎老。

**Jing Yuan:** These three braved great dangers, accompanying me to perilous places, defeating the chief culprit Phantylia, and uncovering the conspiracies of the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus. If you wish to know more, please feel free to ask us.

> **景元：**这三位甘冒奇险，随景元出入死地，驱逐了首恶幻胧，也揭破药王秘传的阴谋。个中细节，炎老有什么想知道的，尽可以提问。

**Huaiyan:** Well, I skimmed through the reports about the Arbor's rebirth from the Master Diviner Fu Xuan. She's been summoned to the Yuque for questioning. There are a lot of doubts within the Alliance about this whole situation...

> **怀炎：**关于建木重生始末，太卜符玄呈交联盟的一系列报告老朽都看过了。她本人也为此受召前往玉阙接受问话。联盟内部对此事疑虑重重……

**Huaiyan:** ...But despite all that, I believe in you.

> **怀炎：**…但是，老朽信得过你。

**Huaiyan:** Since you joined the ranks, you have repeatedly achieved remarkable feats. After the High-Cloud Quintet each went their separate ways, despite the many criticisms within the Alliance, the Marshal still stood firm against the dissenting voices, and entrusted the Luofu to you.

> **怀炎：**你自入行伍起，屡建奇功。「云上五骁」各自隐没后，尽管联盟内对你多有非议，但元帅依然力排众议，将罗浮托付于你。

**Huaiyan:** Over the years, you've served the Alliance with loyalty and wisdom. You've taken down abominations in Thalassa, rescued the Xianzhou Yuque from a siege, and destroyed the demonic planet summoned by the Denizens of Abundance... I still remember those battles vividly.

> **怀炎：**这些年来，你为联盟竭忠尽智。挫败塔拉萨的孽物，解去玉阙仙舟之围，摧毁丰饶民所唤来的妖星…历次大战中的种种，老朽依旧记得。

**Huaiyan:** There are fools who doubt your loyalty. They're happy to see the Divine Foresight fail because it gives them some kind of sick satisfaction. They haven't achieved anything of their own, so they feed off the failures of others.

> **怀炎：**有些蠹虫怀疑你的忠诚，臆断你昏聩无智，他们乐见神策将军失策，是因为他们本性如此，身无建树，只渴望目睹他人的失败作为活下去的养料。

**Huaiyan:** But I've seen enough failures in my time. And I want to believe that your loyalty has never wavered.

> **怀炎：**但老朽见证过太多失败了，我更愿意去相信，相信你的忠诚从未改变。

**Dan Heng:** So, General Feixiao of the Yaoqing is the only one investigating the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis on behalf of the Alliance?

> **丹恒：**那么，这次受联盟派遣前来探问建木灾异始末的，只有曜青的飞霄将军了？

**Huaiyan:** No, no. I am, too.

> **怀炎：**怎么会，当然还有我。

**March 7th:** This old man's words... always catch me off guard.

> **三月七：**这位老爷子说话还真是…出人意表。

**Huaiyan:** The Marshal ordered me to come to the Xianzhou Luofu, but the document only says: "Attend the Wardance and listen to Feixiao's questioning."

> **怀炎：**元帅发出饬令，要我前往罗浮仙舟。但公文里也不过寥寥几个字：「观礼演武仪典，旁听飞霄问话」。

- ▸ What does that mean?

> ▸ 这话是什么意思？

**Huaiyan:** The Marshal is well aware of Jing Yuan's purpose in holding the ceremony and understands the situation he is facing. She mentioned it because she believes both issues are important.

> **怀炎：**对元帅而言，她很清楚景元召开仪典的用意，也知晓他所面临的局势。她这么提，显然是认为二者并重。

- ▸ Since it's the Marshal's orders, do we need to step aside?

> ▸ 元帅的命令？需要回避吗？

**Huaiyan:** No need for that. You're all important witnesses. The Marshal is well aware of Jing Yuan's purpose in holding the ceremony and understands the situation he is facing. She mentioned it because she believes both issues are important.

> **怀炎：**各位是证人，无妨。在我看来，元帅很清楚景元召开仪典的用意，也知晓他所面临的局势。她这么说，显然是认为二者并重。

**Jing Yuan:** Thanks for your kindness and sincerity, Elder Huaiyan, but is it appropriate... to tell everyone here about the Marshal's orders?

> **景元：**炎老襟怀朗照，晚辈铭感五内。但是，元帅交付的饬令，当众说开，这…合适吗？

**Huaiyan:** By introducing the Express' witnesses to me alone, aren't you aiming to discern the intentions behind both my actions and Feixiao's, and whether there's any discord between us? Well, since I'm being open and honest with you, I encourage you young folk to do the same.

> **怀炎：**你独自向我引介列车的证人，不正是想摸清我与飞霄各自的来意，以及我俩之间是否有所抵牾。老朽赤诚待人，年轻人也就别藏着掖着了。

**Huaiyan:** As for the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis, all I need to do is listen. General Feixiao will be the one asking the questions. To be honest, I'm more concerned about the timely start of the Wardance.

> **怀炎：**对建木灾异一事，老朽自始至终要做的只有旁听。真正要提出问题的，是飞霄将军。而我更关心的，则是演武仪典能否如期平安举行。

**Huaiyan:** Oh, by the way, I've prepared a gift for the Wardance.

> **怀炎：**说起来，老朽这次抵达罗浮，为演武仪典带来了一份礼物。

### Admire the sword

*(Investigate case)*

**Huaiyan:** Yes, it's this case right here.

> **怀炎：**对，就是这个匣子。

This case gleams with a muted luster, crafted from a material neither wood nor metal. Its surface is masterfully woven with metallic threads, outlining an intricate pattern of lotus flowers.

**Huaiyan:** There will be numerous contests and celebrations during the Wardance, and the main event will be the Ringmaster's Challenge.

> **怀炎：**演武仪典期间，大大小小比武与庆贺数不胜数。其中最重要的莫过于「守擂竞锋」。

**Huaiyan:** The host will dispatch a skilled warrior to take on challengers from all over the cosmos, showcasing the excellent martial arts of the Xianzhou Luofu. When you mentioned that the Astral Express would be attending the ceremony, I thought the high elder of the Luofu would be the ringmaster...

> **怀炎：**东道主要派出技艺高超的战士守擂，应对四方挑战，以彰显仙舟武德之盛。当老朽听你提及星穹列车会前来观礼，本以为本次守擂之人会是罗浮龙尊……

**Jing Yuan:** You humor me, Elder Huaiyan. The Healer Lady is just a young lady who knows nothing about martial arts. How can I assign her as the ringmaster?

> **景元：**炎老说笑了。衔药龙女年纪尚浅，又不通武事，怎能让她守擂？

**Huaiyan:** I'm no match to you when it comes to joking.

> **怀炎：**论说笑的本事，我还是不及你。

**March 7th:** What's this box for? Why don't you open it, General Huaiyan?

> **三月七：**这匣子是干什么用的？怀炎将军怎么不把它打开啊？

**Huaiyan:** This sword case is intended for the Wardance's award. It's empty now, but in a few days, a precious sword will be delivered and stored inside.

> **怀炎：**这是我为演武仪典的奖品准备的剑匣。其中暂时空空如也，但过不了几日，便有一柄宝剑即将抵达此处，收纳在其中。

**Huaiyan:** I don't mean to boast about our skills, but this sword represents the pinnacle of the Zhuming's craftsmanship. It has a legendary history, full of heroic tales from foreign lands, tales that are too detailed to be summarized in just a few words. Since the delegation delivering the sword hasn't arrived yet, I'll just leave the case here for now.

> **怀炎：**不是老朽自夸，此剑是朱明仙舟工造技艺的极致，曾流落异乡，创下种种英雄伟业。个中离奇，实难一语道尽。不过，送来宝剑的使团尚未驾临，我就先在这儿放个剑匣吧。

**Huaiyan:** I've been wondering... who would be worthy of such a sword? And then it hit me — I can award it to the champion of the Ringmaster's Challenge! The ceremony's champion is sure to be a perfect match for the sword.

> **怀炎：**老朽一直在想…到底什么样的人才能配得上这柄剑呢？思前想后，不如就趁这次守擂竞锋，将它当作奖品送出吧！竞锋胜者想必英雄过人，与宝剑正相匹配。

**Huaiyan:** Moreover, I hear that Yanqing is an excellent swordmaster, and that he will be representing the Luofu as the ringmaster, so it seems like a perfect gift for him.

> **怀炎：**而且我听说彦卿小兄弟剑术过人，他代表罗浮守擂，老朽觉得这件礼物真是再合适不过了。

**Jing Yuan:** Thank you for your generosity, Elder Huaiyan.

> **景元：**炎老有此美意，景元就在此谢过了。

**Yunli:** If you want to give me a sword, just say the word, Grandpa. No need to beat around the bush.

> **云璃：**爷爷，想送我剑就直说，何必绕这么大个弯子。

**Huaiyan:** You've got confidence, my girl, but I don't think you can best Yanqing.

> **怀炎：**丫头，你倒是自信。可我看你却未必能赢过彦卿呀。

> **彦卿：**云璃姑娘有爱剑之心，彦卿是知道的。只可惜剑总会选择与它相配的主人。

**Yunli:** Yeah, and even if someone gets their hands on such a precious sword, it'll probably end up in someone else's.

> **彦卿：**今日在丹鼎司的胜负还没分出，云璃小姐既然有兴趣，不如代表朱明仙舟上台一战？

**Yunli:** That's exactly what I had in mind! Nobody knows who's gonna come out on top! It could be me, could be someone else... It'll probably be me, but whatever happens... it won't be him!

> **云璃：**我正有此意！胜者花落谁家还不一定呢！也许是我，也许是别人，也许还是我，但总之…未必是他！

> **三月七：**我不想说的太失礼…但是，这场连续剧看了那么久，我还真想瞧瞧，他们俩到底谁胜谁负。

**Huaiyan:** Quiet down! We have other guests here! I've prepared this sword to add some excitement to the ceremony, not to have you two squabble. It's not a good look for the Alliance.

**Huaiyan:** While you both seem confident that you'll win, you need to remember there can only be one winner and one loser in the ring, which could lead to hard feelings. Actually, I have an idea...

**Huaiyan:** We don't know who the winner will be, and it might not be either of you, but if you're eyeing that prize, you'll need to work together. I want you to take on an apprentice who will take part in the Wardance and win at least one match.

> **怀炎：**虽说胜者尚未揭晓，也未必就是你二人，但若想得到这彩头，我要你们联手教出一名学徒，让他登上演武仪典，至少赢下一场胜利。

**Huaiyan:** I'd be greatly pleased if this apprentice could represent the Express in the Wardance by displaying their Cloud Knight flair and prowess.

> **怀炎：**若是此人能以云骑剑术代表列车参加演武仪典，大展身手，老朽也会大感快慰！

**Jing Yuan:** Well, Elder Huaiyan's idea is quite interesting. Imparting swordplay skills requires teamwork, and both the winner and the loser will learn a valuable lesson regardless of the outcome. The question is, whom should the two of them take as an apprentice?

> **景元：**炎老这想法着实有趣。传徒授艺，方显合作精神。如此一来，胜者有所斩获，败者也不至于空手而归。只是，他们二人该收谁为徒呢？

**March 7th:** Huh? Who? Wait, me...?

**March 7th:** ...Huh? Wait! Are you serious, General?

> **三月七：**谢谢怀炎将军的厚爱。云璃和彦卿原本是要决一胜负，如今却要转头教我学剑，岂不是太耽误两位练剑备战的时间了？

**March 7th:** Plus, I heard that each swordmaster has their own special moves. What if they let something slip while teaching me? If everyone knows each other's tactics, won't that make it hard to catch people off guard during the Wardance?

> **三月七：**何况，听说剑士们都各有绝招呀，要是教我的时候不小心泄漏，大家知根知底，打起来岂不是破不了招？

**Yunli:** That's considerate of you, March 7th. But don't worry. It'll take you at least a decade of hard training before you can even start learning special moves. No need to freak out – a few Zhuming swordplay tricks will mean you'll be more than equipped.

> **云璃：**三月七你倒是个体贴人。放心吧，不勤修苦练个十来年还教不到绝招呢。你别害怕，以朱明剑术的厉害，只要跟我学上几招，包你受用无穷。

**Huaiyan:** Heh, the whole point is to know each other's moves. Defeating your opponent in just one move? How boring would that be! Plus, what really decides a swordsman's fate isn't some "special move" — it's the solid fundamentals.

**Jing Yuan:** Yanqing, teaching an apprentice is also a way of honing your own skills and gaining insights. You've been an apprentice for years, it's about time you looked at swordplay from another perspective.

> **景元：**彦卿，传徒授艺的过程也是在审视自己所学，加深对技艺的理解。你做了多年弟子，是时候该换换眼界了。

**Yanqing:** I see, General. Then count me in!

> **丹恒：**三月，这件事归根结底要由你自己来决定。

### Talk to the others at the Seat of Divine Foresight

*(Talk to Yanqing)*

**March 7th:** Uh... I think we strayed off topic. How did things even get to this point?

> **三月七：**呃，讨论是不是跑题了？事情怎么会变成这样？

**Dan Heng:** Because... General Huaiyan wants us to stick around on the Luofu for some time.

**Dan Heng:** Since the Crew's actions were mentioned in the Luofu's operations log were given to the Alliance, he probably wants to see firsthand if we're as capable as the report claims, or if we're just some made-up excuse to save face.

> **丹恒：**罗浮上呈联盟的报告中既然出现了列车的行动记录，他一定想眼见为实，看看我们是否真有报告提及的那般能耐，而不是虚应故事找来的借口。

**Dan Heng:** And he wants to see it for himself during the Wardance, which is why he even dragged Yunli into this. What began as a simple contest between two swordmasters... has now evolved into you two collaborating to mentor March.

> **丹恒：**演武仪典就是他的试金石。为此，他甚至将云璃也一块儿拉下了水。原本只是两个年轻人之间的竞技赌斗…现在却成了你俩联手教导三月七。

> **景元：**炎老还是那个令所有人为之头疼的炎老啊。

> **景元：**我要在此致歉。我邀请诸位返航观礼，是存了向两位将军出示人证的心思，但没能坦诚相告，是景元的不是。

**Jing Yuan:** In the coming weeks, I'll also invite all of you to a meeting with General Feixiao, where you may need to answer her questions and clear up any doubts she might have. So, please be prepared for the meeting.

> **景元：**除去炎老之外，在之后的几周内，我还会邀请各位同飞霄将军见上一面，接受她的提问以解其疑惑，希望各位到时候有所准备。

- ▸ No worries. Answering a few questions is no big deal.

**Jing Yuan:** I know.

> **景元：**我知道。

In Aurum Alley, Feixiao meets with her old comrade-in-arms whom she hasn't seen for many years

**Yukong:** The illustrious Merlin's Claw waiting for me, and for so long, too? It's quite an honor. It's been a while, General Feixiao.

> **驭空：**能让大名鼎鼎的天击将军等我这么久，可算是赚足了面子。好久没见了，飞霄将军。

**Feixiao:** It's been thirty years since we last saw each other, right, Yukong?

> **飞霄：**驭空姐姐，自上次分别，咱们快有三十年没见了吧？

**Yukong:** Yes, back then, you were the vanguard of the Yaoqing's Verdant Knights, and I was a pilot of the Luofu's Rainbow Orbit Fleet. Who would have thought that upon meeting again, you'd be a general, and I'd have given up flying... Really does feel like a lifetime ago.

> **驭空：**是啊，三十年前，你是曜青「青丘军」的先锋，我是罗浮「垂虹卫」的飞行士。没想到，再次见面你已成了将军，而我已经放弃了飞翔…如此想来，真是恍如隔世啊。

**Yukong:** Well, I wouldn't say I haven't seen you in thirty years. After all, your great victories are announced through the Yellowbell Resonance System every day, so I'm well aware of your great feats... How's your health holding up?

> **驭空：**其实也不能说三十年没见，毕竟「黄钟」共鸣系统里天天播放着你大捷的战报，我也算是日日得见你所向披靡的英姿。…你的身体还好吗？

**Feixiao:** Still stable, I suppose. Do you still remember the medic who saved me in battle?

> **飞霄：**状况还算稳定。不知你还记不记得，当年在战阵中救起我的那位军医？

**Yukong:** That healer with the odd name and peculiar temperament... What was his name again? Was it Pichu? Or Kaqiu?...

> **驭空：**那个名字古怪、脾气也很古怪的医士…叫什么来着？我只记得他名字里沾个葱姜蒜什么的…

**Feixiao:** Jiaoqiu. He's been my retainer and personal healer, delegated by the Alchemy Commission from the Xianzhou Yaoqing. Over the years, he's dedicated himself to managing my "condition." It's thanks to him that I'm still in good health today.

> **飞霄：**椒丘。受曜青仙舟丹鼎司的派遣，他已是我的幕僚和随身医士。这些年他一直在想法子控制我的「病情」。这么多年过去了，我还活得好好的，他功不可没。

**Feixiao:** Given my background, I'm happy to have made it this far.

> **飞霄：**以我的出身，能一路走到今天，我很满足了。

**Yukong:** I'm relieved to know that you're safe and sound. Well then, since you and Elder Huaiyan are here, I imagine you must have received orders from the Marshal?

> **驭空：**知道你一切平安，我就放心了。那么，这次你和怀炎老先生前来，想必是领了元帅的命令吧？

**Yukong:** As your friend, may I ask how the Alliance intends to punish the General of the Luofu?

> **驭空：**飞霄，看在朋友的面子上，能告诉我，联盟打算如何降罪于罗浮的将军吗？

**Feixiao:** The Arbor's rebirth has frightened the elders who lurk behind the scenes... They fear the resurgence of abominations, much like what happened thirty years ago.

> **飞霄：**建木重生，让那些惯于躲在后方的老家伙们感到了害怕…他们怕孽物再度卷土重来，像三十年前那般。

**Feixiao:** Although the report from the Luofu explained all the details, we don't know if the Ruin Legion really invaded, or how exactly the Stellaron Hunters and the Astral Express became involved. This puzzle has many missing pieces.

> **飞霄：**虽然罗浮呈上报告解释了来龙去脉。但烬灭军团的入侵是否真的存在，星核猎手以及星穹列车到底如何介入此事，其中却有众多细节缺失。

**Feixiao:** As you know, the fugitive Jingliu, who mysteriously disappeared many years ago has resurfaced. This time, she has brought along an outworlder and a coffin, claiming to offer the Marshal "a method to fight against the Aeons."

> **飞霄：**想必你也知道，消失多年的逃犯镜流再度出现了。这次她带来一个化外民和一具棺材，自称向元帅献上「与神相争的法子」。

**Feixiao:** The Luofu Preceptor has also leveled accusations against Jing Yuan for neglecting the Alliance's principles. She asserts that Jing Yuan enabled the exiled Imbibitor Lunae to reenter the Luofu, thereby unlocking the Lunarescent Depths within Scalegorge Waterscape, which in turn disrupted the Vidyadhara's dutiful watch over the Ambrosial Arbor.

> **飞霄：**罗浮的龙师也状告景元不顾盟谊，说他放任流徙在外的饮月龙尊重返罗浮，打开鳞渊境古海，干扰持明守望建木的责任。

**Feixiao:** It is for these reasons that I have come here to the Luofu today.

> **飞霄：**这些都是我今天不得不踏入罗浮仙舟的原因。

**Feixiao:** Well, duty calls. Perhaps I shouldn't have mentioned all of this to an uninvolved person. But since we once fought together, I didn't want to keep you in the dark. Perhaps pretending you didn't hear any of this would be for the best.

> **飞霄：**职责所在，我本来不必向外人解释这么多。但你我曾并肩作战，我不打算瞒你，也希望这些话你过耳就忘，可以吗？

**Yukong:** I understand. I'm sorry, I was out of line. I know I shouldn't be defending General Jing Yuan right now... but, well, you know how I am.

> **驭空：**我明白了，抱歉，是我失仪了。我也知道自己现在不该多嘴为景元将军辩护…但，你也了解我的性格。

**Yukong:** The Luofu has enjoyed centuries of stability since the end of the Sedition of Imbibitor Lunae, much of which can be attributed to General Jing Yuan's masterful strategizing.

> **驭空：**罗浮自饮月之乱结束后，享有数百年的安定，这其中景元将军的擘画功不可没。

**Yukong:** Unfortunately, for long-life species, enduring through the ages always culminates in a failure that undoes all previous achievements — a moment that our adversaries relish.

> **驭空：**可惜对长生种而言，只要活得够久，就总会迎来摧毁往日累积一切的失败——敌人等待的就是那一刻。

**Feixiao:** That's true. And that's why I'm also here for another purpose — to visit Hoolay.

> **飞霄：**说的不错。所以这次我前来，还有另一个目的：探视「呼雷」。

**Yukong:** Hoolay? You mean that Hoolay? The borisin Warhead? The same Hoolay who has been imprisoned in the Shackling Prison for over seven centuries?

> **驭空：**呼雷？你是说那个呼雷？那个步离人战首？从七百多年前就被拘禁在罗浮幽囚狱中的呼雷？

**Feixiao:** "The nemesis of the foxians, who will never be forgiven and shall be imprisoned until the end of the cosmos..." I can't quite remember the exact wording, but yes, the very same Hoolay.

> **飞霄：**「狐人大敌」，「永世不赦」，「直至天地荒灭」之类的…我记不住那么多判词，就是那个家伙。

**Yukong:** Usually only emissaries from the Xianzhou Yaoqing's Sky-Faring Commission visit him once every century. Why do you have to visit him now, of all times...?

> **驭空：**但对呼雷的探视，从来是由曜青仙舟天舶司派出使节，百年一次。为何你要在这节骨眼上……

**Feixiao:** The foxians and the Alliance made a pact to combat the abominations, aiming to achieve justice and free their kin. That werewolf monster is to be forever imprisoned in the dark recesses of The Shackling Prison, facing unending retribution.

> **飞霄：**狐人与仙舟盟誓共讨孽物，除了解放同胞，求的还有正义：那只狼牙恶兽应当永远被关在幽囚狱里，日日受罚。

**Feixiao:** Given the situation on the Luofu, those on the Yaoqing are concerned about Hoolay's imprisonment. I'm afraid that the routine visit every century is no longer sufficient to ease their concerns.

> **飞霄：**以罗浮的状况，曜青内部颇为担心呼雷的关押情况。以往每隔百年一次的例行探视已经安不下老家伙们的心了。

**Feixiao:** That's why I was sent here — to reassure them.

> **飞霄：**我被派来安他们的心。

**Yukong:** It's all bad news.

> **驭空：**尽是些坏消息。

**Feixiao:** Well, not everything. There might be a silver lining. By the way, I've found some clues about the thing you asked for.

> **飞霄：**也不全是。至少，许多事情未必有你想的这么糟糕。你托我寻找的目标，已有了眉目。

**Yukong:** Tell me more!

> **驭空：**快讲讲！

**Feixiao:** The Verdant Knights followed the route you mentioned and discovered the wreckage of the Whistling Flames ship. Unfortunately, there were no survivors, and no cargo.

> **飞霄：**青丘军按照你提供的航路，找到了「鸣火」商会的舰船残骸。很遗憾，里面没有货物，也没有任何幸存者。

**Yukong:** ...

> **驭空：**……

**Feixiao:** However, someone had already been on the scene before we arrived.

> **飞霄：**不过，在我们找到失事地点之前，就已经有人到过现场了。

**Yukong:** Our people? Or someone from the IPC?

> **驭空：**是我们的人吗？还是公司的人？

**Feixiao:** No, neither. Yukong, have you heard of a person named Ruan Mei?

> **飞霄：**都不是。驭空，你知道阮•梅吗？

## 018　March to Mastery: A Star Is Born　授剑传行，腾然流星

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Central Starskiff Haven　|　角色：Trailblazer; March 7th; Dan Heng; Yanqing; Yunli; Jiaoqiu　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''By Words of Many'' completed

### Look for March 7th and Dan Heng

March's future prospects were boundless, a matter worth much celebration.*

> **彦卿：**我答应了怀炎将军，要让三月小姐学会云骑剑术，登上演武仪典，以及至少击败一位对手。我一定会竭尽所能施教，但要是你有违师长教训……

March 7th waves to you and Dan Heng, embarking on the first lesson of her sword training journey...

Switching to March 7th's POV...

With the encouragement of General Huaiyan — deliberate or accidental notwithstanding — March 7th seized the unexpected opportunity to learn the art of Xianzhou swordsmanship and to perform in the Wardance ceremony...

**Yunli:** Seriously? It's the first day, and you two are already late? Why is everyone on the Luofu so laid-back? So disappointing.

**March 7th:** Ah, Master Yunli you're already here. Sorry for keeping you waiting!

> **彦卿：**让你见识见识，这就是罗浮人授徒传艺的周到礼数！哪怕是弟子，我也会将她亲自接来。

> **彦卿：**作为东道主，我会传授三月小姐罗浮剑术的精华，然后让她在擂台上大获全胜。到时候，你就没法再对罗浮的剑法优劣评头论足了吧！

**Yunli:** Stand aside, rookie. Let me show you how we Zhuming swordmasters treat their apprentices.

> **云璃：**练剑需要身法步法配合，这套衣服剪裁贴身，便于活动。我还特意备了些小配饰，可是花了我不少心思。

**March 7th:** You're awesome, Master Yunli!

**Yanqing:** Miss March has quick hands and a flexible body. She's a perfect fit for practicing Luofu swordplay.

**Yunli:** Instead of focusing on her strengths, we should address her weaknesses. The drawback of wielding two swords is not generating enough force. What good is being quick on your feet if you don't have strength? It's not like we're dancing here.

> **云璃：**战阵强弱并不总靠长处，还要看短处。双剑的短处恰恰在于发力。双臂乏力，步法灵活又有什么用？这又不是跳舞。

**Yanqing:** Skilled swordmasters know how to play to their strengths and work on their weaknesses. Start with what you're good at, then tackle your weaknesses. That's the right way to learn.

> **彦卿：**但凡学有所成的剑士都懂得「扬长避短」。先发扬长处，再反哺短处，这才是正确的学习方式。

> **云璃：**哼哼，小少爷真不愧是空想理论派啊！

> **彦卿：**空想？你自诩能听懂剑说话，这又算什么？空想社交派？

**Yanqing & Yunli:** But it does matter!!! Just listen to me, March 7th!

> **三月七：**其中一位是之前咱们曾见过的彦卿小弟弟。另一位，则是朱明将军怀炎的徒孙女，云璃。

**March 7th:** Still, I didn't let the difficulty get to me. In just a few weeks, my sword skills have improved a lot. Both my masters think I have unique talents in swordplay and are literally fighting each other to teach me their skills.

> **三月七：**尽管如此，我却从未因艰辛而退缩。在短短几周的时间里，我的剑术突飞猛进。两位师父都夸我是世间少有的剑术奇才，争先恐后地用各自的方法教导我。

**March 7th:** Thanks to their guidance, I've actually made some progress. When I get back to the Express, I'll definitely show off my skills and impress you all!

**March 7th:** Looking forward to your reply. Yours, March 7th.

> **椒丘：**好比两位的剑法教学，若是顺着食材——我是说弟子的天性，以更适合的烹饪手法——我是说传授更适合她天性的技巧，才能令她事半功倍！

> **彦卿：**演武仪典是个彰显武德，结交四方盟友的好机会。悬剑于仪典之上，出鞘而不伤，展示的不仅是剑，也是云骑的武德威仪。

**Yanqing:** Do you see that airship in the distance? That's the Skysplitter, the venue for the Wardance ceremony.

> **彦卿：**你们看到了吗，远处那艘飞舰，就是这次演武仪典的比武赛场——「竞锋舰」。

**Jiaoqiu:** It doesn't look all that impressive from this distance.

> **椒丘：**远远看来，倒也不算特别。

**Yanqing:** The Skysplitter is actually a decommissioned Luofu military vessel. People aren't allowed on board until the Wardance officially commences.

> **彦卿：**这艘竞锋舰，是以罗浮退役舰船改造而成的。在演武仪典正式举行前，暂时还不准人们登陆。

**Yanqing:** Tomorrow, when the bell rings and the ceremonial cannons roar, I'll be there representing the Cloud Knights of the Xianzhou Luofu, standing in the ring, ready to take on challengers from all over the cosmos.

> **彦卿：**但明日钟声响起，礼炮绽放，彦卿将代表仙舟「罗浮」云骑军，站上擂台，接受四方骁勇之士的挑战。

**Yanqing:** Since I was a kid, I've been training in swordplay and the art of war under the General. Every day I'd swing my sword ten thousand times, and then thrust it ten thousand times, repeating the process over and over...

> **彦卿：**彦卿自小就被将军带在身边，教授剑术与兵法。每日挥剑斩击一万次，刺击一万次，如是往复，如是往复……

**Yanqing:** I understand that I'm not like other kids. I never envied the toys and freedom they all had. I never found sword practice boring or hard.

> **彦卿：**我明白，我和一般的孩子似乎不太一样。我从来没有羡慕过他们拥有的玩具和自由，也从没觉得一心练剑是什么枯燥、艰难的事情。

**Yanqing:** Even in the thick of battle, facing down savage abominations, I never felt scared.

> **彦卿：**即便是登上战场，斩阵杀敌，在与那些恶形恶状的孽物交锋时，我也一无所惧。

**Yanqing:** Each day, I could feel myself getting stronger and stronger... and I racked up countless victories. It's the best feeling in the world.

> **彦卿：**每天都能感觉自己在不断变强、变强、再变强一点…一次次将胜利握在手里，世界上没有比这更快乐的事情了。

**Yanqing:** But then, I faced a really tough opponent, and just one slash shattered my confidence into a thousand pieces. That's when I felt true fear for the first time. Maybe that's what Mr. Jiaoqiu meant by "life and death hinge on a singular moment, every idea fades into nothingness?"

> **彦卿：**但后来，我接了某人一剑。那一剑将我原本完满无缺的自信斩得粉碎。在那一剑到来的瞬间，我第一次感受到了害怕。这也许就是椒丘先生所说的「死生刹那，万念成空」吧。

**Yanqing:** After that, I had to pick up the pieces and try to put myself back together. But no matter how hard I tried, I couldn't seem to find my old, happy self again.

> **彦卿：**那之后，彦卿不得不低下头去，将这些碎片一片片拾起、拼合，试图重新拼出过去那个快乐的自己。但无论怎么做，我似乎都无法再现往日的心境了。

**Yanqing:** I often ask myself, why do I wield my sword? If defeat is inevitable, why do I continue to fight? Is it to reclaim the joy of victory, to meet the general's expectations, or to secure my honor among the Cloud Knights?

> **彦卿：**我时不时会问自己，我究竟为何而挥剑？如果注定要面对下一场失败，我又为何要继续挥剑？是为了找回胜利的快乐？为了回应将军的期待？还是为了留下云骑功勋？

**Yanqing:** While the general could teach me the art of swordplay, he couldn't teach me why I should keep on going. He said the reason must come from within myself. I've been struggling to find that reason.

> **彦卿：**将军能指教我剑术，却不能教我挥剑的理由。他对我说：挥剑的理由，必须由我自己寻得。为此，彦卿百般苦恼，辗转反侧。

**Yanqing:** But after talking with you, Mr. Jiaoqiu, I think I already have that reason in mind.

> **彦卿：**但与椒丘先生这番畅谈，彦卿心中已有了答案。

**Yanqing:** As a member of the Cloud Knights and the general's apprentice, I've had a weight on my shoulders, and I know there's still more to shoulder.

> **彦卿：**作为云骑的一员，将军的弟子，我背负了很多，而且注定要背负更多的东西。

**Yanqing:** But when I wield my sword, it feels like I'm letting go of everything. I love the feeling of giving it my all, facing any obstacle in front of me, pressing forward.

> **彦卿：**但只有在我挥剑时，我能感觉到自己可以放下一切。我喜欢那个倾尽全力向前方阻碍挥出一剑，一往无前的我。

**Yanqing:** That's why I wield my sword.

> **彦卿：**我也正是为了这样的我而挥剑。

**Jiaoqiu:** Oh, Yanqing, so young, yet so grown-up... By the way, how old are you exactly?

> **椒丘：**彦卿啊彦卿，小小年纪就一把年纪了…说起来你今年到底多大了？

**Yanqing:** Age doesn't really matter. All swordmasters will understand how I feel.

> **彦卿：**这和我几岁没关系。只要是练剑之人，自然会理解我的感受。

**Jiaoqiu:** Hmm... I get it. Looks like all the kids on the Luofu live tough lives. So, how about you, Miss Yunli?

> **椒丘：**唉…我明白，罗浮的孩子真是太苦了。那你有什么想说的，云璃小姐？

**Yunli:** It's not polite to ask a girl her age, no matter which Xianzhou ship you're on.

> **云璃：**无论在哪个仙舟上，问女孩子的年龄都是不礼貌的。

**Jiaoqiu:** I'm not asking your age! I'm asking if you have a dream like Yanqing has?

> **椒丘：**我问的不是年龄！我是问…你有没有彦卿兄弟这样的梦想？

**Yunli:** You don't talk like a cook, you sound more like a TV host or something.

> **云璃：**你不像个厨子，倒是更像主持人。

**Jiaoqiu:** ...Need I repeat myself again? I'm a healer.

> **椒丘：**…给我记好了，我是医士。

**Yunli:** Well, I... I don't have a dream like Yanqing does. The only reason I'm participating in the Ringmaster's Challenge is because I made a promise to my grandfather that I'd win the precious sword he's contributed to the Wardance.

> **云璃：**我…没有彦卿小弟这样的梦想。我之所以要挑战守擂竞锋，只不过是因为答应了爷爷，想赢下他赠给演武仪典的宝剑。

**Yanqing:** Sounds like that mind of yours is just filled with swords.

> **彦卿：**你这个人啊，满脑子都是剑。

**Yunli:** I bet you've got nothing better on your mind!

> **云璃：**你脑袋里不也一样没别的吗！

**Yunli:** My... father... was a craftsman on the Xianzhou Zhuming. Because of his foolishness, many innocent people fell victim to the cursed swords he forged.

> **云璃：**我…父亲是朱明仙舟的匠师，只因他的一念愚蠢，许多人死在了他所造的魔剑之下。

**Yunli:** Since I was a kid, it's been clear to me that not everyone deserves to have a weapon in their hands. Giving them a sword is no different than being cruel to the innocent.

> **云璃：**我从小时候起就明白，许多人的手中根本不配持有任何武器。准许他们握剑，就是对无辜之人的残忍。

**Yunli:** So, whenever I come across someone unworthy of a sword, I can't help but want to take it away from them. Given that Yanqing is the Wardance ringmaster, I'm stepping up to challenge him, to ensure the precious sword doesn't fall into the hands of an unworthy master.

> **云璃：**每当遇见有人德不配剑，便难免手心痒痒，想要从他手里夺下武器。这不是…彦卿小弟要为这次演武仪典守擂嘛。我好心上场，以免宝剑所托非人。

**Yanqing:** Hey! What do you mean by "an unworthy master"?

> **彦卿：**什么叫所托非人啊，你给我讲讲清楚！

**Jiaoqiu:** I see. It's not easy for kids on the Zhuming either.

> **椒丘：**唉，明白了，朱明的孩子也很苦。

**Jiaoqiu:** Well, it's better to have a reason for wielding a sword than to be lost and confused. I've saved countless Cloud Knights in my life, and there are plenty of exceptional warriors just like the two... of you...

> **椒丘：**有挥剑的理由，总强过茫然不知所措。我这一生救治过不少云骑，其中也不乏似二位这样出类拔萃的战士……

**Yanqing:** What happened, Mr. Jiaoqiu?

> **彦卿：**怎么话说到一半，椒丘先生？

**Jiaoqiu:** ...Oh, nothing. I was just reminded of some old friends and old tales.

> **椒丘：**…只是想起了几位故人，一些旧事。

**Jiaoqiu:** Judging from my professional perspective as a healer, both of you possess remarkable vitality, your energies flow like raging fires and mighty gales. The upcoming fight... will definitely be impressive.

> **椒丘：**以我身为医士的专业眼光来看，两位的生命力充沛健旺，气息流转如猛火烈风。这场比斗…一定好看的很。

**Jiaoqiu:** Well, we've seen the Skysplitter and toured the Stargazer Navalia. I guess it's time to say goodbye for now.

> **椒丘：**好了，迴星港兜兜转转一遍，「竞锋舰」也瞧过了。是时候要和各位暂时道别了。

**March 7th:** What, you're leaving already? But you haven't asked me about my dreams! I've been working hard too, you know!

> **三月七：**怎么，你要走了吗？你还没问我有没有梦想呢！我也练得很辛苦的！

**Jiaoqiu:** It's getting late, Miss March. Unlike you lot, I'm a grown-up bound by responsibilities and duties. The tasks entrusted to me by the general won't complete themselves.

> **椒丘：**时候不早了，三月小姐。我和你们几位不同，我只是个受职务羁绊的成年人，我家将军交给我的事情可不会自动完成。

**Jiaoqiu:** By the way, Yanqing, is it normal to have so many people wandering around in an automated area like the Stargazer Navalia?

> **椒丘：**对了，彦卿小兄弟，似「迴星港」这般自动运行的区域，平日里也会有咱们这么多人来闲逛吗？

**Yanqing:** ...Actually, this is a restricted area, but since you're all guests, I made an exception so you could take a look around.

> **彦卿：**……其实，这儿本是不允许随便擅闯的地方。只是大家是客人，我才带各位来此看看。

**Jiaoqiu:** I understand. Well, I'll take my leave. I wish you both the best of luck in the ring tomorrow.

> **椒丘：**我明白了。那鄙人就先告辞了，祝二位明日擂场，各得所愿。

Jiaoqiu waves and then leaves

**March 7th:** Seriously... I just spent so much time thinking about my dream, but he didn't even ask me!

> **三月七：**真是的…我想了半天自己的梦想是什么，他居然不问了！

**Yunli:** Now that we're done with our tour of the Skysplitter, shall we continue with our training?

> **云璃：**竞锋舰看完了，该回去继续训练了吧？

**Yanqing:** Why don't we take a day off?

> **彦卿：**今天就休息一天吧？

**Yunli:** What? You want to secretly practice swordplay by yourself? Dream on.

> **云璃：**怎么，你想躲起来一个人练剑？想得美。

**Yanqing:** Hmph, you know cramming before a fight never works out.

> **彦卿：**哼，临阵磨枪，一点儿用场也派不上。

**Yanqing:** For some reason, seeing the Skysplitter has boosted my confidence. So, I've decided to conserve my strength for tomorrow.

> **彦卿：**说来也怪，看到竞锋舰的轮廓，我突然有了信心。所以我打算养精蓄锐，等待明天。

**Yanqing:** Alright, I'll take you out of the Stargazer Navalia.

> **彦卿：**我带你们离开迴星港吧。

*(Begin sub-mission A Startlement of Fur and Feathers)*

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 019　A Startlement of Fur and Feathers　潜迹狼烽，兽骇鸟惊

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Stargazer Navalia　|　角色：March 7th; Yanqing; Yunli

### Leave Stargazer Navalia and return to the sword practicing area

> *Time goes by, and the moment of the Wardance has arrived once more. Right before the young swordmasters are about to step into the ring, they look up at the fighter jet in the sky and start expressing their opinions to the visitors from afar. But they are not the only ones focused on this moment, for in Stargazer Navalia, certain uninvited guests weave their schemes...*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Craftsman:** ...Oh, just shut up, Red Fang! This is not a beast ship, I need some time to take care of things

> **匠人：**…闭嘴吧赤牙，这里的船毕竟不是兽舰，我需要点时间搞定它。

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** You willingly donned the skin of a lowly beast to join this mission, dedicating yourself to our glorious cause. And now you're telling me you can't handle it? Do you realize how many ships we need?

> **云骑士卒：**你自愿披上了贱畜的皮，加入这次行动，为光荣的大业献身。现在你告诉我，你搞不定？你知道我们需要多少条船吗？

**Craftsman:** I'm doing my best, alright? It takes time to figure all this out.

> **匠人：**我在尽力，我在研究，这些都需要时间。

**Sky-Faring Commission Member:** When the guns go off tomorrow, all eyes will be on it. That'll be our only chance...

> **天舶司成员：**明天礼炮一响，所有人的注意都会被吸引，不会有第二次机会——

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** Harr-zaa-xi? W-who's there?!

> **云骑士卒：** Harr zaa xi？谁，谁在那儿？！

**Yanqing:** Who are you guys?

> **彦卿：**你们是谁？

**Craftsman:** An impromptu inspection. Why are there outsiders loitering in Stargazer Navalia? And a bunch of kids at that!

> **匠人：**临时检查。为什么有无关的人在迴星港出没？还是几个小不点。

**Sky-Faring Commission Member:** Hey, kids, didn't your parents ever tell you to stay away from the Stargazer Navalia? I know it's an automated facility, but it doesn't mean you can just break in and do what you want.

> **天舶司成员：**小朋友，你们的父母难道没告诉过你，迴星港虽然是自动运转的设施，但这并不意味着你们几个能随随便便闯进来。

- ▸ I'll take the little ones away right now.

> ▸ 我这就带弟弟妹妹们走。

**March 7th:** Ah, sorry, I'll take the little ones away right now.

> **三月七：**啊，对不住，我这就把小不点们带走。

- ▸ Who are you calling "kids?"

> ▸ 叫谁小朋友呐。

**March 7th:** First of all, I'm an adult. Second, I didn't just break in...

> **三月七：**首先，本小姐是成年人了。其次，我可不算随随便便闯进来的——

**Yanqing:** Yeah, we flew here on a starskiff! Like... woosh!

> **彦卿：**是啦，叔叔，咱们是坐星槎呼一下飞过来的喔！

**Sky-Faring Commission Member:** Well, I'm not trying to tell you off, but this place is off-limits to the public, you know?

> **天舶司成员：**叔叔们没有责问你们的意思。但是，这儿不对一般居民们开放。

**Yanqing:** Big sis! Let's go! I want to play in Ever-Hunt Plains!

> **彦卿：**姐姐，姐姐，咱们快走吧，接下来我要、我要去永狩原玩！

**March 7th:** Huh? Ever-Hunt Plains? Uh... yeah, sure! Big sis will take you there.

> **三月七：**唉？永狩原？呃…好，好的。姐姐带你去永狩原。

*(March 7th, Yanqing, and Yunli leave)*

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** Xuu haa... You should have let me...

> **云骑士卒：** Xuu haa…你应该让我——

**Sky-Faring Commission Member:** Shhh, the overhaul is done, and everything looks good. We should leave.

> **天舶司成员：**嘘，检修结束了，一切正常，我们该走了。

**Yunli:** Could you repeat what you just said, Yanqing?

> **云璃：**彦卿小弟，能不能把刚才的话再学一遍？

**Yanqing:** What did I say?

> **彦卿：**什么话？

**Yunli:** Big sis! Let's go! I want to play in Ever-Hunt Plains!

> **云璃：**姐姐，姐姐，咱们快走吧~

**Yanqing:** Come on. Can't you read the room? Something is definitely off about the three people we just met.

> **彦卿：**救命，你是永远读不懂气氛吗？刚才那三个人，很有问题。

**Yunli:** Yeah, anyone could see that. I just wanted to hear you say it again.

> **云璃：**傻瓜都看明白啦，我只是想再听你说一遍那个。

**Yunli:** That pink-haired fox tried to say something. I'm pretty sure he sensed something fishy. Since he's not familiar with this place, he just dropped us a hint, but you didn't seem to be paying attention at all.

> **云璃：**那只粉毛狐狸刚刚欲言又止，怕是早已瞧出了什么端倪。只是他人生地不熟，所以临走前了留了个话茬暗示咱们，没想到你却不搭理他。

**Yanqing:** I knew that from the beginning!

> **彦卿：**我早就听明白了！

- ▸ That Cloud Knight... didn't recognize Master Yanqing?

> ▸ 那个云骑…不认识彦卿师父？

**March 7th:** That Cloud Knight didn't recognize Master Yanqing at all... That's really weird.

> **三月七：**刚才那个云骑，完全认不出彦卿师父…这确实很蹊跷。

**Yunli:** What, is he famous on the Luofu or something? Not even the Cloud Knights on the Zhuming, who all know my glorious name, might recognize my face.

> **云璃：**怎么，他在罗浮很有名吗？朱明云骑军千千万万，就算听过我的大名，也不见得认得出我的脸。

**March 7th:** You have a point.

> **三月七：**您说的是呐。

- ▸ Looks like they were tampering with the starskiff production line, huh?

> ▸ 他们似乎在捣鼓星槎制造线？

**March 7th:** They said they were doing an overhaul, but it looked like they had no idea how to operate the starskiff production line, right?

> **三月七：**说是检修，但看起来他们似乎并不知道怎么摆弄那条星槎制造的流水线？

**Yunli:** And it's suspicious how they suddenly finished their "overhaul" and walked away as soon as they laid eyes on us.

> **云璃：**这几个人一见到咱们就立刻「检修完毕」，转身就走，实在是可疑。

**Yanqing:** A Cloud Knight, a member from the Sky-Faring Commission, and a craftsman... They're from various departments, and the reason for the overhaul seems legit, but one of them blurted out some weird language just now. Did you hear that?

> **彦卿：**云骑，天舶司的人，还有匠人…种类倒是齐全，检修的理由也说得过去，只是刚才那人嘴里迸出了一句奇怪的语言。你们听见了吗？

**Yanqing:** I have a feeling that if we secretly tail them, we'll definitely catch these guys in the act.

> **彦卿：**我有种奇怪的直觉，如果咱们现在悄悄跟上去，一定能掀开这几个家伙的马脚。

**Yanqing:** Follow my lead, and be careful not to blow our cover.

> **彦卿：**跟上我，小心别让他们发现了。

### Monitor the suspicious character

*(Approach the suspects, optional)*

**Yanqing:** Don't get too close, Miss March! They might spot you!

> **彦卿：**三月小姐，别靠太近了，会被发现的！

*(Approach the marked location)*

**March 7th:** Never mind who they are, let's just film them.

*(Take a photo of the suspects plotting at the scene)*

*(Open Multi-Conditional Photoshoot Tutorial)*

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** We should have just killed those lowly beasts.

> **云骑士卒：**应该宰了刚才那三只贱畜的。

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** Those little brats won't take up much space. There are boxes all over this place. Just dump them into one and no one will notice.

> **云骑士卒：**小崽子占不了多大地方。这儿到处都是箱子，随便往哪个里头一扔，不会有人发现的。

**Sky-Faring Commission Member:** Cut the theatrics, Grulok. Even the slightest slip-up could interfere with Lord Mok Tok's plans.

> **天舶司成员：**少给我节外生枝，伽虏。任何一点意外都会干扰末度大人的计划。

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** So, where are we heading next?

> **云骑士卒：**接下来去哪？

**Sky-Faring Commission Member:** To check the freight skiffs. We've got a lot of preparations to do. Also, don't forget to take those crates with you.

> **天舶司成员：**去查验货船，需要做的准备还有很多。还有，记得带走那几只箱子。

**Sky-Faring Commission Member:** Weapons, supplies... we've got to be well-prepared. Otherwise, we're screwed.

> **天舶司成员：**武器、补给…我们必须做好充足的准备。不然咱们的头颅就只能交代在这儿了。

**Yunli:** So, are they... smugglers? What exactly are they up to?

> **云璃：**这些人…是走私者吗？他们要做什么？

**Yanqing:** I have no clue, but they seem to be moving those crates. I've got an idea. We can hide inside the crates and follow them.

> **彦卿：**三月小姐，你要往哪儿去啊？跟着他们！

### Find a suitable box to hide in

*(Investigate crate)*

Taking advantage of the temporary absence of the group, you cautiously approach a crate and crawl inside.

**March 7th:** Ugh, it's so cramped in here...

**Yunli:** I can hardly breathe.

> **云璃：**要窒息了。

**Yanqing:** Just hang in there...

> **彦卿：**坚持住…

You hear footsteps pacing back and forth beside the crate. If someone were to open the crate at that moment, they would certainly catch you in your ridiculous hiding position. Fortunately, that doesn't happen.

The floating mechanism at the bottom of the crate is activated. You feel it begins to drift slowly forward.

After what feels like an entire Amber Era of holding breath in silence, you heard the sound of the crate touching down.

**Craftsman:** Let's just put the cargo here for now, alright? Then we'll move on to inspect the ships.

> **匠人：**货先放在这儿吧？接下来检查船只的问题。

## 020　Inquisition to Rectitude　诘辩无愧，折狱论非

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Seat of Divine Foresight　|　角色：Trailblazer; Dan Heng; Feixiao; Huaiyan; Jing Yuan; Jiaoqiu; Moze; Xueyi; Hanya; Mok Tok; Hoolay　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''March to Mastery: A Star Is Born'' completed

### Head to the Seat of Divine Foresight to meet Feixiao

> *Within the Seat of Divine Foresight, the Yaoqing's general Feixiao summons the Express crew to inquire about the whole story surrounding the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis. There's none of the terseness that one might expect. The general's objective upon arrival on the Luofu might not have been here in the first place — all she wants to ensure is that Jing Yuan's loyalty has not changed.*

*(Approach Jing Yuan, Feixiao, and Huaiyan)*

**Feixiao:** I am glad to finally meet you in person, guests from the Astral Express. I'm Feixiao, the general of the Xianzhou Yaoqing.

> **飞霄：**百闻不如一见，星穹列车的客人。我是曜青仙舟的将军，飞霄。

**Jing Yuan:** Let me introduce our guests to you. The one dressed in green...

> **景元：**我来为天击将军介绍一番。身着青衣的这位——

**Feixiao:** He's the reincarnation of Imbibitor Lunae, and the person behind him is the newest member of the Crew. I've heard a lot about you.

> **飞霄：**走前边这位是饮月君的隔世之身，跟在他后面的是列车组最近吸纳的新成员，二位鼎鼎大名，我这对耳朵听得可清楚了。

**Feixiao:** Outside the reports from the Luofu, the Sky-Faring Commission of the Yaoqing has also gathered plenty of information about both of you. I've been eager to meet you face to face, for reasons that... I'm sure General Jing Yuan has explained, right?

> **飞霄：**除开罗浮报告中的记述，「曜青」天舶司也搜罗了不少关于二位的见闻。本人一直想会会你们，原因…想必景元将军也解释过了吧？

- ▸ Yeah, we're here to answer your questions as witnesses.

**Feixiao:** That's right, but don't worry, this isn't a trial. I just want to have a chat with you and get a better understanding of the facts.

> **飞霄：**不错，但不是堂前会审。只是想请两位聊聊，廓清些事实。

- ▸ General Jing Yuan didn't tell us about your intentions at all...

**Feixiao:** He didn't? That doesn't sound like the Divine Foresight I know.

> **飞霄：**喔？这倒不像我认识的神策将军啊。

**Jing Yuan:** Don't worry. Just tell her the truth.

> **景元：**两位尽管据实回答，无需担心。

- ▸ You're here about the Ambrosial Arbor's resurrection, no?

**Feixiao:** Straight to the point. I like it.

> **飞霄：**正是。直来直往，我喜欢。

**Feixiao:** According to General Jing Yuan's report, the Ruin Legion is to blame for the Ambrosial Arbor crisis, and all Arbiter-Generals should pay attention to the Ruin Author's movements.

> **飞霄：**景元将军在呈递的报告中，将建木灾异的祸首归罪于烬灭军团，警示众天将应当关注烬灭祸祖的动向。

**Feixiao:** Over the years, the Destruction's minions have wreaked havoc on countless worlds, and the Alliance has been keeping an eye on them. But no one expected them to join hands with the remnants of the Abundance.

> **飞霄：**这些年来，「毁灭」的爪牙摧残了数之不尽的世界，联盟也有所防备，只是未曾想他们竟会与「丰饶」的残党联手。

**Feixiao:** The damage caused by the Ambrosial Arbor crisis was far less severe than expected, which is good news for us. However... it was quite different from the Ruin Legion's usual style of destroying life wherever they go.

> **飞霄：**这场建木灾异造成的损害远比预想中低，可以说是不幸中的万幸。但反过来看…也和烬灭军团所到之处荼毒生灵的风格大相径庭。

**Feixiao:** While I trust the bravery of the Divine Foresight and the Nameless, I'm curious about some details missing from the report. I'd like to take this chance to have an exchange with both of you.

> **飞霄：**我自然相信神策将军与各位无名客的英勇事迹，但也不免对报告未能详述的一些细节感到好奇。借此机会，希望能与二位交流一番。

**Feixiao:** Let me be clear, the questions I'll ask might not reflect my actual thoughts. So, please don't take offense if any of my questions seem a bit harsh.

> **飞霄：**有言在先，我所问的未必是我认为的。提问若有冒犯，还请不要见怪。

**Dan Heng:** Please go ahead, General. But keep in mind we can only answer based on what we know. And perhaps... you already have the answers to your questions in your heart.

> **丹恒：**将军但问无妨。只是我们能回答的，也只是我们所知的；而您所问的…或许内心也早有答案。

**Feixiao:** You have a clever tongue. I like it.

> **飞霄：**伶牙俐齿，我喜欢。

*(Negotiation starts)*

**(Trailblazer):** (The Merlin's Claw is quite articulate. Right now, her intentions are unknown, and Jing Yuan wants us to be honest... Maybe I'll just stick to the facts we know?)

*Opponent information:*

**Feixiao:**

The Cloud Knight General from the Xianzhou Yaoqing. She came to "observe" the Wardance under the orders of the Marshal. However, the true purpose of her trip is to observe the situation on the Luofu after the Ambrosial Arbor crisis. The first impression of this general is that she is straightforward and stern. Therefore, trying to deceive her is probably not a good idea.

*Negotiation strategies:*

*(Emotional Trigger)*

You silently fix your gaze on the Merlin's Claw, a trace of a provocative smile playing at the corners of your mouth.

Feixiao offers a slight smile, displaying no anger.

*(Smoke and Mirrors)*

You've decided to withhold your opinion on this question.

Feixiao offers a slight smile, displaying no anger.

*(Feigned Retreat)*

**(Trailblazer):** Could you please give us another chance? I'd like to answer that question again.

> **开拓者：**（这天击将军说起话来竟也头头是道。眼下她来意不明，景元又希望我们据实回答…也许只说知道的事实就好？）

**Feixiao:** No problem, but remember that a debate is like a duel. The more you hesitate, the farther you are from victory.

*(Round 1)*

**Feixiao:** Let's cut to the chase. Before the crisis struck, the Astral Express was guided here by a Stellaron Hunter, a wanted felon, in an attempt to resolve the Stellaron Crisis.

> **飞霄：**我就开门见山了：起初，星穹列车受通缉重犯星核猎手的引导前来此地，试图解决星核危机。

**Feixiao:** However, everyone in the Cosmos knows of the Stellaron Hunters' reputation. So, why did you place so much trust in them? Could it be that some of you have a connection with them?

> **飞霄：**可凡在银河行走的，是个人都应听说过星核猎手的昭彰恶名。各位无名客为何如此相信他们的一面之词，莫非是有人沾亲带故？

- ▸ It all comes down to the Stellaron.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Apart from the Luofu, there are many other worlds suffering from Stellaron corrosion. For example, Jarilo-VI, the world that the Express stopped at before reaching the Luofu, was one of them.

> **开拓者：**除罗浮外，宇宙中还有不少遭受星核侵蚀的世界。列车之前停靠的雅利洛-VI就是其中之一。

**Dan Heng:** To the Express, Stellarons act as roadblocks on the silver rail and pose risks to the warping process. And that's why dealing with Stellaron issues is part of the duty of the Nameless.

> **丹恒：**对于列车而言，星核的存在会阻塞银轨，加剧跃迁风险。因此处理星核引起的麻烦，也是无名客的分内之事。

**Feixiao:** Ah, I've heard about those problems caused by Stellarons. The Express connects various worlds, so it makes sense for you to take care of this. The Cosmos is a mess, and the Trailblazers are just doing their best to fix it. I understand.

> **飞霄：**唔，星核造成的那些问题我也有所耳闻，列车联结诸界，理应对此做出应对。银河破破烂烂，「开拓」缝缝补补。言之成理。

- ▸ We are the good guys.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** What's wrong with doing something good? Along the way, we've helped countless worlds... well, not literally countless, but you get the idea!

> **开拓者：**难道列车就不能做些善事？我们这一路上帮助过的世界都快数…倒也不至于数不清，但总之，有很多很多！

**(Trailblazer):** The Stellaron Hunters warned us that if we didn't hurry to the Xianzhou Luofu, half of its inhabitants would die.

> **开拓者：**星核猎手警告我们，如果不前往仙舟，至少会有一半罗浮住民死于非命。

**Feixiao:** Your good intentions are commendable, just like the Cloud Knights' determination to eliminate those abominations. Even though there are always people who remain skeptical, I won't question your motives.

> **飞霄：**有心行善，值得赞赏。云骑巡猎讨魔，也秉持向善之心。虽然总有人不明事理，多加非议，但我不会质疑各位无名客的动机。

- ▸ Raise her suspicion about your relationship with Stellaron Hunters.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** You know what? Maybe I do have a connection with Stellaron Hunters...

> **开拓者：**你说的不错，也许我确实和星核猎手有所关联……

**Feixiao:** Oh...? Now that's interesting.

> **飞霄：**哦…这倒是有趣。

**Feixiao:** Looks like you're quite close to the Stellaron Hunters. However, I must warn you that they are wanted felons. While I appreciate your honesty, being honest about this topic might make you look suspicious to some, you know?

> **飞霄：**看来你和星核猎手关系匪浅。但这群通缉犯形迹可疑，你这样诚实自白，我倒是欣赏，只是你得明白，这可是在往自己身上揽嫌疑喔？

- ▸ Talk about Elio's prophecy.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Rumor has it that Elio, the leader of the Stellaron Hunters, possesses the power to see into the future.

> **开拓者：**据传，星核猎手的首领艾利欧拥有目视未来众多可能性的力量。

**(Trailblazer):** He foresaw that the Xianzhou and the Express would have important roles to play in the war against Nanook. That's why we were lured here to the Luofu, to deal with the Stellaron crisis and fulfill the prophecy.

> **开拓者：**他预见仙舟和列车都将参与对抗纳努克的战争。而将我们引来解决罗浮的星核危机，正是为了实现这一预言。

**Feixiao:** General Jing Yuan believes in this prophecy too, as mentioned in the report. I'm curious why you didn't question it at all?

> **飞霄：**从报告来看，景元将军也相信了这一说辞。这倒令我好奇，为何你们对此并无质疑？

**Feixiao:** Could it be because one of the Stellaron Hunters is actually an old acquaintance of General Jing Yuan?

> **飞霄：**难道是因为猎手中还有一位将军的旧相识？

**Jing Yuan:** ...

> **景元：**……

**Huaiyan:** Please be cautious with your words, Merlin's Claw. Let's avoid sowing doubt among our comrades. That Stellaron Hunter used to be my disciple, so are you putting my loyalty in question, too?

> **怀炎：**天击将军请慎言。对无名客的问话不应变成同僚间的猜疑。那名星核猎手也曾是老朽的弟子，你是否也要考验我的忠诚？

**Feixiao:** I'm simply bringing up the doubts about General Jing Yuan that exist within the Alliance. Since I'm representing them, perhaps you can just imagine me as one of those old geezers?

> **飞霄：**炎老，我说的这些正是联盟内部对景元将军的非议。只是今日他们借我之口问了出来，要不您把我的样子想像成那几个老家伙的嘴脸？

*(Round 2)*

**Feixiao:** Let's move on to the next question. The report suggests that Dan Shu, the Master of the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus, colluded with the Lord Ravager and used the power of the Stellaron to resurrect the Ambrosial Arbor.

> **飞霄：**接着，报告提及「药王秘传」魁首丹枢心怀不轨，与绝灭大君勾结，动用星核复苏了建木。

**Feixiao:** But here's the thing: Dan Shu was just a chief alchemist. Even if she colluded with our enemies and summoned the Stellaron, how did she manage to bypass the Vidyadhara guards around the Ambrosial Arbor?

> **飞霄：**可丹枢不过一介丹士长，竟如此手眼通天。她勾结外敌，召来星核也就罢了，古海建木可是持明一族的领地，她是如何绕过守卫的？

- ▸ The Preceptors of the Luofu seem suspicious.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** The answer seems rather simple, doesn't it? The Preceptors of the Luofu abandoned their duty to guard the Ambrosial Arbor and colluded with the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus.

> **开拓者：**还能为什么？显然是罗浮龙师监守自盗，抛弃了守望建木的责任，与药王秘传勾结。

**Feixiao:** That's a possibility, but there's another scenario to consider: General Jing Yuan brought back Mr. Dan Heng, who had been in exile for a long time. As a result, Dan Heng parted the Seas, eventually leading to the resurrection of the Ambrosial Arbor.

> **飞霄：**这倒也不是没可能。但为何不是景元将军将流放许久的丹恒先生带回了罗浮，丹恒分开古海，才致使建木失控重生。

**Dan Heng:** I never intended to trespass on the Xianzhou ship, but...

> **丹恒：**我原本没有擅入仙舟的打算，只是……

**Feixiao:** If you had no intention to trespass, then why did you return to the Xianzhou ship before your exile was lifted?

> **飞霄：**你既然没有擅入仙舟的打算，那为何在流放令终止前却重返仙舟？

**Dan Heng:** I was concerned about the safety of my companions, so I had to act brazenly. I alone bear the blame.

> **丹恒：**是我担心伙伴的安危，不得不擅自行动。相关罪责，应当由丹恒一人承担。

**Feixiao:** Please don't worry, Mr. Dan Heng. If someone must be held accountable, it won't fall solely on your shoulders.

> **飞霄：**丹恒先生不必紧张，若是真有罪责，这也不是你一人能担起的。

- ▸ I've personally met Dan Shu.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** I personally met Dan Shu once. Her closest friend was killed during the war on the Fanghu, and she harbored deep hatred towards The Hunt. So she spent years making preparations in the Alchemy Commission, in order to take revenge on the Xianzhou.

> **开拓者：**我和丹枢有过一面之缘。她的挚友死于方壶的战争，她憎恨「巡猎」…在丹鼎司中准备多年，正是为了向仙舟复仇。

**Feixiao:** Revenge... is also a form of The Hunt. However, that doesn't explain how she managed to bring the Stellaron into the Scalegorge Waterscape, which was guarded by the Vidyadhara.

> **飞霄：**复仇…这同样是一种「巡猎」。但这不意味着她就有能力将星核带入持明把守的鳞渊境。

**Dan Heng:** Well, you should ask Dan Shu herself for the answer.

> **丹恒：**这个问题应该由丹枢自己回答。

**Feixiao:** Unfortunately, Dan Shu is dead, and even her corpse has crumbled into ash. That's one less clue we can pursue.

> **飞霄：**可惜丹枢已死，就连尸骸也碎成了一捧飞灰。可追究的线索又少了一个。

- ▸ The exile of Imbibitor Lunae is what led to this calamity.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** If the Luofu hadn't exiled Dan Heng, leaving the Vidyadhara with no leader, they wouldn't have fallen into chaos, and there wouldn't have been the opportunity to bring the Stellaron to the Roots of the Arbor unnoticed.

> **开拓者：**要不是罗浮放逐了丹恒，持明族群龙无首，化作一盘散沙，星核又怎会被神不知鬼不觉地放入建木玄根中？

**Feixiao:** Imbibitor Lunae was exiled due to his own crimes. Now, Dan Heng, as his reincarnation, is free from his sins. However, why did he risk trespassing on the Xianzhou Luofu before his exile was lifted?

> **飞霄：**饮月君被放逐，是他自取其咎。丹恒作为其转世之身，往罪早已一笔勾销。他为何要在流放令尚未解除时就冒险登上罗浮？

**Dan Heng:** I was concerned about the safety of my companions, so I acted brazenly. I admit I was reckless at that time, but...

> **丹恒：**我担心伙伴安危，擅自行动，确有不是。但……

**Feixiao:** But you returned despite everything. Now let's consider the outcome: The Arbor was resurrected, and the Luofu required a high elder to repair the seal and suppress the plaguemark. It's hard not to think about the stakes involved, right?

- ▸ Refuse to answer anything.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** I don't believe we are in a position to answer this question. We Nameless came here to resolve the Stellaron crisis, and we cannot be expected to know the details of Dan Shu's conspiracy.

**Feixiao:** You make a valid point. That's how innocent people should react. Let's skip this question for now.

> **飞霄：**不妨事，但也要记得，辩驳亦如战场，越是犹豫，便越是远离胜利。

*(Round 3)*

**Feixiao:** According to the report, Lord Ravager Phantylia is the mastermind behind the entire conspiracy.

> **飞霄：**报告宣称，绝灭大君幻胧是阴谋的策动者。

**Feixiao:** She disguised herself as an amicassador of the Sky-Faring Commission and traveled with you, only to vanish without a trace later on. It seems too convenient to label her as a scapegoat.

> **飞霄：**在与你们一行同路时，她以天舶司某位接渡使的身份示人，而后又蒸发得无影无踪。这祸首倒是来去自由，拿来戴罪背锅也忒方便了。

- ▸ Fu Xuan, the Master Diviner, witnessed everything firsthand.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Back then, we were working with Diviner Fu, and she also witnessed Phantylia's true form. You're not going to doubt one of your own people, are you?

> **开拓者：**我们与罗浮的符太卜一起行动。她也见证了幻胧露出真面目。你该不会连自己人也不信吧？

**Feixiao:** Of course not, we can trust the testimony of Master Diviner Fu Xuan. However... even if she saw it with her own eyes, we can't completely rule out the possibility that she might have been deceived. After all, it's suspicious how the culprit just bizarrely vanished.

> **飞霄：**太卜符玄是罗浮重臣，她的证词自然可以信任。但…即便是本人亲眼所见依然有被蒙蔽的可能。祸首来去自由，又离奇蒸发，当然让人心生疑惑。

- ▸ Phantylia is a heliobus.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** Phantylia is one of the heliobi, the energy lifeforms that once fought against the Xianzhou. They're known for their unpredictable and elusive nature.

> **开拓者：**幻胧是曾和仙舟为敌的能量生命，「岁阳」的一员。这东西变化无常，难以捉摸。

**Huaiyan:** Just as (Trailblazer) said, when the Ambrosial Arbor resurrected, its roots broke through the Creation Furnace on the Luofu, accidentally releasing the heliobus fiendfire sealed inside. This can be used as circumstantial evidence.

> **怀炎：**开拓者小朋友说的没错。这次建木重生，根须蔓延，还凿穿了罗浮的造化洪炉，误走了其中所封印的岁阳妖火。此事倒是可以作为旁证。

**Feixiao:** If Elder Huaiyan accepts the explanation, so do I.

> **飞霄：**炎老认可，我自然没有问题。

- ▸ Phantylia was eager to obtain the power of Ambrosial Arbor.

(+1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** I was there, fighting Phantylia alongside General Jing Yuan, but she absorbed the power of the Ambrosial Arbor and gained an almost indestructible physical form. Perhaps that was her intention all along.

> **开拓者：**我跟随景元将军一同与幻胧交锋。她汲取了建木的力量，生成了一具难以消灭的肉体。也许打从一开始，她就是为此而来。

**Feixiao:** So, a pawn of the Destruction wanted a flesh-and-blood body to live in? Hmm, interesting. That's quite a new perspective.

> **飞霄：**毁灭的卒子渴望一具肉身活下去？这倒是个未曾考虑的角度。有意思。

- ▸ Refuse to answer anything.

(-1 morale)

**(Trailblazer):** I'm not sure how to answer that question.

> **开拓者：**我不知道该怎么回答将军的问题。

**Dan Heng:** The Lord Ravager is a master at manipulating situations and sowing discord among her enemies. General Feixiao, given your suspicions of the internal affairs on the Luofu, I wouldn't be surprised if...

> **丹恒：**这名绝灭大君擅长操弄局势，布计令对手自灭。如今飞霄将军对罗浮内部的怀疑，恐怕也是——

**Feixiao:** So, I'm not even allowed to ask questions? Asking questions is considered sowing discord now? Well, that's convenient for you, just like Phantylia vanishing without a trace.

> **飞霄：**不妨事，但也要记得，辩驳亦如战场，越是犹豫，便越是远离胜利。

*(If negotiation fails)*

**Feixiao:** Well, it appears that you are still unable to give explanations for several details about how the Nameless got involved in the Ambrosial Arbor crisis.

> **飞霄：**很好，从各位的回答来看，这些问题都能有所解释。

*(If negotiation is successful)*

**Feixiao:** Well, it seems that your answers have addressed all my questions.

> **飞霄：**看来关于无名客如何涉入这场建木灾异的细节…各位仍有一些无法自圆其说的地方啊。

**Feixiao:** Generals, I am finished with my questioning.

> **飞霄：**两位将军，我的问话就到此为止了。

**Huaiyan:** So, what do you think, General Feixiao? Have the doubts in the report been cleared up?

> **怀炎：**那么，飞霄将军觉得如何？报告中的诸多疑点，是否得到了解释？

**Feixiao:** The two Nameless have been honest in their answers. Even though there are some tricky details, my intuition tells me there is nothing wrong.

> **飞霄：**两位无名客的回答倒是颇为坦诚，就算其中有些难以解释的细节，但以我的直觉看来，倒也没什么不妥。

**Feixiao:** However, the three questions I posed earlier were not just for the Nameless, but for you too, General Jing Yuan.

> **飞霄：**不过我方才所提到的三个问题，不仅仅是在向这两位无名客发问，也是在向景元将军传递某种声音——

**Feixiao:** First, the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus grew uninterrupted on the Luofu, yet the Six Charioteers were not aware of it. That was a dereliction of duty.

> **飞霄：**其一，药王秘传在罗浮内部不断壮大，六御却无所察觉，任其滋长，是为「失职」。

**Feixiao:** Second, you believed in the Stellaron Hunters' prophecy and entrusted outsiders to solve the crisis, even granting them access to the plaguemark. That was a dereliction of responsibility.

> **飞霄：**其二，对星核猎手的说辞信之不疑，又将解决危机的重责交托外人，任其触及寿瘟祸迹，是为「失责」。

**Feixiao:** Third, you insist on holding the Wardance right after the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis, putting the Luofu back in the spotlight. That is a dereliction of wisdom.

> **飞霄：**其三，于建木灾异之后，一意举行演武仪典，将罗浮再度置于寰宇焦点，是为「失智」。

**Huaiyan:** Merlin's Claw, is this your line of thinking... or the Ten Lords'?

> **怀炎：**天击将军，这是你的意思…还是「十王」的意思？

**Feixiao:** From the moment I walked in, I made it clear that the questions I'd ask might not reflect my actual thoughts.

> **飞霄：**我想，打从进殿起我就说了，我所问的未必是我认为的。

**Jing Yuan:** ...The Disciples of Sanctus Medicus were deeply rooted and had been plotting for a long time. I admit it was my negligence for not noticing it earlier.

> **景元：**…药王秘传的势力盘根错节，潜谋已久。景元失察，确有疏失。

**Jing Yuan:** As for the Stellaron Hunters' prophecy, I didn't believe all of it, but in the end, the Luofu did survive the Ambrosial Arbor crisis. So, I think it's safe to say that Elio's prophecy about the future holds some merit.

> **景元：**星核猎手的预言，我倒也未必全信。但在见招拆招之间，罗浮已从建木灾异中得以保全，可见艾利欧对未来的预言，亦有可观之处。

**Jing Yuan:** And as for the Wardance... Do you think I'm unaware of the risks? However, risks can also be opportunities. The Luofu has lain low for too long. I believe it's time to stir up the dregs hidden in the depths and wash them away once and for all.

> **景元：**至于演武仪典…景元岂会不知道开门揖盗的风险？不过风险亦是转机，罗浮这潭池水沉寂久了，也是时候该搅和搅和，让沉渣泛起，激浊扬清了。

**Feixiao:** Just as I expected from our sophisticated Divine Foresight. You have a way with words. I like it.

> **飞霄：**神策将军不愧是文化人，几句话赶得上一本仙舟成语大全了，我喜欢。

**Feixiao:** But unfortunately, ever since the report was submitted, the Alliance has been filled with rumors and speculation. Even within the Luofu, there are people accusing you of neglecting your duties, resulting in the Ambrosial Arbor's resurrection.

> **飞霄：**但很遗憾，自报告上呈之日起，联盟内就充斥着流言和臆测。就连罗浮内部也有人参本上奏，指责将军疏怠职守，放任建木重生。

**Jing Yuan:** So, what are your thoughts on all of this, General Feixiao?

> **景元：**那么，飞霄将军又是怎么想的？

**Feixiao:** As a fellow Arbiter-General, I fully understand the difficulties of this position.

> **飞霄：**你我同为天将，自然都清楚坐这把交椅的难处。

**Feixiao:** Personally, I think all these rumors are meaningless drivel. Across the sea of stars, the Divine Foresight knows better than anyone else what happened on the Luofu and the meaning behind it...

> **飞霄：**在我看来，这些统统是蚊蝇无意义的嗡鸣。相隔星海，罗浮上所发生的事，唯有神策将军最清楚其危机和背后隐含的意义……

**Feixiao:** ...Just as what happened on the Xianzhou Yaoqing recently.

> **飞霄：**…正如曜青仙舟最近所遭遇的情况。

**Jing Yuan:** You mean, the Xianzhou Yaoqing is also...?

> **景元：**你是说，曜青仙舟也…？

**Feixiao:** The scouts of the Verdant Knights have sent back reports that borisin are making trouble again. The borisin packs that were once divided and scattered have started swallowing each other up, forming larger and larger packs. Moreover, there's an entity named "Mangas" behind it all.

> **飞霄：**青丘军的斥候回报，步离人又开始蠢蠢欲动了。原本一盘散沙的步离猎群，开始彼此攻伐，互相吞并，结成更大的猎群。在他们的背后，有个叫「蟒古思」的东西在指引他们。

**Huaiyan:** An "entity"?

> **怀炎：**东西？

**Feixiao:** According to the report, this entity... isn't actually a borisin. It's a woman claiming to be the Messenger of the Master of Immortality. She's described as having "twelve faces and twelve pairs of fangs, as cruel as poison and as elusive as quicksand." The borisin believe she'll give them a chance to rise again.

> **飞霄：**斥候送来的情报说，那东西…不是步离人。那是一个自诩「长生主使者」的女人，「十二重面目，十二对獠牙，残酷如猛毒，变化如流沙」。步离人相信她将为他们带来重新崛起的机会。

**Jing Yuan:** That's Phantylia.

> **景元：**是幻胧。

**Feixiao:** That's right.

> **飞霄：**不错。

**Feixiao:** You're lucky that I'm the one who came this time. If it were the Patina Justice or the Seer Strategist, this conversation might not be so friendly.

> **飞霄：**幸好此行是我前来，若是换做尘冥将军或是戎韬将军，这场问话或许就不会这么友善了。

**Feixiao:** I've always had faith in my instincts, so I don't doubt your good intentions. But the Alliance has its fair share of questions and doubts about the Luofu. So, my plan is to come up with an acceptable answer to satisfy the Alliance.

> **飞霄：**我向来相信直觉，自然不会怀疑各位光明磊落的侠士。但眼下罗浮面对的质疑难关也确凿无疑。因此我打算确保最关键的一点，给联盟一个交代。

**Jing Yuan:** What's in this plan, General Feixiao?

> **景元：**飞霄将军打算怎么做？

**Feixiao:** General Jing Yuan, you already know what has to be done, but since you don't want to be the bad guy, I'll take care of it for you. You need the final word from the Ten-Lords Commission to quell any doubts.

> **飞霄：**景元将军心知肚明。只是你不愿做这坏人，那便由我代劳——建木一事仍需要十王司一锤定音，压服众声。

**Feixiao:** And for that, I'll have to ask the two Nameless to visit the Shackling Prison.

> **飞霄：**为此，我要委屈两位无名客前往幽囚狱一趟。

- ▸ Are you imprisoning us?

**Feixiao:** No, I'm not imprisoning you. While you're there, I'll ask a judge in the Ten-Lords Commission's Interrogation Division to record a detailed testimony with the Karmic Mirror from both of you. We'll fill in the gaps that weren't covered in the report and silence any protests within the Alliance.

> **飞霄：**不是扣留。我会申请十王司「问字部」的判官以业镜台为诸位留下一份详细证言，补上报告所缺，也是为了堵上联盟内抗议的嘴巴。

**Dan Heng:** ...I'm okay with that.

> **丹恒：**…我没有问题。

- ▸ Me too.

**Huaiyan:** Your willingness to help is truly heartwarming, youngsters.

> **怀炎：**好！年轻人心肠滚热，如此体贴，老朽感动得眼泪都掉下来了。

- ▸ I object!

**Feixiao:** Worried that I might go back on my word and keep you in The Shackling Prison? Relax, if I wanted to do that, you'd already be behind bars. Once you're done with your testimony, you're free to come and go as you please.

> **飞霄：**你担心我出尔反尔，将你们扣在幽囚狱里？那大可不必…我要是想做早就做了。一旦完成证言，诸位来去自由，礼遇如常。

- ▸ Will you let us come back...?

**Feixiao:** Worried that I might go back on my word and keep you in The Shackling Prison? Relax, if I wanted to do that, you'd already be behind bars. Once you're done with your testimony, you're free to come and go as you please.

> **飞霄：**你担心我出尔反尔，将你们扣在幽囚狱里？那大可不必…我要是想做早就做了。一旦完成证言，诸位来去自由，礼遇如常。

**Jing Yuan:** Then, as the Merlin's Claw requests...

> **景元：**那就依天击将军所请——

**Feixiao:** Oh, there's one more thing. This testimony is for silencing the voices of opposition within the Alliance, but I would like to urge General Jing Yuan to listen to the pleas of the foxians on the Xianzhou Yaoqing.

> **飞霄：**此外，我来罗浮还有一桩要务。如果说十王司盖上大印的证言是为压服联盟高层里反对的声音。那么曜青仙舟狐人内部的声音要恳请景元将军听听。

**Jing Yuan:** So, you are here for Hoolay?

> **景元：**天击将军是为「呼雷」而来？

**Feixiao:** Exactly. Hoolay is locked up in the Luofu's Shackling Prison. Since he is the Brood Lord of the borisin, I want to transfer him onto the Xianzhou Yaoqing and imprison him there. The recent movements of the borisin suggest they're planning something big, so we must act preemptively.

> **飞霄：**正是。我要带走关押在罗浮幽囚狱中的步离人巢父「呼雷」，移交曜青仙舟看管。步离人近日的动向足以证明他们将有大图谋。我们必须及早因应。

**Jing Yuan:** It makes sense to have the foxians keep an eye on their arch-nemesis. Since you trust my judgment, I'll repay that trust. What do you think about all this, General Huaiyan?

> **景元：**呼雷是狐族世仇，由狐人镇压看守也合情合理。飞霄将军信任罗浮的处置，罗浮自然也对将军的处置报以信任。炎老认为如何？

**Huaiyan:** I was worried this would turn into a heated argument, but it seems like both of you are on the same page, solving each other's problems! I couldn't have asked for a better outcome!

> **怀炎：**老朽本来担心这是一场唇枪舌剑的会面。是我多心了！两位既然都做出决定，互解难题，那真是再好不过！

**Feixiao:** And as for Hoolay, I'll send my lieutenants Jiaoqiu and Moze to check on his condition in prison and ready him for transport. If there are no more questions, shall we get this started?

> **飞霄：**移交呼雷一事，我会派遣下属椒丘、貊泽先行对其囚牢状况进行探视，以便后续展开押解。若无其他疑问，这就开始吧？

### Go to The Shackling Prison's entrance

*(Talk to Huaiyan, optional)*

**Huaiyan:** Well, I'm glad we managed to talk this out.

> **怀炎：**化干戈为玉帛，确是好事。

*(Talk to Feixiao, optional)*

Feixiao give you a once-over, and despite lacking any apparent reason, you feel scrutinized, as if she were sizing up an opponent.

**Feixiao:** Do you actually believe in the Stellaron Hunters' prophecy?

> **飞霄：**你相信星核猎手的预言？

- ▸ I do.

**(Trailblazer):** Yes, I do. That's why I'm here.

> **开拓者：**我相信。所以我来了。

- ▸ No, I don't.

**(Trailblazer):** No, I don't. That's why I'm here, to prove that the prophecy is not real.

> **开拓者：**我不相信。所以我来了，为了证明他看到的命运不存在。

- ▸ I don't know.

**(Trailblazer):** The Xianzhou's Matrix of Prescience can also predict the future... Do you believe in its prophecies?

> **开拓者：**仙舟的穷观阵也能预卜未来…你相信它预言的未来吗？

**Feixiao:** ...I understand.

> **飞霄：**…我明白了。

- ▸ Do you really believe us?

Feixiao gives a nod.

**Feixiao:** While words can be deceiving, your gaze, your breathing, and your scent... they reveal more than your words do.

> **飞霄：**语言可以掩饰，但你说话时的目光，呼吸节奏，还有气息都在代你开口。

**Feixiao:** I trust my intuition more than any words.

> **飞霄：**比起别人说什么，我更相信自己的直觉。

- ▸ What are you looking at?

**Feixiao:** You're definitely a unique individual, just like Huaiyan said.

> **飞霄：**你果然像怀炎说的那般，是个特别的存在。

**Feixiao:** I hope you'll walk alongside the Reignbow's path as a friend.

> **飞霄：**但愿你行在与帝弓为友的道路上。

*(Talk to Feixiao again, optional)*

**Feixiao:** You're definitely a unique individual, just like Huaiyan said.

> **飞霄：**你果然像怀炎说的那般，是个特别的存在。

**Feixiao:** I hope you'll walk alongside the Reignbow's path as a friend.

> **飞霄：**但愿你行在与帝弓为友的道路上。

*(Talk to Jing Yuan)*

**Jing Yuan:** I'm really sorry about all this, (Trailblazer), but the testimony in the Shackling Prison won't take up much of your time.

> **景元：**实在抱歉，开拓者。幽囚狱中的笔录不会占用你们太长时间。

**Jing Yuan:** I'll call for a messenger to take you to the Interrogation Division. You just need to give your testimony and then you can head back. So, no need to worry.

> **景元：**我会召来接引者，带两位送往十王司问字部判官处，两位只需留下证言即可返回，不必担忧。

**Dan Heng:** As you wish, General.

> **椒丘：**小子，越狱的老毛病又犯了？别打量了，你现在已是自由之身。万一教判官们发现你有什么不轨企图，高低把你抓进去关个几百年。

**Jiaoqiu:** We're Jiaoqiu and Moze. General Feixiao sent us to extradite the borisin criminal Hoolay to the Yaoqing.

> **椒丘：**椒丘、貊泽奉天击将军飞霄之命，前来引渡步离人重犯「呼雷」前往曜青。

**Jiaoqiu:** We're here to inspect the conditions of his imprisonment and make preparations for the handover and transportation. I assume you've been briefed, your honor.

> **椒丘：**此次前来是为探视其关押状况，为后续交接押送事宜预先准备。判官大人想必已收到了知会。

**Xueyi:** Your visit request has been approved. I'll be your guide for this trip.

> **雪衣：**探视的申请已有批复。吾将是此行的引路人。

**Xueyi:** Prisoner Hoolay, the Warhead and Brood Lord of the borisin Abominations of Abundance and the arch-nemesis of the foxians, is responsible for 2,123 wars of aggression and countless associated crimes.

> **雪衣：**罪囚呼雷，乃丰饶孽物步离人之战首、巢父，狐人之大敌。其人需为先后两千一百二十三场侵略战争及其连带罪行负责。

**Xueyi:** Due to his heinous acts, he has been imprisoned in the depths of The Shackling Prison and subjected to the punishment of the Forest of Swords until the end of time. He shall never be pardoned...

> **雪衣：**以其凶残暴戾，所造杀孽不可胜纪，判入幽囚狱底，受无间剑树之刑直至天地荒灭，永无宽赦……

**Jiaoqiu:** No need to repeat his crimes and sentence, your honor. He is the greatest enemy of us foxians. The stories of his atrocities are used to terrify our children. I'm well aware of every crime he's committed. Let's move on to the next step.

> **椒丘：**判官大人，这判书上的文字就不必念了吧。呼雷其人是我狐族的天敌宿仇，他干下的暴行，可是曜青父母拿来止小儿夜啼的素材，每一桩我都清清楚楚。咱们快进到下一步吧。

**Xueyi:** When it comes to visiting criminals, there are rules in place to ensure your safety.

> **雪衣：**探视重犯，每条规矩都关乎汝等安危。

**Xueyi:** I know you've heard legends about Hoolay since you were children, but your knowledge about him is likely very limited. Only the judges of the Ten-Lords Commission truly know what kind of abomination is locked up at the bottom of The Shackling Prison.

> **雪衣：**就算两位曜青使者自幼耳闻呼雷的传说，但对他的所知依旧少得可怜。唯有十王司判官才清楚，幽囚狱底下关押的到底是一头什么样的孽物。

**Xueyi:** It has been centuries since Jingliu, the former Sword Champion of the Luofu, captured Hoolay. And during all those years, we never provided him with any food. Yet he's somehow managed to stay alive. It defies all the documented physical characteristics of the borisin.

> **雪衣：**自罗浮前任剑首镜流俘获呼雷以来，数百年过去了，幽囚狱从未允许呼雷进食给养。但即便馁饿至今，他依旧活着。这和记录在案的步离人生理完全不符。

**Xueyi:** The Forest of Swords, forged by the Punishment Division, is a device of intense torment used to execute sinful abominations. Most borisin die within three days in the Forest, but Hoolay is different. Every time the blades pierce him, his body instantly heals. Despite the brutal punishment, he somehow manages to survive.

> **雪衣：**刑字部所造的无间剑树，本是对罪行累累的孽物施加痛苦业报的处决刑具。大部分步离人在受剑树刑后熬不过三日。但这妖物不同，剑片穿刺，即刻复原。受刑至今，他也依旧活着。

**Xueyi:** The complex rules are there because of his abnormal characteristics. Do you understand now?

> **雪衣：**这头恶兽的种种妖异之处，便是所有规矩存在的意义。话说到这儿，两位明白了吗？

**Jiaoqiu:** I apologize for any offense caused... Please continue, your honor.

> **椒丘：**是椒丘冒犯了…请大人继续。

**Xueyi:** I've given you the instructions regarding Hoolay's visitation. Please make sure you read them carefully.

> **雪衣：**关于呼雷的探视禁忌事宜，吾已交付二位，请认真阅读。

*(Quick Read)*

Reminders When Visiting Hoolay

**Prisoner: Hoolay:** Summary of Crimes: The borisin Warhead, a Denizen of Abundance. Accountable for numerous acts of invasions and associated war crimes. Accountable for the long-term enslavement of foxians and utilizing their blood for alchemical purposes. Detention Method: This prisoner must be subjected to the punishment of the Forest of Swords and never be pardoned. They must be transferred to different locations on a regular basis, their shackles must be checked for sturdiness during the transfer, and the air filters in their new cell must be intact and functional. Notes: 1. The confinement of this prisoner is the foundation of the alliance between the Xianzhou and the foxians. Any individuals objecting to this sentence should be punished for "Sowing Discord." 2. Anyone who contacts or converses with this prisoner must take poison-proof pellets or wear a breathing mask equipped with a filter cartridge to counteract the effects of Lupitoxin. 3. The prisoner's place of detention must be changed on a yearly basis, and the new location must remain unknown to individuals below the rank of Cloud Knight Lieutenant or Judge. 4. Do not mention "Jingliu" when conversing with this prisoner.

**Xueyi:** And please take this pellet before proceeding.

> **雪衣：**还有，去往下一层之前，务必要吃下这丸丹药。

> **貊泽：**来历不明的药，我是不会吃的。

> **雪衣：**那汝等就无法进入底层探视。
