> **椒丘：**吃了吧。呼雷这厮和所有步离人一样，能释放名唤「狼毒」的信息素，唤起内心的恐惧。

**Jiaoqiu:** Thousands of years ago, we foxians were enslaved by the borisin, not because we were naturally weaker, but because of their Lupitoxin. This pill is for our own mental well-being.

> **椒丘：**数千年前，我族受步离人长久奴役，无力反抗。不是因为狐人天然低他们一等，原因就在这个。判官大人的药可是为咱们俩的心理健康着想呢。

**Moze:** I understand.

> **貊泽：**明白了。

**Jiaoqiu:** I knew you were a reasonable person. Now that we've taken the medicine, let's proceed.

> **椒丘：**我就知道你是个通情达理的人。药我们已咽下了，走吧。

**Dan Heng:** The judges will use a complicated interrogation device called the Karmic Mirror. It accurately tracks every physiological change during questioning and records it.

> **丹恒：**判官折狱，会借助「业镜台」之类的复杂审讯系统。那东西会在每一次问答时，如同透镜般解读受审者身体的变化与信号，留下存证。

> **丹恒：**经由此处做出的审断，足以令其他仙舟的管理者信服…至少他们不会妄加推翻。这也是飞霄执意需要我们来此的缘故吧。

- ▸ Since the judge isn't here yet, I'll just wander around a bit.

Looking down, you see a honeycomb of densely-packed prison cells extending deep into the darkness beyond your view.

You hazard a guess on the number of prisoners they are capable of holding here... Three thousand? Thirty thousand? Three hundred thousand? Wait, this may not even be the entire Shackling Prison!

*(Investigate jade abaci)*

The jade abaci here glow with an unusual luminescence as they perform their calculations, possibly on the status of the prison cells or the security of each area.

**Hanya:** Welcome, dear guests from the Express. Judge Hanya of the Interrogation Division, we've met before.

> **寒鸦：**怎么，开拓者不想见到我？也罢，我可以唤来擅用针灸的判官，精通斧锯的判官，又或是长于鞭索的判官来问话…多种选择，必有一款适合您。

**Hanya:** Just kidding. We judges work in shifts, and it just happens to be my shift, (Trailblazer). Maybe we're destined to cross paths.

> **寒鸦：**开个小玩笑。判官执勤轮流周转，开拓者只是刚巧碰上了我，可见我俩冥冥中有些缘分。

**Hanya:** Please allow me to express my gratitude to you again for subduing the demons in the Fyxestroll Garden.

> **寒鸦：**此前绥园伏魔一事，真是有劳您奔波了，容寒鸦再度致谢。

**Dan Heng:** Looks like while March 7th and I were clueless, you already made many friends on the Xianzhou Luofu.

> **丹恒：**看来在我和三月七不知不觉的时候，你已经在罗浮仙舟上颇有人缘了啊。

**Hanya:** Even though (Trailblazer) and I have met before, we can't show any favoritism under the Ten Lords. So, please do as I command as we head to Scrivener Hall and beyond. Don't do anything without my permission.

> **寒鸦：**虽说开拓者与我有一面之缘，但十王御下，不容徇私。前往「录事厅」留下证言以及之后的一切流程，请两位务必听我号令，不得擅自行动。

**Hanya:** This is not a place for ordinary mortals. (Trailblazer) and Mr. Dan Heng, please come with me.

> **寒鸦：**此处不是阳世凡人久待之地。丹恒先生，开拓者，随我来。

**Dan Heng:** Please lead the way, your honor.

> **丹恒：**请判官为我们引路。

**Hanya:** Please let me activate the mechanism before we all move forward, and please watch your steps.

> **寒鸦：**待我启动机关，各位再向前走，务必小心脚下。

### Go through the shifting screen and head to the depths of The Shackling Prison

*(Talk to Hanya, optional)*

**Hanya:** Be very careful and watch your step as you make your way through.

> **寒鸦：**这一路行去，各位一定要留神自己的落脚处。

**Hanya:** The terrain inside the Shackling Prison is treacherous. One wrong move, and you could end up plunging into the depths. And if you're really unlucky, you might be frozen solid or burned to a crisp before anyone can help you.

> **寒鸦：**幽囚狱内地势险怪，稍有不慎便会坠入深处。运气要是背些，只怕在救援赶到前，会被冻入幽邃坚冰里，又或是被焦炎烧成炭烬。

**(Trailblazer):** Gee...

> **开拓者：**噫……

**Hanya:** But with your skills, I don't think you need to worry about that.

- ▸ So, are there really eighteen floors here?

> **寒鸦：**开拓者说笑了，幽囚狱各处山重水复，层层相叠。还请来了持明的龙脉巧匠，用尽了洞天之术的奇巧。两位肉眼所见的只是幽囚狱的一部分。

- ▸ Is that true, Dan Heng?

**Hanya:** As for how many floors there are...? Well, not even us judges know for sure.

> **寒鸦：**要问此处到底有多少层？让两位见笑了，即便身为判官的我也不知道。

**Hanya:** But there's a legend among us judges that a short-life prisoner once tried to escape but ended up falling into the depths... and when we found him at the bottom, he was already an old man on the brink of death...

> **寒鸦：**不过判官之间流传，有短生种犯人试图逃脱，却不慎坠下…当我们寻到他时，他已是个垂垂老者了……

**Hanya:** Did he spend his whole life falling? Or did some long-life prisoner drain his life essence? We'll never know. Anyway, please don't try anything like that.

> **寒鸦：**如此想来，他究竟是在下落中度过了一生的时间，还是被某些长生种犯人吸食精气变老？这就不得而知了。总之，两位可不要轻易尝试啊。

- ▸ Why keep the terrain so dangerous?

**Hanya:** You're funny, (Trailblazer). This prison does contain many layers. We even brought in Vidyadhara Ichor Line craftsmen to introduce the ingenuity of delve enchantment. What you're seeing now is just one part of The Shackling Prison.

> **寒鸦：**开拓者说笑了，幽囚狱各处山重水复，层层相叠。还请来了持明的龙脉巧匠，用尽了洞天之术的奇巧。两位肉眼所见的只是幽囚狱的一部分。

- ▸ Is that true, Dan Heng?

**Dan Heng:** Perhaps. Judging from the appearance, much of the architecture in this prison has a Vidyadharan style... So perhaps they did have a hand in building this place back then.

> **丹恒：**也许吧，从外观看，这座幽囚狱的许多建筑都留有持明的制式…也许在古早之前，持明确实参与营建了此处。

**Hanya:** Well, the paths here serve as both passageways for the Ten-Lords Commission and barriers for the prisoners.

> **寒鸦：**只不过幽囚狱的路既是为十王司人员所设的通道，也是为犯人所设的障碍。

**Hanya:** Most prisoners locked up here have committed serious crimes violating the Xianzhou Legal Code, and many of them are Denizens of Abundance like borisin, houyhnhnm, and wingweavers. To be honest, these abominations... are pretty good at trying to break out.

> **寒鸦：**此地关押的多是些大奸大恶，触犯仙舟律条的犯人。这班人中又以步离、慧骃、羽夷之类的丰饶孽物居多。这些丰饶民，说句不妥帖的，各个身怀绝技…谋划脱狱之事时有发生。

**Hanya:** So, as long as the treacherous terrain here makes it harder for them to escape, the Ten-Lords Commission doesn't have any complaints.

> **寒鸦：**只消能为他们逃离此处多增添一份困难，这些艰难道路对咱们十王司的人来说算得了什么。

**Hanya:** Please come this way.

> **寒鸦：**两位这边走。

### Take the elevator

*(Attempt to leave the area, optional)*

Hanya blocks your way forward. She has been entrusted with the important task of ensuring that you only explore areas she deems safe.

*(Talk to Hanya, optional)*

**Hanya:** I heard the Wardance is about to be held in the mortal world. I remember watching a sword duel at the Wardance with my sister when we were young.

> **寒鸦：**听闻阳世即将召开演武仪典。寒鸦犹记得自己小时候曾与姐姐观赏过其中一场剑斗。

**Hanya:** The Cloud Knight who was the Luofu's ringmaster was an amazing swordsman, and their sword flashed like lightning. The outworld challenger had fiery red hair and a hand made of steel which roared like thunder. They battled for a long time before a winner could be declared.

> **寒鸦：**为罗浮守擂的云骑剑士英武非凡，剑光如电。异邦的挑战者一头火发，一只手竟是镔铁铸成，拳锋如雷。两人相斗良久，难分胜负。

**Hanya:** Time sure flies. Now, both my sister and I are residents of the netherworld, and we will never again feel the thrill of a competition.

> **寒鸦：**一眨眼，时间已经过去了这么久。寒鸦和姐姐都成了冥世之身，再不会为什么比赛激动雀跃了。

**Hanya:** Are there a lot of people in Starskiff Haven due to the Wardance, (Trailblazer)?

> **寒鸦：**开拓者，这一回演武仪典，星槎海的人流多吗？

- ▸ Yeah, it's bustling.

**Hanya:** Sounds amazing. It's a shame I'm too busy to attend... but even if I could, I'd probably just scare people off with my current appearance.

> **寒鸦：**真好啊。可惜寒鸦公务繁忙，不能观赏赛事…以我如今的样貌，去了只怕也会吓到旁人。

- ▸ Aren't we going to take the elevator?

**Hanya:** Sorry, I got a bit distracted there.

> **寒鸦：**对不起，是我走神了。

**Hanya:** Let's get going, then.

> **寒鸦：**咱们走吧。

### Investigate the source of the strange noise

> *In light of Feixiao's request, you stepped into the Shackling Prison and left your statements. The Yaoqing messenger that she dispatched whisked away the long-detained borisin leader, Hoolay, who was being held on the Xianzhou Luofu. Deep in The Shackling Prison, the abyssal treachery of the conspiracy gradually dawned upon you.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hanya:** Ugh, what an eyesore. This place is packed with boxes and crates.

> **寒鸦：**箱笼堆叠，真是碍眼。

*(Approach crates)*

**Dan Heng:** These crates... they look oddly familiar.

> **丹恒：**这些箱子…看起来有些眼熟。

**Hanya:** A few days ago, the spiritfarers received reports about an IPC transport ship that was attacked by borisin. Then, a bunch of those abominations were dumped into this place.

> **寒鸦：**前些日子冥差收到传唤，说是公司的运输船遭受了步离人袭击。随后一大群孽畜便被丢进了这里。

**Hanya:** I had a feeling there would be trouble during the Wardance. But throwing both the pirates and the cargo in jail? That's a new one.

> **寒鸦：**想来演武仪典召开，总有不轨之徒蠢蠢欲动。只是货物与劫匪一同被扔进监狱的事情，我倒是从没见过。

- ▸ The mechs in those crates are no joke.
- ▸ Looks like the IPC has caught the attention of the borisin...

**Hanya:** I heard the Intelligentsia Guild crafted something dangerous. We have many records in the Hall of Karma about these wise ones. They love to tinker with forbidden technologies, always trying to push the limits of ingenia.

> **寒鸦：**我听说啦，博识学会的人似乎造出了某种危险的东西。因果殿的卷宗中有不少关于这些智叟的记录，他们为了突破机巧技术的极限，总喜欢涉足禁忌研究。

**Hanya:** I caught a glimpse of the mechs in those crates, and they bear a striking resemblance to borisin. I wonder what they're planning this time?

> **寒鸦：**我见过箱中的机甲，造型与步离人颇为近似。真不知道这回他们打算做什么？

**Hanya:** Well, business first. Let's keep moving.

> **寒鸦：**正事要紧，我们走吧。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Hanya:** Strange...

> **寒鸦：**奇怪

**Hanya:** I don't remember checking the containment facilities the second time...

> **寒鸦：**我印象中并未对收容物进行二次检查……

**Dan Heng:** Watch out!

> **丹恒：**小心！

**Hanya:** Emergency! All wardens prepare for action!

> **寒鸦：**情况紧急，武弁听令！速来支援！

**Hanya:** Emergency! All wardens prepare for action!

*(Begin battle against Sableclaw Wolftrooper ×1, Howling Casket ×2)*

> **寒鸦：**不对劲，守卫们毫无回应…

*(After the enemies enter the Moon Rage state)*

**Hanya:** This thing... is going wild, just like a borisin.

> **寒鸦：**这东西…发起狂来和步离人一样。

**Dan Heng:** Looks like we're on our own for this one.

> **丹恒：**看来，我们得自救了。

*(After the battle)*

**Hanya:** But how is this even possible? The spiritfarers followed the protocols, cutting off power to the mechs and sealing the crates. How could these mechs still start moving?

> **寒鸦：**这怎么可能，冥差们已经按照规程切断了箱子里机器的动力，还在箱子之外施加了封印。为何这些机甲还能行动？

**Dan Heng:** It's just like what happened in the Artisanship Commission before. These goods went haywire and attacked everyone in sight.

> **丹恒：**和当时在工造司里的情况一样，这些货物仿佛失去了控制一般攻击在场所有人。

> **丹恒：**丹鼎司的人检查过机甲，这东西内部隐含着非同一般的结构。

**Hanya:** Something seems to be jamming communications within The Shackling Prison.

> **寒鸦：**从刚才开始，似乎有某种东西在干扰幽囚狱内的通讯。

**Dan Heng:** It's probably these mechs causing the trouble.

> **丹恒：**说不定正是这些机甲干的好事。

**Dan Heng:** These things showing up in The Shackling Prison can only mean one thing...

> **丹恒：**这样危险的东西出现在幽囚狱里，唯一的目的就是——

**Hanya:** A prison break.

> **寒鸦：**劫狱。

**Dan Heng:** And whoever delivered these "goods" clearly wanted them to go through the Xianzhou's strict inspection process, to show the Sky-Faring Commission and Cloud Knights how dangerous they were. They wanted these mechs to end up right here in The Shackling Prison.

> **丹恒：**而送来这些「货物」的人，显然希望仙舟严格的审核流程能将它的危险暴露在天舶司和云骑的眼前。他们想确保的是它一定会被送入幽囚狱。

**Hanya:** If these things already started taking action while nobody was paying attention, then the whole prison is in trouble, I'm afraid.

> **寒鸦：**如果在所有人无法察觉的时候，这些东西已经开始行动。那整座监狱现在的状况就非常危险了。

**Hanya:** And to make things worse, another group of visitors just entered the depths of The Shackling Prison, the messengers from the Xianzhou Yaoqing.

> **寒鸦：**更危险的是，除两位之外，刚才还有一队来访者进入了幽囚狱深处——曜青仙舟的使者。

**Hanya:** And the prisoner they came to visit... might be the target whom these wolf-shaped mechs were delivered here for. If that vicious beast manages to break free... it will be a catastrophe for the Xianzhou Luofu!

> **寒鸦：**他们探访的对象…也许就是这些狼形机甲被送入此地的目标。若是让镇压在底部的那头恶兽逃了出来…恐怕罗浮仙舟也将有大灾难！

### Verify your identity and open a path to the Shackling Prison

A few moments prior, Xueyi leads the Yaoqing messengers to the bottom of the prison.

> **雪衣：**到了。

> **椒丘：**判官大人，这便到了吗？

**Jiaoqiu:** Shouldn't there be a cage here?

> **椒丘：**这儿可没什么囚笼啊？

**Xueyi:** The most notorious felons are locked away in solitary delves deep down in the prison. Those delves can't be opened without proper authorization.

> **雪衣：**犯尽十恶，大逆不道的罪人都被镇于幽狱之底，以单独的洞天囚禁。若无谕令，等闲不会开启。

**Xueyi:** "The blue bird paves the path, and the Stygian Lanterns illuminate it." Help me light up these lanterns, and the way to the bottom of the Shackling Prison will reveal itself.

> **雪衣：**「青鸟开道，冥灯照路」。助我点亮这些灯座，通往幽狱之底的前路自会显现。

*(Talk to Moze, optional)*

*(Quick Read)*

Guidance for Stygian Lantern

The markings on the stone indicate the order in which the Stygian Lanterns need to be lit from the bottom to the top.

*(Investigate the barrier in front of the cycrane, optional)*

Towering spirits with gruesome appearances glare menacingly at you, as if warning you to proceed with caution.

Their gazes converge at a pitch-black void, and you wonder what it's for.

*Awaken the Messenger*

Visiting the Shackling Prison's crypts. Authorization complete.

*(Investigate the barrier in front of the cycrane, optional)*

When a "key" in the shape of a colossal sky-bearing column slots into the "keyhole," it unlocks the path to the Shackling Prison crypts, where criminals of high severity are held in subdued captivity.

That was the Nether Key — the key that keeps felons locked away behind the ultimate gate.

### Follow Xueyi to the bottom of The Shackling Prison

*(Light up lanterns in the correct order)*

**Xueyi:** The path is open. Once we descend to the bottom of the prison, please do not do anything reckless.

> **雪衣：**两位，路已开启。一旦下到幽狱之底，绝不要轻举妄动。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Xueyi:** Inside the delve behind this door is the greatest enemy of the foxians, Hoolay.

> **雪衣：**在这扇门后的洞天里，关押的便是狐人的大敌，呼雷。

**Voice From Behind the Door:** Arrrrgh...

> **门后的声音：**呃啊啊啊啊啊——

**Xueyi:** According to custom, envoys from the Yaoqing visit the Xianzhou Luofu once every century to check on this abomination's imprisonment and condition.

> **雪衣：**依循常例，曜青使节每百年一次抵达罗浮仙舟，确认这头孽物的关押事宜与其生死状况。

**Xueyi:** Even though the Ten-Lords Commission sentenced Hoolay to the Forest of Swords, suffering every day for the rest of his life, I understand that the Yaoqing messengers want to see him dead...

> **雪衣：**尽管十王司判处呼雷受无间剑树之刑，令其日日苦楚，万世不休。但吾很明白，曜青使者们最想看到的是呼雷咽气的死状……

**Xueyi:** Unfortunately, for the past seven centuries, they've had to return disappointed, because this beast simply can't be killed.

> **雪衣：**可惜，这七百年来他们全都失望而归了，那是头杀不死的恶兽。

**Jiaoqiu:** If we can use his toxin to create medicine and save an innocent life, it might help balance out some of the sins he has committed.

> **椒丘：**世间至毒若能入药，换回一条无辜的生命，也或许能稍稍抵偿他所犯下的累累血罪了。

**Jiaoqiu:** Could you be the key to a cure for the General, Hoolay?

> **椒丘：**治愈将军的法子，会是你吗，呼雷？

**???:** And once again, the envoys of the Yaoqing will leave disappointed. However, I won't say the same for me and my brothers...

> **？？？：**这一次，曜青的使节也将失望而归。但我和我的兄弟们就不同了……

**Xueyi:** Who's there?!

> **雪衣：**谁？！

**???:** I'm just a humble counselor of the Rhinohound Pack. You can call me... Mok Tok

> **？？？：**在下是「犀犬猎群」一介小小的策问官，你可以叫我…末度。

**Xueyi:** Wardens! Intruders on the lowest level! Send reinforcements!

> **雪衣：**武弁！底层遭到入侵，速速增援！

**Mok Tok:** Nobody will hear you here, at the bottom of the Shackling Prison.

> **末度：**在幽狱之底，没人能听见诸位的呼喊。

**Mok Tok:** Thank you for opening up the prison for us, your honor. We'll take it from here.

> **末度：**多谢你替我们打开了囚牢，判官。剩下的事情，交给我们就好。

**Jiaoqiu:** No wonder I kept smelling that familiar stench. So, it wasn't just my imagination. Do your thing, Moze!

> **椒丘：**难怪我始终嗅到一股熟悉的兽臭味，原来这不是我的错觉啊。貊泽，动手！

**Xueyi:** We mustn't let these abominations get any closer!

> **雪衣：**两位，绝不能让这些孽物靠近！

*(Begin battle against Sableclaw Wolftrooper ×0, Eclipse Wolftrooper ×0, Howling Casket ×0)*

**Xueyi:** Detestable things, there are too many of them.

> **雪衣：**可恶，这些孽物人数太多了。

**Mok Tok:** Once we were inside The Shackling Prison, we found "soldiers" available for us everywhere.

> **末度：**一旦进入幽囚狱，可用之「兵」到处都是。

*(During 2nd wave)*

**Mok Tok:** I suggest you surrender now.

> **末度：**我奉劝诸位及早投降。

**Mok Tok:** Of course, I'll still kill you all.

> **末度：**当然，我绝不会留各位性命。

**Mok Tok:** ...But I promise it'll be swift.

> **末度：**…至少这样省去了无谓的痛苦。

*(During 3rd wave)*

**Xueyi:** How presumptuous! Die now!

> **雪衣：**口出狂言！受死吧！

**Jiaoqiu:** Leave if you can, Moze.

> **椒丘：**貊泽，找机会离开。

**Moze:** ...It's not time yet.

> **貊泽：**…还没到这时候。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Xueyi:** Guard the gate — they can't be allowed in!

> **雪衣：**守住门关，绝不能放他们进去！

**Jiaoqiu:** Hurry!

> **椒丘：**快走！

**Hoolay:** I've waited... for too long...

> **呼雷：**我等了…太久了……

**Hoolay:** My first meal after regaining my freedom... Is the flesh and blood of my fellow kindred. A sad scene indeed.

> **呼雷：**重获自由的第一餐…竟是同胞的血肉，实在令人悲伤。

**Mok Tok:** It's been a long time, Warhead. Welcome back.

> **末度：**久违了，战首。我们来迎你归巢。

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Hoolay:** How many times has the old verdant home spun around since our last hunt? Ah, Duran's whelp! Tell me your name!

> **呼雷：**距离上次狩猎，青丘究竟旋转了多少次？都蓝的崽子！告诉我你的名字！

**Mok Tok:** O, Great Hoolay, nemesis of the foxians, and the hunter of all beings, I'm Mok Tok, a humble counselor of the Rhinohound Pack. I am only one insignificant heir spawned from your bloodline.

> **末度：**伟大的呼雷汗，狐人之敌，众生的猎者。我只是犀犬猎群中一个小小的策问官，也许曾是您血脉中产下的最微不足道的子嗣之一。我名末度。

**Mok Tok:** It's been... at least seven centuries since you led our Pack through the hunting grounds of the stars. I'm thrilled to see that you are as cunning and skillful as ever.

> **末度：**距离您上次率领我族驰骋星海的猎场，已经过去…至少七百年时间了。看到您依旧如过去般狡猾机敏，我无限欢欣。

**Hoolay:** Seven centuries... Seven centuries have passed, but why have Duran's whelps grown to look like this? Tell me, Mok Tok, why have you grown to resemble our most despicable slaves and enemies, the foxians?

> **呼雷：**七百年…七百年过去了，都蓝的崽子为什么长成了你这般模样？回答我，末度，为什么你长成了我们最可鄙的奴隶和敌人——狐人的模样？

**Mok Tok:** I've been commanded to release you from this cursed prison. It is Sain Jiyaa's will. That's why I had to take a magic pill, don the skin of a lowly beast, and play along with their hypocrisy.

> **末度：**我受命前来，将您从可憎的囚牢中释放。这是Sain Jiyaa注定如此。为此，我不得不服下魔药，披上贱畜的皮，用伪装来应对他们的虚伪。

**Hoolay:** Since you have an escape plan, tell me, what's our next move to get off of this ridiculously large ship?

> **呼雷：**既然你有逃离的计划，那么说说，接下来该怎么做才能离开这艘大到没边的天船？

**Mok Tok:** My brothers and I will lock down this prison, trapping the prison guards inside. This will buy us some time.

> **末度：**我和弟兄们会封死这座监狱的门关，把狱卒闷死在里面。这样我们能暂时得到喘息的时间。

**Mok Tok:** The rest of my crew, who are undercover like me, will secure the vessels for our escape. According to the plan, we only have a tight two-hour window to get out of here.

> **末度：**那些和我一样伪装的弟兄们会为您的逃离准备船只。按照计划，我们总共有两个时辰离开这儿。

**Mok Tok:** Originally, I intended to carry out the plan tomorrow, but they're planning to transfer you to the Xianzhou Yaoqing now, so I seized this opportunity to set you free.

> **末度：**原本我打算在明日执行营救您的计划，不过他们打算将您送往曜青仙舟关押，我必须趁此良机，将您解放出来。

**Hoolay:** What a reckless plan, you idiot! Even if we manage to get the ships, our chances of escaping are slimmer than a foxian slipping through my claws!

> **呼雷：**有勇无谋的计划，蠢货！就算有船，我们能逃出去的机会也比狐人逃过我爪子的机会要少得多！

**Mok Tok:** But we have no other choice! The angel serving under the Master of Immortality sent me with a message, "Only your return will end the prolonged divisions among the borisin."

> **末度：**为了救回您，我们别无选择！派我前来的长生主的天使如此说过，「只有您的回归才能结束步离人漫长的分裂」。

**Mok Tok:** Every one of my crew is ready to lay down their lives if it means setting you free.

> **末度：**此行的所有兄弟都已做好了牺牲的准备——只要能让您离开这儿。

**Hoolay:** A sneaky weakling like you is actually showing some reckless bravery with this plan. Fine, you'll get what you want... and all of Duran's whelps will too.

> **呼雷：**像你这样靠狡诈求生的弱者，居然在计划中表现出近乎愚蠢的勇悍。你会得偿所愿的…所有都蓝的崽子都会得偿所愿的。

**Mok Tok:** Well then, my Lord, we should leave now.

> **末度：**那么，大人，我们该走了。

**Hoolay:** Before we go, give me one of those magic pills.

> **呼雷：**在离开前，把你们吃下的魔药给我一丸。

**Mok Tok:** My Lord... you seriously want to don the skin of a lowly beast? That would be a disgrace to your greatness, my Lord...

> **末度：**您…您居然要披上贱畜的皮？这有辱您伟大的身份……

**Hoolay:** You idiot! Greatness means nothing without freedom! Once I get out, I'll need a disguise that won't raise any suspicion.

> **呼雷：**白痴，失去自由的伟大一文不值！一旦离开此地，我需要一张至少看起来不那么可疑的皮囊。

**Mok Tok:** As you wish, my Lord.

> **末度：**谨遵您的命令。

**Hoolay:** Is that foxian slave my meal?

> **呼雷：**那个狐人奴隶，是献给我的食粮吗？

**Mok Tok:** No, he's an envoy from the Xianzhou Yaoqing. I plead you to endure him a bit, my Lord, as he's more valuable as a hostage. Take him, guys! Time to move out!

> **末度：**不，他是曜青仙舟的使节。请您暂时忍耐爪牙，他还有作为人质的价值。带上他，咱们走！

**Jiaoqiu:** ...

> **椒丘：**……

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 021　Rogues on the Run　狼戾亡命，群凶未定

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：The Shackling Prison　|　角色：Trailblazer; Dan Heng; Hanya; Xueyi; Moze; Hoolay; Jiaoqiu; Mok Tok; Jing Yuan; Feixiao; Huaiyan　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Inquisition to Rectitude'' completed

### Head to the bottom of The Shackling Prison to investigate the status of the Yaoqing messenger

> *In the Shackling Prison, unidentified machines and prison breakers wreak chaos with impunity. Their goal is to rescue the borisin Warhead. Meanwhile, the Yaoqing messenger's fate is unknown. At the behest of Hanya, you all have no choice but to delve deeper into the Shackling Prison to confirm if the convict is still behind bars...*

At the same time, (Trailblazer), Dan Heng, and Hanya realize that the situation in the prison is out of control...

**Wraith Warden:** Madam Hanya, the wardens in all areas are regrouping. Those ingenium enemies are wandering around and causing heavy casualties.

> **幽府武弁：**寒鸦大人，各区域武弁正在重整队伍。那些机巧敌人正在到处游走，杀伤了不少同僚。

**Wraith Warden:** And to make things worse, those iron wolves broke open the cages and let the criminals out! We've called for backup, but it seems like all communication is jammed.

> **幽府武弁：**不止如此，那些铁狼还破坏囚笼，放走了罪犯！我们已经向外求援了，但看起来所有的通讯都被阻绝了。

**Hanya:** Let's preserve our strength and first take down the isolated prisoners. By decree of the Ten Lords, all prison breakers and intruders shall be apprehended, dead or alive.

> **寒鸦：**保存实力为先，优先镇伏落单的囚犯和劫狱者。奉十王敕，凡有脱狱、劫狱者，不禁杀伤。

**Aurumaton Spectral Envoy:** Just a short time ago, the Nether Key mechanism was activated. It was probably Madam Xueyi and the Yaoqing messengers, heading to the bottom of the prison. Given the situation, I'm afraid that area is exactly where our enemies are targeting.

> **金人勾魂使：**一刻之前，「断狱轮钥」的机关启动。想来是雪衣大人和曜青使节进入幽狱之底进行探视了。以眼下失联的情况来看，贼人的目标正是此处。

**Hanya:** We mustn't let that vile beast escape from its cage!

> **寒鸦：**绝不可让那头恶兽从牢笼中脱出！

**Aurumaton Spectral Envoy:** We've gathered all the prison guards who can still fight, and we'll split up and secure each floor.

> **金人勾魂使：**已清点尚能战斗的狱卒，即将开赴各层搜捕。

**Hanya:** Go!

> **寒鸦：**去吧！

**Dan Heng:** Those intruders are quite bold. They must have been planning this for quite a while.

> **丹恒：**那些劫狱者如此胆大妄为，一定为这次行动计划了许久。

**Hanya:** Indeed, but borisin tend to favor direct and aggressive approaches. Crafting careful and precise plans like this... is simply not their style.

> **寒鸦：**你说的不错。只是奇怪步离人从来更乐衷于暴力杀伤，制定如此精密周详的计划…不像他们一贯的风格。

**Hanya:** Let's focus on the current situation right now. Please help me!

> **寒鸦：**眼下情势危急，不是复盘审视的时候。两位，请先助我一臂之力！

- ▸ Looks like the testimony will have to wait, huh?

> ▸ 证言的事先放一边吧？

- ▸ Like I said, wherever we go, we always end up in trouble.

> ▸ 果然咱们所到之处没有太平。

**Dan Heng:** What's the fastest way to the bottom of this prison, Miss Hanya? If we don't get there quickly, we'll soon be outnumbered by all the enemies inside.

> **丹恒：**寒鸦小姐，最快前往幽狱之底的通路在什么地方？若不能在时间上争分夺秒，恐怕整个幽囚狱都会成为我们的敌人。

**Hanya:** We'll have to bend the rules. Please follow me.

> **寒鸦：**为今之计，只有使些取巧的办法了，诸位随我来。

*(Unlock Achievement Shackles Shattered)*

**Hanya:** By decree of the Ten Lords, all prison breakers and intruders shall be apprehended, dead or alive.

> **武弁：**是！

Large pieces of metal scraps are strewn all over the floor. You can't put your finger on it, but you're certain that this thing is still alive. Bionic tissues are relentlessly regenerating beneath their exoskeleton.

> **寒鸦：**守住此地。

### Go to the bottom of The Shackling Prison

**Hanya:** The Nether Key has been activated. I hope we're not too late...

> **寒鸦：**「断狱轮钥」已打开了通路，希望我们来得还不算晚……

**Hanya:** Hang in there, sister!

> **寒鸦：**姐姐，你要坚持住啊！

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hanya:** ...

> **寒鸦：**曜青使者呢？姐姐呢？他们还活着吗？

### Look for Xueyi's tracks in the mess

*(Investigate warden corpses, optional)*

This warden fought to her death, and her weapon's nowhere to be seen. She must have lost it somewhere in the prison.

You can clearly tell that the warden was serving her duty in an intense battle when she died. You reset the mask over her face and pray for her heroic soul to rest in peace.

*(Investigate borisin corpses, optional)*

Sharp fangs grew unchecked along the deceased's protruded lower jaw, bearing an uncanny resemblance to the other borisin corpses.

You begin to see why the borisin could easily infiltrate the lower levels of the Shackling Prison. They not only shapeshifted into the Cloud Knights, but into foxians too.

Whoever impaled this borisin from behind probably caught them off guard. It could be a judge or a Yaoqing messenger.

These are the remains of a borisin. Judging from their twisted limbs, it is likely that this massive wolf once had their limbs restrained.

The corpse in a Cloud Knight's armor catches your attention. They do not seem to be your average foxian, for one. This body looks like it underwent a drastic shapeshifting, one that failed to conceal the guard hairs around their neck.

*(Investigate gate to Hoolay's cell, optional)*

In the suffocating, empty darkness, an air of deathly stillness lingered about the gates of the cage that once confined Hoolay, chilling all who gaze upon it to the marrow.

The cage should have been virtually impossible to break out of, yet even it had failed to trap the savage beast for long.

*(Investigate Xueyi's body)*

**(Trailblazer):** Xueyi... is here.

> **开拓者：**雪衣…在这儿。

**Dan Heng:** Miss Xueyi bravely sacrificed herself... My condolences, Miss Hanya.

> **丹恒：**雪衣小姐壮烈成仁…寒鸦小姐，请节哀。

**Hanya:** Condolences? Well, that's unnecessary.

> **寒鸦：**哀悼？我没那个意思。

**Hanya:** You misunderstand. Thanks to the Ten Lords' blessing, my sister turned into an ingenium centuries ago. Dying and returning as just a soul to the Hall of Karma's document office has become a routine for her.

> **寒鸦：**你误会了。蒙十王恩赐，姐姐在数百年前早已成了机巧之身。死亡后其「灵魂」回归因果殿的案牍库，对她来说早已是家常便饭。

**Hanya:** While I'm not keen on seeing her throw away her body like it's some replaceable part, but...

> **寒鸦：**虽然我也不想看到她每次如此轻率地消耗自己的身体，就像一个可以被轻率替换的零件那般。但……

**Hanya:** By the way, have you seen bodies that might be the Yaoqing messengers? One of them is a foxian wearing exquisite clothes, and the other wears a dark cloak, like a fugitive.

> **寒鸦：**对了，有没有找到可能是曜青使节的伤亡者？其中一人是衣装华美的狐人，另一个披挂暗色袍子，瞧着像是个亡命之徒。

**Dan Heng:** ...No, I haven't found any foxian bodies, only borisin ones.

> **丹恒：**…不，除去步离人之外，这里的遗骸中我没瞧见长得像狐人的死者。

**Hanya:** So, the intruders and Hoolay are holding the Yaoqing messengers hostage.

> **寒鸦：**也就是说，劫狱者和呼雷挟持了曜青使者。

**Hanya:** This is the worst situation. With hostages in their grasp, the prison guards can't do anything about the prisoners. If anything happens to the messengers, the relationship between the Yaoqing and the Luofu... could be irreversibly damaged.

> **寒鸦：**这真是最糟糕的情况了。人质在手，狱卒们对这些家伙恐怕也无能为力。万一有什么闪失，罗浮仙舟与曜青仙舟的关系…恐怕也将陷入不可挽回的撕裂。

**Hanya:** We must rescue the messengers, get out of here, and spread the news. We mustn't give up as long as there's a glimmer of hope!

> **寒鸦：**必须救下使节，逃离此处，向外递送消息。只要还有一线希望，就绝不能放弃！

*(Hanya notices a broken cycrane)*

**Dan Heng:** What have you found, Miss Hanya?

> **丹恒：**寒鸦小姐发现了什么？

**Broken Cycrane:** Han... Han...

> **损坏的机巧鸟：**寒…寒……

- ▸ What the heck?!

> ▸ 什么鬼！

- ▸ A cyber specter!

> ▸ 赛博幽灵！

**Broken Cycrane:** Re... Reconstru...

> **损坏的机巧鸟：**重…重新……

**Hanya:** It's my sister. Looks like her soul hasn't returned to the Hall of Karma yet.

> **寒鸦：**是姐姐。看来她的灵魂恪尽职守，暂时还没回到因果殿中。

**Hanya:** At such a critical moment... her "death" can give us information that we can turn into an advantage.

> **寒鸦：**恰恰是在这节骨眼上…姐姐的「死亡」反倒成了我们的情报优势。

*(Approach the cycrane)*

**Xueyi (cycrane version):** Now is not the time for j-j-jokes!

> **丹恒：**有一名曜青使者逃脱了？另外一人被挟持向上逃去？

> **雪衣（机巧鸟版）：**除步离人外…还有…别的…入侵者…看不见……

> **寒鸦：**姐姐，帮我个忙，劫狱者们切断了幽囚狱对内与对外的一切联系。眼下只有你是唯一能不受阻碍逃离此处的人…呃，鸟？

> **雪衣（机巧鸟版）：**吾…明白。小妹…保重，汝和吾…毕竟不同……

The cycrane flaps its wings and unsteadily flies out of the lower levels of the Shackling Prison, charging straight into the depths of the shadowy passage

### Speak to the Yaoqing messenger

*(Approach Moze)*

The man seems to materialize from the shadowy corners of the prison, as if he could vanish into thin air at any moment.

The "fugitive wearing a dark cloak," spoken of by Hanya, now stands before you. His face is devoid of any expression, cold and indifferent, yet... inexplicably, you could decipher this expressionless facade as if it whispered, "You're finally here."

**Moze:** The prison management on the Luofu is truly concerning...

> **貊泽：**罗浮的监狱管理，真是堪忧……

**Moze:** ...

> **貊泽：**至少有两拨来历不同的劫狱者闯进了这里，狼崽子们，还有这些魔阴身士卒。

**Moze:** Their hiding technique... it's pretty similar to the Wind Mantle technique used by the Vidyadhara serving Caelorum Venti on the Yaoqing. What do you think?

> **貊泽：**这种藏形匿影的技法…和曜青「天风君」座下持明掌握的「风幔」颇为相似。你们有眉目吗？

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Moze:** Well, never mind if you don't have any clues. Now is not the time to exchange assassination techniques.

> **貊泽：**不知道就算了，眼下也不是交流杀手技巧的时候。

**Moze:** I'm Moze. My friend fell into the clutches of that wolf. I was planning to escape and report back as quickly as possible, but now it seems...

> **貊泽：**我是貊泽，我的朋友落在了头狼的手里。我本打算离开这里尽快报信，但现在看起来……

**Hanya:** Thank you for your help, Mr. Moze. We'll do our best to ensure your friend's safety.

> **寒鸦：**貊泽先生，我们会尽可能保证你朋友的生命安全。

**Moze:** No, you can do nothing for him now, and you shouldn't be focusing on his safety at the moment.

**Moze:** The best-case scenario would be, we seal the gates and trap the enemy inside with us. We can't let that monstrous wolf escape.

> **貊泽：**最好的结果便是封锁大门，与敌同埋于深狱之中。绝对不能让那头巨狼离开此处。

**Moze:** Hopefully, someone will eventually notice that something is off and come to help. As for whether we'll still be alive by then, it's not something we should think about.

> **貊泽：**如此一来，时间久了，外界察觉便会有援兵赶到。至于那时候我们是不是还活着，这也不是我们该考虑的选项。

**Dan Heng:** You sure have a way of comforting people, Mr. Moze.

> **丹恒：**貊泽先生真是知道如何安慰人心。

**Moze:** As a person of the Yaoqing, I've always searched for something meaningful to give my life to.

> **貊泽：**作为曜青人，我们的一生都在追寻为有价值的事情献身。

**Dan Heng:** I have to make it out alive. My companions are waiting for me.

> **丹恒：**我必须活着离开此处，我的伙伴还在等我。

**Hanya:** As a netherworld dweller, I've already died once.

> **寒鸦：**寒鸦身为冥世之人，早已死过一次。

- ▸ Well, then I'll give it my all.

> ▸ 那我也豁出去了。

**Dan Heng:** Yeah, we won't give up, not until the very last moment.

> **丹恒：**不到最后一刻，还不是放弃的时候。

- ▸ I want to make it out alive, too.

> ▸ 我也想活着出去。

**Dan Heng:** Don't worry. Even if we end up dying here, I won't let it happen to you first.

> **丹恒：**不必担心，有我在这儿，就算有牺牲，也绝不让你成为第一个。

**Hanya:** Well, you're just as good at comforting people as Mr. Moze.

- ▸ Are we really out of options?

> ▸ 真的没有别的选项了吗？

**Hanya:** None of you are part of the Ten-Lords Commission, so sacrificing yourselves isn't the right move. As a judge, it's my responsibility to guard this prison, and if someone should step up to stop Hoolay from escaping, it should be me, not you.

**Hanya:** Once we reach the gate, I hope you'll help me seal it. After that, I'll do everything I can to fight the borisin, and you find a safe place to hide and wait for reinforcements.

> **寒鸦：**三位都不是十王司属僚，断没有为此牺牲的理由。我身为判官，把守幽狱是分内之责。若有人能阻止呼雷逃离为害，这个人也应当是我，而不是你们。

**Hanya:** Now, let's proceed upward!

### Pursue the jailbreaker's trail

*(Investigate borisin corpse next to the lantern, optional)*

The borisin's large stature easily lends them an intimidating quality that stays with them even after their demise. Looking at this pile of borisin corpses would send chills down anyone's spine, for it feels as though they can reanimate and pounce onto their prey any moment.

*(Investigate Aurumaton Spectral Envoy corpse, optional)*

This aurumaton spectral envoy appears to have been smashed to smithereens with only a single strike. Hoolay likely slammed his arm in to deliver this shockingly destructive force.

*Traverse: To the Transitory Hold floor*

### Pursue the jailbreaker's trail

**Hanya:** We must seal the entrance before the borisin fugitives get out.

> **寒鸦：**现在，我们向上去！

**Hanya:** The Prison Wardens and mechanisms won't be able to hold them for long. Follow me!

> **寒鸦：**我们必须在步离逃犯离开前封死大门。

*Traverse: To the Grimfrost Hold floor*

**Hanya:** The situation is critical. We cannot delay any longer!

> **寒鸦：**幽狱武弁和机关拖不了他们太久，随我来！

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hanya:** We're almost there! The gate is just ahead!

> **寒鸦：**快到了！前方不远处就是门关！

*(Investigate Aurumaton Spectral Envoy corpse, optional)*

Sparks continue to fizzle atop the spectral envoy's metal head. Despite having killed enemies that outnumber them, they were eventually left without the means to fight back before being wrecked to pieces.

*(Talk to warden, optional)*

**Wraith Warden:** There are many fugitives. Please be careful, Your Honor.

> **幽府武弁：**逃犯众多，大人请务必小心。

*(Idle text from Aurumaton Spectral Envoy)*

We must fulfill our duty...

*(Talk to Aurumaton Spectral Envoy, optional)*

**Aurumaton Spectral Envoy:** The fugitives are charging at the gate. Please be careful, Your Honor.

> **金人勾魂使：**逃犯正在冲击门关，大人请务必小心。

*(Idle text from warden)*

Mission accepted. Stop the fugitives...

*(Approach enemies)*

**Borisin Wolftrooper:** (Howling noise)

> **步离狼卒：**（嚎叫声）

**Hanya:** I will hold them back. Don't let Hoolay escape!

> **寒鸦：**诸位，我来拖住他们，请务必阻止呼雷逃脱！

*(Begin battle against Eclipse Wolftrooper ×1, Howling Casket ×1, Sableclaw Wolftrooper ×1)*

*(Approach shifting screen)*

The Shackling Prison has become a killing field. The prison guards try to resist, but Jiaoqiu can only watch them fall...

**Hoolay:** A-Rraaa...

> **呼雷：** A Rraaa——

**Hoolay:** This brings back such... familiar memories! Memories of chasing, gnawing, and tearing... They're back, they're back! They've all come back!

> **呼雷：**如此…如此熟悉的回忆！那些追逐、啃噬、撕扯的回忆…回来了，都回来了！它们统统都回来了！

**Mok Tok:** My Lord, we're just one step away from freedom. Once we pass through the gate, no one will be able to stop us.

> **末度：**大人，离自由仅有一步之遥了。穿过大门，就再也没人能阻止我们了。

**Hoolay:** So, this hostage is useless now? Kill him, Mok Tok.

> **呼雷：**那么这个人质也就毫无价值了？末度，杀了他。

**Jiaoqiu:** (He's getting away... Do something, Jiaoqiu... It's just like the old days, you useless loser... Just do something...)

> **椒丘：**（他要离开了…快想想办法…就像过去那样，椒丘，你这个无能为力的废物…想想办法啊……）

**Mok Tok:** Maybe this Yaoqing messenger can strike a deal in exchange for his life. He can use his status to help us escape from the Xianzhou. What do you think, Mr. Jiaoqiu?

> **末度：**也许，曜青的使节可以为了活下来和我们做笔交易，用他的身份帮助我们离开仙舟。我说的对吗，椒丘先生？

**Hoolay:** Just look at you! Fallen so low during my absence! You're even negotiating with livestock? As far as I recall, no one from the Yaoqing would ever consider a deal with Duran's offspring.

> **呼雷：**我不在的日子里，你们到底堕落成了何等可悲的模样？竟然开始向牲畜讨价还价？在我的记忆里，曜青人根本不会和都蓝的子孙谈这笔交易。

**Jiaoqiu:** (I've got to... make this beast stay here... whether it's for the sake of Feixiao or the Yaoqing...)

> **椒丘：**（我必须…让这畜生留下…不管是为了飞霄，还是为了曜青……）

**Jiaoqiu:** Deal. I certainly have more value alive...

> **椒丘：**成交。我当然有我活着的价值……

**Jiaoqiu:** My identity, my knowledge of the Xianzhou, and my understanding of many things that your minions have no knowledge of... They'll all be valuable.

> **椒丘：**我的身份，我对仙舟的了解，还有，我知道许多战首的部下们不知道的事情。

**Hoolay:** Lowly beast, muster up that pitiful tongue of yours and plead for what remains of your life. Speak up.

> **呼雷：**贱畜，鼓动你那条可怜的舌头，为自己残存的性命摇旗呐喊吧。说说看。

**Jiaoqiu:** I'm afraid you're unaware, the woman who defeated you, Jingliu... She has recently returned to the Luofu.

> **椒丘：**想必战首不知道，击败你的那个女人…「镜流」。她最近回到了罗浮。

**Jiaoqiu:** Your opportunity for revenge is right in front of you.

> **椒丘：**您复仇的机会，近在眼前。

**Hoolay:** ...

> **呼雷：**……

**Mok Tok:** My Lord! This slave is talking nonsense. I've never heard anything about this! May I just kill him now?

> **末度：**大人！这个奴隶是在胡说八道，我从未听过这样的消息！请允许我立刻杀了他！

**Hoolay:** Silence, Mok Tok! And you, slave... From now on, you'll stay by my side. You'll only speak and move when I allow you to. Otherwise, I'll dismember you, from head to tail, inch by inch. Got it?

> **呼雷：**闭嘴，末度！至于你，奴隶…从现在起，你必须寸步不离地跟在我身边。我让你开口，你才能开口；我让你迈步，你才能迈步。否则我会将你从头到尾，寸寸剐碎。你明白吗？

**Mok Tok:** But my Lord...

> **末度：**大人——

**Hoolay:** We must stay here for some time, Mok Tok. Fear not, because once I step out of the prison, it's the people of the Xianzhou who should tremble in fear. I'll show them what true calamity means.

> **呼雷：**末度，我们必须在这儿停留片刻了。不过你不用害怕。从踏出监狱的那一刻起，该害怕的是仙舟人。因为我将会让他们理解，什么是真正的灾难。

**Hoolay:** Now, Duran's whelps! Follow me!

> **呼雷：**现在，都蓝的崽子们，跟我走！

**Borisin Wolftrooper:** Prison guards, stop them! For our great lord Hoolay!

> **步离狼卒：**是狱卒，拦住他们！为了呼雷大人，万死不辞！

**Borisin Wolftrooper:** (Howling noise)

> **步离狼卒：**（嚎叫声）

*(Investigate borisin corpse next to the Space Anchor)*

These intruders possess extraodinary strength. They wield no weapons, for they are the deadliest weapons themselves.

*(Idle text from warden next to Hexanexus)*

Please stop those fugitives...

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** Report! After we lost contact with The Shackling Prison, we received a message from a cycrane...

> **云骑士卒：**报！幽囚狱消息断绝，我们接到了一只机巧鸟的传讯……

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** The prisoners have revolted, and the criminal Hoolay... is nowhere to be found!

> **云骑士卒：**狱中犯人暴动，重犯…呼雷不知去向！

**Jing Yuan:** What about the Yaoqing messengers and the Nameless?

> **景元：**曜青使者，还有无名客呢？

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** No news at the moment! We're trying to restore contact with The Shackling Prison as soon as possible!

> **云骑士卒：**暂无消息！我们正在尽快恢复与幽囚狱的联系！

**Feixiao:** I see. You're dismissed.

> **飞霄：**退下吧。

**Feixiao:** General Jing Yuan, General Huaiyan, it's just as we suspected... the rise of the Ambrosial Arbor was not the end of things, but the beginning.

> **飞霄：**景元将军，怀炎将军，如我们所料…建木升起并非事情的结束，而是开始。

**Feixiao:** The hand of the Ruin Legion has already sown the seeds of "Destruction." From this moment on, the Xianzhou Alliance will face an even greater challenge, from both the Abominations of Abundance and the "Destruction" itself.

> **飞霄：**那只自烬灭军团伸向仙舟的手，已经将「毁灭」的种子埋下了。从这一刻开始直至不可见的未来，仙舟联盟将要面对更可怕的挑战——来自「毁灭」与丰饶孽物的双重挑战。

**Feixiao:** If this is what they want, I'm always up for a challenge!

> **飞霄：**如果这就是他们想要的，我随时奉陪！

**Feixiao:** As the Marshal's special envoy, I'll dispatch the Luofu Cloud Knights to pursue Hoolay myself.

> **飞霄：**我将以元帅特使的身份调度罗浮云骑，展开对呼雷的追捕。

**Jing Yuan:** Looks like the hidden dregs have finally come to the surface. And as I said before, it's time to wash them away, once and for all.

> **景元：**池水下的东西终于按捺不住了。而我要做的，如我之前所说，「激浊扬清」。

After a brief but devastating loss of communications, the locked-down Shackling Prison is once more under control. The Express' Nameless may have emerged unharmed, but one of the Yaoqing envoys has been taken hostage.

Meanwhile, the borisin Warhead Hoolay, long confined to the depth of Shackling Prison, has vanished without a trace.

A pursuit between the hunter and the hunted will soon play out on the Xianzhou Luofu.

*(Mission completed)*
